Geometry-weighted cross-node interaction transfers target-specific evidence into carrier states, a shared per-node GRU maintains this information during transport, and a two-time readout combines stored carrier--identity compatibility with current detection--carrier geometry to recover the historical identity.
  In the controlled one-identity-per-carrier setting, CVG-SGM achieves 1.000 re-link accuracy, compared with 0.570 for both no-memory and parameter-count-matched no-write controls. It also generalizes to scene-disjoint, unseen longer visibility gaps. On real RGB-D/SAM2 feature-level episodes, it correctly re-links 36/36 held-out detections, compared with 28/36 for the strongest fixed carrier--identity control. Counterfactual carrier-memory swaps redirect predictions according to the swapped bindings, supporting the role of stored relational information in identity recovery. The current approach remains limited by shared-carrier identity ambiguity and does not provide end-to-end raw-video re-identification.

</details>


### [35] [Progressive Subset Selection and Cascaded Consistency for Unsupervised Vision-Language Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27515)
*Hao Yan,Guanghui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种简单的无监督视觉-语言模型适配框架，结合渐进式margin子集选择与级联一致性学习，仅更新归一化参数与文本原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉-语言模型在目标域偏移下性能下降；无监督适配虽无需标签，但伪标签不可靠且无验证集用于模型选择或早停。

Method: 用预测margin构建类平衡伪标签子集并逐步扩张，从可靠监督锚点开始适配；采用级联一致性学习，以弱增强视图上的时间平滑教师预测监督强增强视图学生预测，并做置信度过滤；仅更新视觉编码器的归一化参数和由文本提示初始化的类原型以保持预训练嵌入结构。

Result: 在CLIP与SigLIP骨干的多个识别基准上，相比零样本基线有一致提升，并与近期无监督PVLM适配方法性能相当。

Conclusion: 渐进伪标签选择与教师引导一致性构成了无需目标标签、轻量有效的PVLM分类器适配策略。

Abstract: Pretrained Vision-Language Models (PVLMs) achieve strong zero-shot recognition but often suffer performance degradation under target-domain shift. Unsupervised adaptation provides a practical way to improve target-domain performance without labeled data, but it remains challenging because pseudo-labels generated by the model can be unreliable and no labeled validation set is available for model selection or early stopping. We propose a simple framework for unsupervised PVLM adaptation that combines progressive margin-based subset selection with cascaded consistency learning. The subset selection strategy constructs a class-balanced pseudo-labeled set using prediction margins and gradually expands it during training, enabling adaptation to start from reliable supervision anchors before incorporating broader target-domain data. Cascaded consistency learning further exploits unlabeled samples by using temporally smoothed teacher predictions on weakly augmented views to supervise student predictions on strongly augmented views, with confidence filtering to reduce the influence of uncertain pseudo-labels. To preserve the pretrained embedding structure, the framework updates only normalization parameters in the visual encoder and class prototypes initialized from text prompts. Experiments across multiple recognition benchmarks with both CLIP and SigLIP backbones show consistent improvements over zero-shot baselines and competitive performance against recent unsupervised PVLM adaptation methods. These results suggest that progressive pseudo-label selection and teacher-guided consistency provide an effective and lightweight strategy for adapting PVLM classifiers without labeled target data.

</details>


### [36] [HealBench: A Fine-Grained Benchmark for Evaluating Psychological Comfort in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27516)
*Junjie Xu,Yuzhe Yang,Rui Yang,Luwei Xiao,Wenxin Wu,Xingjiao Wu,Liang He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出HealBench，一个关注"心理舒适感"的文本到图像生成与编辑基准（3万图文对、19大类92子类），并构建HealBench-Eval评测集（1,095条）与HealScorer评分器，系统评估14个生成模型与提示改写策略，揭示视觉质量与心理舒适感之间的落差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图与图像编辑研究已在照片级真实感、语义保真度和美学上取得进展，但"输出是否让人感到心理舒适"这一以人为本的维度仍缺乏研究。

Method: 基于认知评价理论界定心理舒适感（由观者对恢复性、情感支持性视觉线索的评价所形成的情感反应）；通过视觉语言模型匹配、人类偏好模型、专家筛选与示例引导增强的人机协同流程，从10万候选中筛选出3万高质量图文对构建HealBench；构建特征聚类的HealBench-Eval评测集；对14个代表性生成模型及提示改写策略进行客观与主观评测；并开展强化学习实验验证HealScorer可作奖励信号。

Result: 发现常规视觉质量与感知舒适感之间存在明显差距：强语义对齐与高美学并不保证生成图像具有情感恢复性；HealScorer可作为奖励信号用于评估与提升生成图像的情感与恢复性质量。

Conclusion: 研究确立心理舒适感作为以人为本图像生成的一个独立维度，并将HealBench定位为面向情感感知生成模型的基础性资源。

Abstract: Despite advances in photorealism, semantic fidelity, and aesthetics, text-to-image generation and image editing remain understudied in a human-centered dimension: whether their outputs feel psychologically comforting. Grounded in cognitive evaluation theory, we define psychological comfort as an affective response shaped by viewers' appraisal of restorative and emotionally supportive visual cues. We introduce HealBench, a fine-grained benchmark of 30,000 high-quality image-text pairs spanning 19 categories and 92 subcategories, selected from 100,000 candidates through a human- and expert-in-the-loop pipeline combining vision-language model matching, human preference models, expert filtering, and exemplar-guided augmentation. We also construct HealBench-Eval, a feature-clustered evaluation set of 1,095 instances. Using HealBench-Eval, we conduct objective and subjective evaluations of 14 representative image generation models and prompt-rewriting strategies. Results reveal a gap between conventional visual quality and perceived comfort: strong semantic alignment and aesthetics do not guarantee emotionally restorative images. Reinforcement learning experiments further show that HealScorer can serve as a reward signal for assessing and improving the emotional and restorative quality of generated images. These findings establish psychological comfort as a distinct dimension of human-centered image generation and position HealBench as a foundation for affect-aware generative models.

</details>


### [37] [Multiscale Temporal-Spectral-Spatial EEG Learning With Modality-Specific and Modality-Invariant Representations for Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27521)
*Jian Zhang,Manrong Liu,Yilei Huang,Wenhua Liu,Xiaohui Yang,Lijun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以EEG为核心的多模态情感识别框架GMSI-Net，通过TSSR编码器、模态特异分支与模态不变分支（加权对比损失对齐），在DEAP和MAHNOB-HCI上取得约96%和94%的效价/唤醒度识别准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态生理信号可为情感识别提供互补线索，但存在两大挑战：一是情感相关EEG模式在时间、频带、电极和脑区维度上高度变化；二是不同生理模态既含模态特有信息又含共享情感信息，如何同时保留与对齐二者尚待解决。

Method: GMSI-Net由三部分组成：(1) 时间-空间-频谱-脑区（TSSR）编码器，建模多尺度时间-通道模式、微分熵特征以及由局部到全局的脑区关系；(2) 模态特异表示（MSR）分支，采用双向跨模态注意力与可学习门控，在控制信息传递的同时保留模态特有线索；(3) 模态不变表示（MIR）分支，学习共享情感表示，并用自适应加权对比损失实现跨模态对齐；外周生理信号由独立的多尺度Conv-Transformer编码器处理。评估采用被试内随机十折交叉验证。

Result: DEAP上效价与唤醒度分别为96.21%(1.47)和96.41%(1.60)；MAHNOB-HCI上分别为94.10%(2.86)和94.48%(3.77)（括号内为标准差）。

Conclusion: 将EEG特征与互补的生理信息相结合可有效提升情感识别性能；同时保留模态特异信息并对齐模态不变表示是可行的多模态融合思路。

Abstract: Multimodal physiological signals provide complementary cues for emotion recognition. They also raise two related challenges. Emotion-related EEG patterns vary across time, frequency bands, electrodes, and brain regions. Different physiological modalities also contain both modality-specific and shared affective information. This paper presents GMSI-Net, an EEG-centered multimodal emotion recognition framework. It has three key components. The temporal-spatial-spectral-region (TSSR) encoder models multiscale temporal-channel patterns, differential entropy features, and local-to-global brain-region relations. The modality-specific representation (MSR) branch uses bidirectional cross-modal attention and learnable gates. It controls information transfer while retaining modality-specific cues. The modality-invariant representation (MIR) branch learns a shared affective representation. An adaptively weighted contrastive loss aligns this representation across modalities. Independent multiscale Conv-Transformer encoders process the peripheral physiological signals. GMSI-Net is evaluated on DEAP and MAHNOB-HCI using within-subject random ten-fold cross-validation. The model reaches 96.21 (1.47) and 96.41 (1.60) for valence and arousal on DEAP. It reaches 94.10 (2.86) and 94.48 (3.77) on MAHNOB-HCI. These results indicate that combining EEG features with complementary physiological information can improve emotion recognition.

</details>


### [38] [OmniVLoc: Topo-metric Visual Localization for Omnidirectional Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27522)
*Zhuo Yu,Shuyang Zhang,Huihua Zhu,Wenjia Ouyang,Zuo Zuo,Jianhao Jiao,Yilong Zhu,Xiaopin Zhong,Zongze Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 OmniVLoc，一个基于拓扑-度量（topo-metric）框架的全向相机环绕视图定位系统，通过融合全局地点识别与单目深度估计、求解尺度感知位姿图并进行球面光束法平差，实现度量级位姿恢复，并设计分层验证机制提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全向相机能提供环绕视野，突破传统视觉定位系统的视场限制，但现有定位系统未能充分利用全向相机的特性，在场景内容变化与光照引起的外观变化下难以提供准确、鲁棒的定位性能。

Method: 采用 topo-metric 框架；整合先进的全局地点识别与单目深度估计方法，提出多约束视觉定位范式；求解尺度感知位姿图为每帧查询恢复度量级位姿，并执行精确的球面光束法平差（spherical bundle adjustment）；设计分层验证机制，在不同阶段过滤错误输出。

Result: 在公开数据集与自建数据集上的大量实验验证了 OmniVLoc 在精度与可靠性上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: OmniVLoc 通过充分利用全向相机特性并结合多约束优化与分层验证，实现了准确、鲁棒的全向视觉定位；源代码与数据集将在论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Omnidirectional cameras provide surround-view information, significantly overcoming the field-of-view constraints for traditional visual localization systems. However, existing localization systems fail to fully exploit the characteristics of omnidirectional cameras, struggling to deliver accurate and robust localization performance amidst scene content variations and illumination-induced appearance changes. In this work, we propose OmniVLoc, a surround-view localization system based on a topo-metric framework. By integrating state-of-the-art global place recognition and monocular depth estimation methods, we introduce a multi-constrained visual localization paradigm. By solving a scale-aware pose graph and performing precise spherical bundle adjustment, we accurately recover the metric-level pose for each query frame.Furthermore, a hierarchical verification mechanism is designed to filter out erroneous outputs across different stages, effectively bolstering the overall system robustness. Extensive experiments on both public and our custom datasets validate the superior accuracy and reliability of OmniVLoc. To benefit the community, our source code and dataset will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [39] [Less is More: Concise Retrieval and Critic-based Re-ranking for Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27538)
*Yuqian Zheng,Hyoju Ahn,Hyeongjin Ahn,Eunil Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Less-is-More CIR：一个两阶段的免训练框架，用于零样本组合图像检索（ZS-CIR），通过"简洁检索 + 评审重排序"减少冗余字幕带来的语义噪声，提升检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ZS-CIR 方法通常借助大型视觉-语言模型生成目标图像字幕再用于检索，但生成的字幕往往包含冗余语义细节，引入噪声并导致检索结果次优。作者由此出发，希望抑制这类语义噪声。

Method: 两阶段、免训练框架。第一阶段"Concise Retrieval"：生成仅保留最具判别性修改语义的简洁、面向目标的字幕，以减少检索中的语义噪声。第二阶段"Critic Re-ranking"：用评审重排序模块对已检索到的候选进行评估，从修改一致性（modification consistency）与参考图像保持度（reference preservation）两个角度重排序。

Result: 大量实验表明该框架在 ZS-CIR 任务上具有有效性与鲁棒性；代码已开源（https://github.com/dxlabskku/LM-CIR）。摘要未给出具体指标数值。

Conclusion: "少即是多"的思路在 ZS-CIR 中成立：精简字幕并用评审模型重排序，能在无需额外训练的情况下缓解语义噪声问题，是一个简单有效的改进范式。

Abstract: Zero-shot composed image retrieval (ZS-CIR) aims to retrieve a target image based on a reference image and modification text. Recent approaches commonly rely on large vision-language models to generate target image captions for retrieval. However, these captions may include redundant semantic details, introducing noise when retrieving the target image and leading to suboptimal retrieval results. To address this issue, we propose Less-is-More CIR, a two-stage training-free framework that combines Concise Retrieval and Critic Re-ranking. In the first stage, concise target-oriented captions are generated that preserve only the most discriminative modification semantics, thereby reducing semantic noise in the retrieval. In the second stage, a critic re-rank module further evaluates the retrieved candidates from the perspectives of modification consistency and reference preservation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed framework for the ZS-CIR. Our code is available at: https://github.com/dxlabskku/LM-CIR.

</details>


### [40] [SynCalib: Breaking Dataset Shortcuts in LiDAR-Camera Calibration via Extrinsic-Controllable Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27547)
*Huihua Zhu,Shuyang Zhang,Dongqing Chen,Zhuo Yu,Jianhao Jiao,Yilong Zhu,Siyu Teng,Xiaopin Zhong,Zongze Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向LiDAR-相机标定网络训练的数据生成框架，利用带深度真值的全景图像数据集合成多模态标定数据，缓解现有方法因外参多样性不足导致的数据集捷径问题，提升泛化性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的在线LiDAR-相机标定方法虽然精度高、无需标定靶，但训练数据中外参配置缺乏多样性，网络容易过拟合到固定的标称外参，出现"数据集捷径"现象，导致实际部署时性能严重下降、泛化能力差。

Method: 构建高泛化性的数据生成框架：以已有的带深度真值标注的全景图像数据集为基础，在严格的外参约束条件下合成多模态（LiDAR与相机）标定数据，用于训练标定网络。

Result: 大量实验表明，该流程生成的数据能显著提升典型学习型标定网络的泛化性和鲁棒性；在多种相机-LiDAR配置下开展真实场景评测，验证了仅用合成数据训练的模型具备零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: 外参多样性充足的数据生成是解决数据集捷径问题的有效途径；所提框架可为学习型在线标定提供可推广的训练数据，并计划公开数据生成代码、预训练模型与在线基准数据集以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Online LiDAR-camera calibration remains a fundamental yet challenging task in the field of robotics. Recently, deep learning-based data-driven approaches have emerged, offering high-precision, targetless calibration capabilities. However, existing methods often suffer from a critical dataset shortcut phenomenon due to the lack of extrinsic diversity in their training data. Networks tend to overfit to fixed nominal extrinsic configurations, leading to severe performance degradation and poor generalization in real-world deployments. To address this limitation, we propose a highly generalizable data generation framework tailored for training LiDAR-camera calibration networks. Our framework leverages existing omnidirectional image datasets annotated with depth ground truth to synthesize multi-modal calibration data under rigorous extrinsic constraints. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the data generated by our pipeline significantly enhances the generalization and robustness of typical learning-based calibration networks. Furthermore, we conduct comprehensive real-world evaluations across various camera-LiDAR configurations, validating the zero-shot transfer capability of models trained exclusively on our synthetic data. To facilitate future research and community use, the code for data generation, the pre-trained models, and online benchmark datasets will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [41] [CityPaint: A Free Lunch from 2D Diffusion for City-Scale 3D Texturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27564)
*Yuchen Zou,Lihuang Fang,Siyu Zhu,Xiao Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CityPaint 利用 2D 扩散模型，在无需城市级 3D 训练数据的情况下，为城市级 3D 场景生成全局一致的高保真纹理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市级 3D 环境在虚拟现实、游戏和城市数字孪生中需求旺盛，但真实城市级 3D 训练数据稀缺，现有方法只能拼接独立生成的资产或依赖合成城市语料，导致真实感与全局一致性受损。相对而言，2D 图像数据及其生成模型丰富且蕴含强视觉先验，因此作者希望借助 2D 扩散模型弥补 3D 数据缺口。

Method: 提出 CityPaint 框架：融合几何先验与 2D 扩散模型的生成能力，设计 top-K 相机烘焙策略用于迭代式纹理投影；针对 2D 到 3D 投影产生的伪影，提出解耦精修机制，分别优化地形纹理与建筑纹理。

Result: 实验表明 CityPaint 能生成全局连贯、局部细节精细的纹理，在视觉质量与跨视角一致性上均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 以 2D 扩散先验替代稀缺的城市级 3D 训练数据，是生成城市级 3D 纹理的可行路径；top-K 相机烘焙与地形/建筑解耦精修是保证全局一致性与局部质量的关键设计。

Abstract: Generating high-fidelity, city-scale 3D environments plays a crucial role in applications such as virtual reality, gaming, and urban digital twins. However, the scarcity of real-world city-scale 3D training data forces existing methods to either stitch independently generated assets or learn from synthetic urban corpora, often sacrificing realism and global consistency. In contrast, 2D image data and their corresponding generative models are abundant and encode rich visual priors. To bridge this gap, we propose CityPaint, a novel framework that leverages 2D diffusion models to generate globally consistent city-scale 3D textures without relying on city-scale 3D training data. Specifically, CityPaint integrates geometric priors with the generative power of 2D diffusion models, introducing a robust top-$K$ camera baking strategy for iterative texture projection. To address the artifacts of 2D-to-3D projection, we further propose a decoupled refinement mechanism that separately optimizes terrain and building textures. Extensive experiments show that CityPaint produces globally coherent textures with fine local details and achieves favorable results against strong baselines in both visual quality and cross-view consistency.

</details>


### [42] [AnomalyJev: Towards Evaluating Image Anomaly Explanations via Claim-Level Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27565)
*Qizhou Wang,Jiawen Zhu,Guansong Pang,Christopher Leckie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量框架 AnomalyJev，通过提取可验证声明并与真值注释对照，高效评估多模态大模型异常检测解释的忠实性与完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于MLLM的异常检测方法能生成文本解释，但可靠且高效地评估这些解释仍很困难。LLM裁判虽可借助结构化评分标准进行评估，却需要大量token生成，成本高、难以规模化。

Method: 将评估锚定在可用真值上：从每个解释中提取可验证声明，并与对应注释进行验证。以AnomalyJev实例化该思路——一种轻量框架，利用真值定义的查询和基于指针的头部，从上下文化表示中提取可验证声明，无需token生成，随后对照真值验证声明以评分忠实性与完整性。同时构建含640条解释、1600余条专家评分的人工标注集，覆盖域内与跨数据集设置。

Result: 尽管设计紧凑，AnomalyJev与人类标注者的一致性优于多数开源MLLM裁判，接近旗舰MLLM裁判的水平，而运行时与资源消耗仅为其一小部分。

Conclusion: 基于真值声明验证的轻量评估框架可在显著降低计算成本的同时，接近旗舰LLM裁判的评估质量，为MLLM生成解释的评估提供可扩展方案。

Abstract: Recent multimodal LLM (MLLM)-based anomaly detection (AD) methods can generate textual explanations that help interpret their predictions. However, reliably and efficiently evaluating these explanations remains challenging. While LLM judges can assess explanations through structured rubrics, they often require substantial token generation, making evaluation costly and difficult to scale. We instead propose to anchor evaluation in the available ground truth by extracting verifiable claims from each explanation and verifying them against the corresponding annotations. We instantiate this idea as AnomalyJev, a lightweight framework that extracts verifiable claims from contextualised representations using ground-truth-defined queries and pointer based heads, without token generation. The claims are then verified against the ground truth to score faithfulness and completeness. For evaluation, we construct a human-annotated set of 640 explanations with over 1,600 expert ratings across in-domain and cross-dataset settings. Despite its compact design, AnomalyJev achieves stronger agreement with human annotators than most open MLLM judges and approaches that of flagship MLLM judges, at a fraction of their runtime and resources.

</details>


### [43] [Text-Anchored Modality Experts with Uncertainty-Guided Routing for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27571)
*Xiaohua Huang,Bingrui Lei,Xiuyan hao,Ling Zhou,Yuan Zong,Wenming Zheng,Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TAME 将多模态情感分析重新表述为"以语言先验为锚、由不确定性引导选择性信任非语言证据"的精炼范式，采用非对称混合专家结构并用预测不确定性驱动路由，在三大数据集上多数指标达到 SOTA 且大幅降低参数与计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态情感分析方法将各模态视为对称等价，忽略了语言承载主要语义先验、而非语言信号（音频、视频）仅为补充且噪声较大的事实，导致融合时易被低质非语言信号干扰。

Method: 提出 TAME（文本锚定的模态专家 + 不确定性引导路由）：1）非对称混合模态专家框架，文本特征作为固定且始终激活的共享专家，音频-文本与视频-文本跨模态分支作为可动态路由的辅助专家；2）引入轻量 Prophet Head 量化各单模态的样本级预测不确定性，并将其注入模态路由器，实现可靠性感知的专家聚合与融合。

Result: 在 CMU-MOSI、CMU-MOSEI 与 CH-SIMS 三个数据集上，TAME 在多数指标上取得 SOTA；相比混合专家基线，参数量最多减少 78.7%，FLOPs 最多减少 41.2%。

Conclusion: 非对称专家构建结合不确定性引导路由是多模态情感分析的一种有效范式，可在提升性能的同时显著降低模型开销。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis (MSA) aims to understand user-generated videos by integrating multiple modalities. Existing methods often treat modalities as symmetric, overlooking the fact that language provides a dominant semantic prior while nonverbal signals are complementary and noise-prone. To address this, we propose a Text-Anchored Modality Experts with Uncertainty-Guided Routing, termed TAME, that reformulates MSA as an uncertainty-guided refinement paradigm, where the language prior is calibrated and enhanced by selectively trusted nonverbal evidence. Specifically, TAME adopts an asymmetric Mixture-of-Modality-Experts framework, in which textual features act as a fixed and always-activated shared expert, while audio-textual and video-textual cross-modal branches serve as dynamically routed auxiliary experts. Furthermore, we introduce lightweight Prophet Heads to quantify the sample-level predictive uncertainty of each individual modality, and this uncertainty metric is further incorporated into the modality router to realize reliability-aware expert aggregation and fusion. Experiments on CMU-MOSI, CMU-MOSEI, and CH-SIMS datasets demonstrate that TAME achieves state-of-the-art performance on the majority of metrics while reducing parameter count by up to 78.7% and FLOPs by up to 41.2% over the mixture-of-expert baseline. %These results confirm that asymmetric expert construction with uncertainty-guided routing is an effective paradigm for MSA.

</details>


### [44] [Real In, Real Out: What If We Only Use Real Data for Scene Text Editing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27592)
*Xingsong Ye,Yongkun Du,Jiaxin Zhang,Chong Sun,Zhineng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 TextRIRO，将每个真实样本转化为自身的配对监督，用字符列打乱+图像裁剪构造自配对训练数据，基于条件扩散架构建立强基线，显著缓解合成到真实域差，并构建 TextRIRO-3M 大规模真实数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景文本编辑（STE）通常需要编辑前后的配对图像，现有方法严重依赖合成配对数据和复杂解耦策略，难以忠实刻画自然场景的多样性与复杂性，常产生伪影或幻觉。作者因此质疑这一惯例，探索能否用真实样本自身构成配对监督。

Method: 探索多种破坏原图的方式（裁剪、打乱、掩码）以构造自配对训练数据，并在条件扩散架构上构建强基线 TextRIRO。实验表明"字符列打乱+图像裁剪"（Shuffle&Crop）能在破坏文本语义的同时保留关键风格线索，使模型直接从真实文本图像学习编辑行为。

Result: 在真实场景基准上，TextRIRO 在准确性和视觉真实感两方面均取得有前景的表现，显著缓解合成到真实的域差。此外，利用 TextRIRO 拼接编辑后与原图构建 TextRIRO-3M 大规模真实长文本识别数据集，训练识别模型可显著提升识别准确率。

Conclusion: TextRIRO 以简洁且面向真实的设计将 STE 提升到真实、可靠、广泛适用的新水平，并为下游任务提供高质量训练资源。

Abstract: Aiming to modify textual content while preserving the original style, Scene Text Editing (STE) has significant practical value in many applications. STE typically requires paired images before and after editing for training. However, recent approaches rely heavily on synthetic paired data and complex disentanglement strategies. They struggle to faithfully capture the diversity and complexity of natural scenes, often producing artifacts or hallucinations. In this work, we challenge the common practice and investigate whether STE can be driven by turning each real sample into its own paired supervision. Concretely, we explore multiple ways to disrupt the original image (e.g., Crop, Shuffle, Mask) to construct self-paired training data, and build a strong baseline (TextRIRO}) upon a conditional diffusion architecture. Our experiments show that combining character-column shuffling with image cropping (Shuffle&Crop) effectively destroys text semantics while preserving key style cues, enabling the model to learn editing behaviors directly from the real text image. With this concise yet real-oriented design, TextRIRO achieves promising performance in both accuracy and visual realism across real-world benchmarks, substantially mitigating the synthetic-to-real domain gap. Furthermore, we leverage TextRIRO to build TextRIRO-3M, a large-scale realistic dataset for long-text recognition by concatenating edited and original images. Experimental results show that training recognition models with TextRIRO-3M significantly improves recognition accuracy, demonstrating that TextRIRO provides highly useful training resources for downstream tasks. Overall, TextRIRO lifts STE to realistic, reliable, and widely applicable new levels. Code is available at https://github.com/YesianRohn/TextRIRO.

</details>


### [45] [Causal Spatiotemporal State Evolution for Monocular 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27594)
*Qiaowei Miao,Yijing Guo,Lipei Zhang,Qifeng Cheng,Yuru Xiao,Ziqian Ni,Sheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出前馈框架，用因果时空Mamba实现有方向的时序状态传播，并以几何接地的统一运动监督约束帧间状态转移，在单目视频上学习因果4D视觉几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈模型擅长静态3D几何估计，但扩展到动态4D场景需将视觉几何建模为因果演化状态。现有方法采用无序时序聚合，缺乏物理时间的方向结构；且监督上把几何与运动视为互斥目标，未利用二者固有物理耦合，缺少统一的时序状态转移约束，导致4D表示不一致。

Method: 1) 因果时空Mamba（CSM）：在时序聚合中引入方向性状态传播，通过互补的时序-空间与时序-空间（Temporal-Spatial与Spatial-Temporal）双扫描，跨帧持续传播几何感知状态以建模时空演化；2) 统一运动监督（UMS）：利用相机与深度头的几何感知预测构建与3D结构一致的前向运动场，用几何接地的监督显式约束帧间状态转移，促进稳定传播。

Result: 在动态基准上于视频深度、相机位姿与光流估计三项任务均取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: CSM的方向性状态传播与UMS的协同能有效刻画连续场景演化，显著降低时序漂移，增强4D表示学习的时空一致性。

Abstract: While feed-forward models excel in static 3D geometry estimation, extending them to dynamic 4D scenes requires modeling visual geometry as a causally evolving state. Current methods typically rely on unordered temporal aggregation, which lacks the directional structure of physical time. Furthermore, existing supervision often treats geometry and motion as disjoint objectives, failing to exploit their inherent physical coupling. Consequently, they lack a unified constraint to effectively supervise temporal state transitions, leading to inconsistent 4D scene representations. To address this, we propose a feed-forward framework for causal 4D visual geometry learning from monocular videos. At its core, Causal Spatiotemporal Mamba augments temporal aggregation with directional state propagation. Through complementary Temporal-Spatial and Spatial-Temporal Scans, CSM continuously propagates geometry-aware states across frames to model spatiotemporal evolution. To align this causal state evolution with observable dynamics, we introduce Unified Motion Supervision. Rather than treating motion as an independent objective, UMS leverages geometry-aware predictions from the camera and depth heads to construct a unified forward motion field that is consistent with the underlying 3D structure. This geometry-grounded supervision explicitly constrains inter-frame state transitions, enabling stable states propagation across frames. Extensive evaluations on dynamic benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across video depth, camera pose, and optical flow estimation. These results validate that the synergy between the directional state propagation of Causal Spatiotemporal Mamba and Unified Motion Supervision effectively captures continuous scene evolution, significantly reducing temporal drift and reinforcing spatial-temporal consistency in 4D representation learning.

</details>


### [46] [HiQGS: Hierarchical Spatiotemporal Querying for Feed-Forward Gaussian Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27604)
*Qiaowei Miao,Qifeng Cheng,Yijing Guo,Yuru Xiao,Ziqian Ni,Sheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 HiQGS，一个基于查询的分层前馈框架，从稀疏单目已定位帧重建高斯场景，通过分层时空编码器按图像位置、观测时间与相机位姿组织多分辨率几何-外观特征，并用时间自适应解码器以目标时间戳查询分层表示，预测对应的高斯场景状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏已定位单目帧进行前馈高斯重建，需要在视角与时间同时变化的观测上推理。现有方法存在两类局限：一类直接从与观测对齐的特征预测高斯原语，使高斯分配与单帧紧耦合；另一类通过紧凑隐查询聚合多帧信息，但未显式按观测与目标时间戳的时间关系组织证据。因此跨空间尺度、视角与时间戳的证据难以对齐和整合。

Method: HiQGS 包含两个核心模块：(1) 分层时空编码器，将多分辨率几何-外观视觉特征按图像位置、观测时间和相机位姿组织，保留互补的外观与结构线索；(2) 时间自适应解码器，利用目标时间戳查询所得到的分层表示，检索相关的时空证据并预测对应的高斯场景状态。

Result: 在静态与动态场景重建基准上的实验表明，该方法具有较好的渲染质量和几何精度，验证了分层视觉编码与目标时间感知高斯查询的有效性。

Conclusion: 按空间、时间与位姿显式结构化观测证据，并以目标时间戳进行查询式解码，可有效解决稀疏单目帧下跨尺度、跨视角、跨时间证据的对齐与整合问题，为前馈高斯场景重建提供有效框架。

Abstract: Feed-forward Gaussian reconstruction from sparse posed monocular frames requires reasoning over observations that vary jointly in viewpoint and time. Existing methods either predict Gaussian primitives from observation-aligned features or aggregate multi-frame information through compact latent queries. However, the former tightly couples Gaussian allocation to individual frames, while the latter does not explicitly structure observed evidence according to its temporal relation to the requested timestamp. Consequently, evidence across spatial scales, viewpoints, and timestamps can be difficult to align and integrate. We present \textbf{HiQGS}, a query-based hierarchical framework for feed-forward Gaussian scene reconstruction from sparse monocular frames. HiQGS introduces a \textbf{Hierarchical Spatiotemporal Encoder} that organizes multi-resolution geometry-appearance visual features according to image location, observation time, and camera pose, thereby preserving complementary appearance and structural cues. Our \textbf{Time-Adaptive Decoder} use target timestamp queries the resulting hierarchical representation to retrieve relevant spatiotemporal evidence and predicts the corresponding Gaussian scene state. Experiments on static and dynamic scene reconstruction benchmarks demonstrate strong rendering quality and geometric accuracy, validating the effectiveness of hierarchical visual encoding and target-time-aware Gaussian querying.

</details>


### [47] [DART: Scaling a Shape Representation Across Anatomical Classes and Imaging Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27605)
*Koushik Swarna,Pedro R. A. S. Bassi,Lam Ngo,Wenxuan Li,Jiancheng Yang,Alan Yuille,Zongwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DART 从分割概率中学习共享的形状表征，借助解剖文本描述纠正分割结果，无需医学图像即可跨类别、跨模态扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分割模型在最难分割的结构上仍会输出不符合解剖学合理性的形状（如血管断裂、腺体碎裂）。学习式形状表征可纠正此类错误，但多数方法只覆盖固定的解剖类别；扩展类别与成像模态通常需要新的体素级标注，代价高昂。

Method: 提出 DART（Diffusion for Anatomical Reshaping with Text）：从分割概率中学习一个共享形状表征，并以解剖学文本描述作为条件来纠正概率图，纠正过程不依赖医学图像输入。基于已有分割预测，将单一形状表征从 50 类扩展到 377 个解剖类别，无需为纠正训练提供新的体素级标注。

Result: 在 13 个公开基准上，CT 胆囊单例的绝对 Dice 提升达 31%，MRI 前列腺单例提升达 10%；可纠正训练中未出现的 4 类病灶，并从 CT 迁移到 MRI 而无需重训；纠正后的标注还能反哺分割模型，使某模型 Dice 从 57.1% 提升到 67.5%；同一流程支持 45,000 例 CT 扫描的标注。

Conclusion: 在类别内与跨类别共享形状信息，可在将分割扩展到更多解剖类别和成像模态时减少对新体素级标注的需求。模型已公开于 Hugging Face。

Abstract: Current state-of-the-art segmentation models still produce anatomically implausible shapes, such as broken vessels and fragmented glands, on the structures that are hardest to segment. Learned shape representations capture what structures look like and can help correct these errors, but most cover a fixed set of anatomical classes. Extending this coverage across classes and imaging modalities is costly because it often requires new voxel-level annotations. We propose Diffusion for Anatomical Reshaping with Text (DART), which learns a shared shape representation from segmentation probabilities. It uses anatomical descriptions to correct these probabilities without receiving medical images. Across 13 public benchmarks, absolute Dice gains reach 31% for individual gallbladder cases in CT and 10% for individual prostate cases in MRI. Using existing segmentation predictions, we scale a single shape representation from 50 to 377 anatomical classes without new voxel-level annotations for correction training. The shared representation also corrects four lesion classes absent from training and transfers from CT to MRI without retraining. Corrected labels also improve segmentation models, raising one model's Dice from 57.1% to 67.5%. The same pipeline supports annotations for 45,000 CT scans. These results suggest that sharing shape information within and across classes can reduce the need for new voxel-level annotations when extending segmentation to more anatomical classes and imaging modalities. The model is available at https://huggingface.co/Koushik45/DART-Seg

</details>


### [48] [WaveSpec: Multi-Spectral Iris Presentation Attack Detection via Wavelength-Conditioned Spectral Coherence Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27608)
*Raghavendra Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 WaveSpec 多光谱虹膜活体检测框架，通过特征级光谱一致性适配显式建模各波段光谱响应的一致性，在 MSIrPAD 跨攻击仪器协议下显著降低检测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多光谱虹膜呈现攻击检测方法多将波长信息仅用于条件化或特征融合，难以显式判断样本的光谱响应在各波段间是否一致，从而限制了检测能力。

Method: 每个 NIR 波段先用 DINOv2 主干编码；提出集合条件光谱一致性适配器（SCSA），利用可用光谱集合的共识、各波段相对共识的偏差以及波长身份来更新波段特征；适配后特征投影为结构、光谱与异常表示，经自适应池化后分类为真实或攻击。在 MSIrPAD 基准上采用严格跨攻击仪器协议（单一攻击仪器训练、未见攻击测试）评估。

Result: 平均 D-EER 从 SpectraIrisPAD 的 8.45% 降至 3.33%；BPCER@APCER=1% 从 43.16% 降至 12.07%。消融显示 SCSA 适配器与光谱残差是性能提升的关键；波段子集分析表明在部分光谱观测下仍有效，五波段齐全时最佳。计算开销有限，NVIDIA L40S-48Q 上五波段样本端到端延迟 11.15 ms。

Conclusion: 在特征层面进行光谱一致性适配是多光谱虹膜活体检测的有效方向，可在跨未知攻击泛化性上带来大幅提升，且额外计算成本较小。

Abstract: Multispectral iris presentation attack detection benefits from observing the same iris across multiple near-infrared wavelengths, but existing methods often use wavelength information mainly for conditioning or fusion. This can limit their ability to explicitly model whether the spectral response of a sample is coherent across bands. In this paper, we propose \textbf{WaveSpec}, a multispectral iris PAD framework that performs feature-level spectral coherence adaptation. Each NIR band is first encoded with a DINOv2 backbone. The proposed Set Conditioned Spectral Coherence Adapter then updates each band feature using the consensus of the available spectral set, the band-specific deviation from that consensus, and wavelength identity. The adapted features are projected into structural, spectral, and anomaly representations, adaptively pooled, and classified as bona fide or attack. We evaluate WaveSpec on the MSIrPAD benchmark under a strict cross-artefact protocol, where the model is trained with one presentation attack instrument and tested on unseen attacks. WaveSpec reduces the average D-EER from 8.45\% for SpectraIrisPAD to 3.33\%, and reduces average BPCER@APCER=1\% from 43.16\% to 12.07\%. Ablation results show that the SCSA adapter and spectral residual are central to the improvement, while a band-subset analysis shows that WaveSpec remains effective under partial spectral observations and performs best when all five bands are available. The method adds only modest computational overhead, with 11.15 ms end-to-end latency per five-band sample on an NVIDIA L40S-48Q GPU.

</details>


### [49] [Provisioning Robust and Efficient LiDAR Perception for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27617)
*Tingxu Han,Zijie Shen,An Guo,Lyuke Wang,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen,Wei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将LiDAR 3D检测的鲁棒性与资源效率联合建模为"在延迟与显存约束下保持对点云损坏的精度"的检测器构建问题，通过合成极端天气损坏样本、基于七模型集成估计扰动敏感度并据此安排课程式训练，同时窄化网络结构并用离线邻域密度测量决定各采样阶段的点数与近邻数，在KITTI等设置上同时提升损坏条件下AP并显著降低延迟与峰值显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LiDAR的3D目标检测是自动驾驶的核心感知服务，实际部署要求对点云损坏具有鲁棒性，同时满足推理延迟与峰值GPU内存约束。已有工作将鲁棒性与资源效率分开处理：紧凑检测器在损坏下脆弱，鲁棒检测器部署代价高。因此需要统一地构建既鲁棒又高效的检测器。

Method: 1）构造模拟极端天气的损坏样本；2）用7个异构3D检测器组成集成，通过置信度变化、BEV重叠和未匹配检测数估计扰动敏感度，据此对干净样本与损坏样本排序，离线以课程学习方式训练候选检测器，使模型更多从困难损坏样本中学习；3）为提升效率，窄化网络，并利用离线邻域密度测量决定各采样阶段处理的点数与近邻数。

Result: 评价指标为干净/损坏条件下的AP、推理延迟与峰值分配GPU内存。在KITTI上用PointRCNN做Car检测时，损坏条件下Moderate AP比原始检测器提升3.32点，平均延迟降低19.8%，峰值分配GPU内存降低53.9%。在两个数据集、两种检测器架构、三个类别上，损坏条件下AP平均提升4.03点，平均推理延迟和峰值分配GPU内存分别降低14.4%和38.0%。

Conclusion: 将鲁棒性与资源效率纳入同一约束优化框架是可行且有效的：敏感度驱动的损坏样本课程训练带来鲁棒性收益，结构窄化与密度感知的采样配置带来效率收益，二者结合可在多种数据集、架构和类别上稳定实现"更准、更快、更省显存"。代码已开源。

Abstract: LiDAR-based 3D object detection is a core perception service for autonomous driving, but deployment requires robustness to point-cloud corruption while meeting latency and memory requirements. Existing approaches address robustness and resource efficiency separately, resulting in compact detectors that are fragile under corruption or robust detectors that remain costly to deploy. We therefore formulate LiDAR perception provisioning as constructing a detector that remains accurate under point-cloud corruptions while meeting constraints on inference latency and peak GPU memory. To achieve robustness, we first craft corrupted samples that mimic extreme weather conditions. Then we train candidate detectors offline by ordering clean and corrupted samples by perturbation sensitivity, through which detectors learn more from hard corrupted samples. We estimate perturbation sensitivity using an ensemble of seven heterogeneous 3D detectors by tracking changes in confidence, BEV overlap, and unmatched detections. To improve efficiency, we narrow the network and use offline neighborhood-density measurements to determine how many points and neighbors each sampling stage processes. We evaluate our method using AP (Average Precision) under clean and corrupted conditions, inference latency, and peak allocated GPU memory. On KITTI for Car detection with PointRCNN, our method improves Moderate AP under corruption by 3.32 points over the original detector while reducing mean latency by 19.8% and peak allocated GPU memory by 53.9%. Across two datasets, two detector architectures, and three object classes, our method improves AP under corruption by 4.03 points on average over the corresponding original detectors, while reducing mean inference latency and peak allocated GPU memory by 14.4% and 38.0%, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/drunklibai/robust-efficient-lidar-perception.

</details>


### [50] [PACE-MoE: Adaptive Domain Sampling and Domain-Specific LoRA for Medical Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27656)
*Zhenxuan Wang,Jisheng Dang,Ronghao Lin,Bimei Wang,Jingwen Zhao,Wenjun Ma,Bin Hu,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PACE-MoE，一个策略引导框架，联合进行自适应域采样与域特定参数适配，用于多域医学视觉定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多域后训练策略依赖固定的域采样和共享参数更新，忽视了"哪些域需要训练"和"更新施加在何处"的适配需求。

Method: PACE 依据当前策略反馈评估各影像域的训练价值，通过域准入、回放和最终全域网均衡逐步调整域覆盖；同时使用域特定的 LoRA 适配器将更新路由到对应影像域，并保持预训练主干与视觉编码器不变。该设计与原始奖励形式及 GRPO 目标兼容，无需修改。

Result: 在七个医学影像域上，相比固定域训练持续提升定位性能，平均 box IoU 相对 Vanilla 基线提升 3.64%；在相同域特定适配器架构下，仅自适应采样即带来 2.07% 的平均 box IoU 提升。

Conclusion: 协同的数据-参数路由是多域医学视觉定位的有效策略。

Abstract: Medical visual grounding requires models to align clinical descriptions with spatial evidence across heterogeneous imaging domains. However, existing multi-domain post-training strategies typically rely on fixed domain sampling and shared parameter updates, overlooking the need to adapt both which domains receive training and where their updates are applied. To address this issue, we propose PACE-MoE, a policy-guided framework that jointly performs adaptive domain sampling and domain-specific adaptation for medical visual grounding. PACE dynamically assesses the training value of each imaging domain from current-policy feedback and progressively adjusts domain coverage through domain admission, replay, and final all-domain balancing. Meanwhile, domain-specific LoRA adapters route updates to the corresponding imaging domains while preserving the pretrained backbone and visual encoder. This coordinated design aligns training-data allocation with parameter adaptation without modifying the original reward formulation or Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) objective. Experiments across seven medical imaging domains show that PACE-MoE consistently improves grounding performance over fixed-domain training, achieving a 3.64% relative improvement in mean box IoU over the Vanilla baseline. Under matched domain-specific adapter architectures, adaptive sampling alone further improves mean box IoU by 2.07%, demonstrating the benefit of policy-guided domain allocation. These results establish coordinated data-parameter routing as an effective strategy for multi-domain medical visual grounding.

</details>


### [51] [Reusing Assurance Evidence in AI Pipelines: Validity Conditions, a Fail-Closed Release Gate, and a Replay of 714 Public Data Version Control Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27672)
*Atanu Mukhopadhyay,Shravya Reddy Nalla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文指出AI流水线中复用先前治理控制结果的风险：仅当声明依赖完备且发布门禁校验证据与候选版本一致时复用才安全。作者形式化了签名证据记录的有效性与change-local重执行等价于全量重跑的充分条件，并实证测量依赖缺失导致的陈旧复用问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对AI流水线每次变更后重跑所有治理控制代价高昂，团队因此复用旧结果；但依赖声明不完备或门禁不校验证据归属时，复用会产生不安全的发布决策，需量化该失效模式的普遍程度。

Method: (1) 形式化定义签名证据记录对某候选版本的有效性，给出change-local重执行与全量重跑得到相同发布决策的充分条件；(2) 用Ed25519数字签名实现参考门禁并与另两种门禁进行9种操纵场景的对比；(3) 回放714个公开DVC流水线的38,083次提交，测量按声明依赖复用stage时的陈旧复用率，并抽样40个事件人工审计；(4) 评估静态导入闭包、影子重执行、"不明变更后全量重跑"三种补救策略。

Result: 参考门禁阻断9种操纵场景中的8种，仅信任规划器的门禁阻断1种、仅验签门禁阻断3种，而策略未声明的依赖可绕过所有门禁。实测中按声明依赖复用可节省83.4%的stage执行，但3.3%的提交复用了导入代码或引用配置已变更的stage，影响31%的流水线；人工审计在40个抽样事件中确认36个存在未声明依赖，评估类stage（其输出即证据）风险最高。将每个stage的静态导入闭包纳入依赖可把陈旧复用降至0.4%且仍节省81.6%的执行，影子重执行收益远低，不明变更后全量重跑仅节省45.3%。

Conclusion: 依赖声明完备性与门禁证据校验是安全复用的两个必要条件，而现实中前者常被违反且影响广泛。将静态导入闭包纳入依赖声明能在保持高复用率的同时几乎消除陈旧复用，是优于影子重执行和粗放式全量重跑的方案。

Abstract: Rerunning every governance control after every change to an artificial intelligence (AI) pipeline is expensive, so teams reuse earlier results. Reuse is safe only if each control's declared dependencies cover everything that can change its outcome, and only if the release gate checks that reused evidence describes the candidate being released. This article makes both conditions explicit and measures how often the first fails in practice. It defines when a signed evidence record is valid for a candidate and gives sufficient conditions under which change-local re-execution reaches the same release decision as rerunning every control. A reference gate implementing these checks with Ed25519 digital signatures blocks eight of nine manipulation scenarios, against one for a gate that trusts the planner and three for a gate that only verifies signatures; a dependency the policy never declared passes every gate. A replay of 38,083 commits from 714 public Data Version Control (DVC) pipelines measures that failure mode. Reusing stage results according to declared dependencies avoids 83.4% of stage executions, but in 3.3% of commits it reuses a stage whose imported code or referenced configuration changed, and 31% of pipelines are affected. A manual audit confirms the undeclared dependency in 36 of 40 sampled events. Evaluation stages, whose outputs are the evidence, are the most exposed. Adding each stage's static import closure lowers stale reuse to 0.4% while still avoiding 81.6% of executions; sampled shadow re-execution removes far less, and rerunning everything after unexplained changes avoids only 45.3%.

</details>


### [52] [NeoWorld-3: From Pixels to Code-Defined Physical Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27690)
*Eachen Soong,Zijian Niu,Haoran Xu,Yihong Dong,Yunbo Wang,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NeoWorld-3 是一个编码智能体框架，通过可调、可执行的程序从视觉观测重建可直接用于仿真的交互式三维世界，并在实机双臂机器人上验证了 Real-Sim-Real 闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒式三维生成方法只追求视觉真实感，缺乏物理可交互性，无法直接用于仿真与机器人操作。论文希望重建结果同时具备几何保真度与"物理就绪"属性。

Method: 框架由两部分核心组成：NeoSDK 提供底层计算引擎，负责几何表示、部件组合、可微参数精修与物理校验；NeoMCP 将这些能力封装为结构化、可工具调用的 API，供视觉语言智能体进行视觉检查、代码编辑与程序迭代优化。整体采用层级式协调者-工作者架构，可从小型铰接物体扩展至复杂组合场景。

Result: 在物体级与场景级重建基准上均取得最优的几何保真度与物理就绪性；在重建数字孪生中规划的操作轨迹可直接在真实双臂机器人上零样本执行成功，完成 Real-Sim-Real 闭环。

Conclusion: 以可执行程序为中间表示、以智能体迭代精修为手段的重建范式，能够弥合视觉生成与物理仿真之间的鸿沟，为机器人仿真训练与数字孪生构建提供可行路径。

Abstract: Simulation-ready world reconstruction demands physical interactivity beyond visual realism, which existing black-box 3D generation fails to deliver. We present NeoWorld-3, a coding-agent framework that reconstructs interactive 3D worlds from visual observations via tunable, executable programs. At its core, NeoSDK provides the underlying computational engine for low-level geometries, part composition, differentiable parameter refinement, and physics validation. At the same time, NeoMCP exposes these capabilities as structured, tool-callable APIs for vision-language agents to visually inspect, edit code, and iteratively refine programs. Through a hierarchical coordinator-worker architecture, the framework scales seamlessly from standalone articulated objects to complex compositional scenes. Evaluations across object-level and scene-level reconstruction benchmarks demonstrate that NeoWorld-3 achieves state-of-the-art geometric fidelity and physical readiness. Crucially, manipulation trajectories planned within our reconstructed twins achieve successful zero-shot execution on a physical dual-arm robot, completing the Real-Sim-Real loop.

</details>


### [53] [A Principled Optimal Transport Framework for Frame Selection in Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27692)
*Ziwei Zhao,Yuhang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OTFS，将长视频关键帧选择建模为结构化证据分配问题，用半不平衡熵正则最优传输把多源证据映射到视频帧，配合覆盖感知的精确动态规划抽取子集，形成免训练两阶段流水线，在三个长视频理解基准上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解受限于模型视觉上下文预算与视频长度不匹配，必须做帧选择。现有查询感知方法多为启发式管线（给帧打一维查询相关分数后采样/排序/抽取），缺乏显式优化目标，难以捕捉复杂推理所需的异构证据。

Method: 从优化视角将帧选择形式化为结构化证据分配问题，提出最优传输框架 OTFS，把多个证据源映射到视频帧；为体现"重要证据应保留、冗余帧无需承载质量"的非对称性，采用半不平衡熵正则最优传输目标，并给出高效求解器 OTFS-Sinkhorn；再结合覆盖感知子集抽取的精确动态规划。整体为免训练的两阶段流水线。理论上证明单源情形下该形式导出帧上的 Gibbs 型分布，使 Q-Frame 的温度缩放分布成为其特例。

Result: 在三个长视频理解基准上，OTFS 一致优于强免训练基线；并提供代码。

Conclusion: OTFS 为长视频帧选择提供了有原理依据的最优传输新视角，既统一并推广了既有查询感知方法（如 Q-Frame），又在实证上取得稳定提升，代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-video understanding with multimodal LLMs is fundamentally constrained by the mismatch between video length and the model's limited visual context budget, making frame selection essential for practical reasoning. Existing query-aware methods typically rely on heuristic pipelines: they assign one-dimensional query-conditioned scores to frames and then apply sampling, ranking, or extraction rules to form the final subset. While effective in practice, such methods are generally not derived from an explicit optimization objective and struggle to capture the heterogeneous evidence required for complex reasoning. In this paper, we revisit long-video frame selection from a principled optimization perspective. We formulate it as a structured evidence-allocation problem and propose OTFS, an optimal transport framework that maps multiple evidence sources onto video frames. To reflect the asymmetry of this problem---important evidence should be preserved, whereas redundant frames need not absorb mass---we instantiate this view with a semi-unbalanced entropic optimal transport objective and an efficient solver, OTFS-Sinkhorn. Combined with an exact dynamic program for coverage-aware subset extraction, OTFS yields a practical two-stage training-free pipeline. We further show that, in the single-source case, our formulation induces a Gibbs-type distribution over frames, making Q-Frame's temperature-scaled distribution a special case of our framework while also providing a broader perspective for understanding related prior methods. Experiments on three long-video understanding benchmarks show that OTFS consistently outperforms strong training-free baselines. Codes are available at https://github.com/barry664/OTFS.

</details>


### [54] [PanoGraphGS: Unifying Panoptic Segmentation and Hierarchical 3D Scene Graphs with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27696)
*Jinghan Gao,Siyu Wang,Dongyu Feng,Yiyuan Pan,Rong Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanoGraphGS 将全景三维高斯表示学习与层次化 3D 场景图构建统一到一个框架中，并发布实时全景场景理解数据集 NaviGS，在对象节点准确率上平均超过最强基线 50 个百分点以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导导航需要 3D 场景图来组织物理实体及其容纳与空间关系，而构建这种结构化地图要求场景表示在不同视角下能一致地区分语义区域与对象实例。现有表示往往难以做到跨视角的全景一致性。

Method: 在共享高斯几何上学习语义与实例特征；用基于原型的语义学习和基于关联的实例学习获得跨视角一致的全景预测；该表示支持新视角全景分割与直接的 Gaussian 级全景解码；再用一个语义感知场景图模块恢复由楼层、房间、stuff 与 object 组成的层次结构及容纳与空间关系。

Result: 在 Replica 与 NaviGS 上，3D 场景图识别的对象节点准确率平均比最强基线高出 50 个百分点以上；2D 全景分割在所评估方法中达到 SOTA。同时发布了提供 3DGS、网格、RGB、语义与深度数据的 NaviGS 真实世界数据集。

Conclusion: 结果说明 PanoGraphGS 在层次化 3D 场景理解与 2D 全景分割两方面均有效，可作为支持语言引导导航的统一场景表示方案。

Abstract: 3D scene graphs support language-guided navigation by organizing physical entities and their containment and spatial relations into hierarchical maps. Building such structured maps requires a scene representation that consistently distinguishes semantic regions and object instances across viewpoints. To this end, we present PanoGraphGS, a unified framework that integrates panoptic 3D Gaussian representation learning with hierarchical 3D scene graph construction. The framework learns semantic and instance features on shared Gaussian geometry. Prototype-based semantic learning and association-based instance learning produce consistent panoptic predictions across viewpoints. The learned representation supports novel-view panoptic segmentation and direct Gaussian-level panoptic decoding. A semantic-aware scene graph module further recovers a floor-room-stuff-object hierarchy with containment and spatial relations. We further release NaviGS, a real-world dataset for panoramic scene understanding. The dataset provides 3DGS, mesh, RGB, semantic, and depth data. For 3D scene graph identification, PanoGraphGS outperforms the strongest evaluated baseline in object-node accuracy by more than 50\% points on average across Replica and NaviGS. PanoGraphGS also achieves state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated methods in 2D panoptic segmentation. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of PanoGraphGS in both hierarchical 3D scene understanding and 2D panoptic segmentation. The project website is available at: https://github.com/gaojh0333/PanoGraphGS.

</details>


### [55] [VLA-Retain: Benchmarking Cross-Dimension Effects of Vision Tuning in VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27698)
*Borui Xu,Han Chen,Bingsheng He,Ming Wu,Jason Zeng,Michael Heinrich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VLA-Retain 基准，在 LIBERO-Plus 的七个扰动维度上比较 5 个开放 VLA 模型微调前后的表现，发现目标域视觉微调并不局部，目标增益无法预测保留能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型对观测条件变化敏感，现有做法是在目标域数据上微调视觉模块，但已有基准只评估未微调模型，适配研究也只报告目标增益，因此无法判断这种适配是否损害其他条件下的鲁棒性，需区分目标增益与非目标变化、隔离适配与架构差异、排除泛化遗忘并比较不同调优策略。

Method: 构建 VLA-Retain 基准：选取 5 个开放 VLA 模型，分别在相机视角或传感器噪声数据上微调，并与各自原始基座检查点在 LIBERO-Plus 数据集（覆盖 7 个扰动维度）上对比评测。

Result: 四点发现：①目标视觉微调并非局部，10 个模型-域配置中 8 个非目标平均增益为负；相机视角微调下 X-VLA 目标提升 69.4 个百分点但其余平均下降 4.6，OpenVLA-OFT 分别提升 39.3 与 1.4。②退化不能仅用同任务无扰动下的性能损失解释。③更广的训练覆盖能提升总体增益，但不能消除维度级退化。④调优策略依赖模型：DELTA 式特征保持使 X-VLA 非目标平均增益升至 +1.2，却使 OpenVLA-OFT 降至 -23.1。

Conclusion: 仅凭目标域成功不足以评判 VLA 适配，跨维度保留能力应作为定向适配的关键评价标准。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models transfer knowledge from large-scale pretraining into robot control, but their manipulation performance remains sensitive to changes in observation conditions, which recent work addresses by fine-tuning the visual module on target-domain data. However, it remains unclear how such adaptation affects robustness beyond the target condition, as existing robustness benchmarks only evaluate models without fine-tuning and adaptation studies only report the target gain. Measuring this cross-dimension effect requires separating target gains from off-target changes, isolating adaptation effects from architectural differences, ruling out generic forgetting, and comparing tuning choices. Therefore, we introduce VLA-Retain, a benchmark that compares five open VLA models, tuned on Camera-view or Sensor-noise data, with their own base checkpoints on LIBERO-Plus dataset spanning seven perturbation dimensions. Our study reveals four findings. First, targeted vision tuning is not local, and its target gain does not predict retention. Mean off-target gain is negative in 8 of 10 model--domain configurations. Under Camera-view tuning, X-VLA gains $69.4$ percentage points on the target but loses $4.6$ points on average elsewhere, whereas OpenVLA-OFT gains $39.3$ and $1.4$ points, respectively. Second, these regressions are not explained only by performance loss on the same tasks without perturbations. Third, broader training coverage raises overall gain over target-specific training but does not eliminate dimension-level regressions. Fourth, tuning strategies are model-dependent, as DELTA-style feature preservation raises X-VLA's mean off-target gain to $+1.2$ points but lowers OpenVLA-OFT's to $-23.1$. Overall, these findings show that target-domain success alone is insufficient and highlight cross-dimensional retention as a key criterion for targeted VLA adaptation.

</details>


### [56] [VEOcc++: Learning Persistent Voxel Feature Maps for Embodied Online Semantic Occupancy Mapping and Offline Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27704)
*Ruoyu Wang,Jiahan Li,Yong Liu,Kexin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VEOcc++ 是一个统一的体素中心框架，通过持久稀疏体素特征图把在线增量建图与离线全局地图精修两阶段连接起来，在 EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet 上取得 67.12% IoU 与 58.40% mIoU 的在线增量建图 SOTA，离线精修后进一步提升至 67.79% IoU 与 59.37% mIoU，并在移动机器人上实现零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义占据建图同时需要满足两类互补需求：一是在因果约束下在线增量维护最新全局地图，二是在探索结束后借助额外算力离线精修完整的场景表示。现有方法往往割裂处理这两个阶段，缺少共享表示的统一下来。

Method: 提出 VEOcc++，以持久稀疏体素特征图为核心统一两阶段：探索阶段地图随新观测区域扩展，通过可靠性引导的更新融合对齐后的局部特征，并由上下文感知解码生成当前语义占据地图；探索结束后，将缓存的局部特征整合为互补证据，与已完成的在线表示结合进行多尺度空间精修。

Result: 在 EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet 上在线增量建图达到 67.12% IoU 与 58.40% mIoU，刷新 SOTA；在局部预测器固定的条件下，其在线流程相比 VEOcc 融合提升 3.70 IoU 与 3.74 mIoU，说明全局建图设计的显著收益；离线精修仅使用探索期观测即把完整地图提升到 67.79% IoU 与 59.37% mIoU；移动机器人零样本部署验证了向未见环境的可迁移性、机载可行性与大规模室内建图的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过持久稀疏体素特征图统一在线增量建图与离线全局精修，VEOcc++ 同时提升了实时性与最终地图质量，且具备良好的迁移性与实际部署能力。

Abstract: Semantic occupancy mapping supports embodied perception during exploration and detailed scene understanding afterward, giving rise to two complementary requirements: online incremental mapping for maintaining an up-to-date global map under causal constraints, and offline global map refinement for improving the completed scene representation with additional computation. We present VEOcc++, a unified voxel-centric framework that connects both stages through a persistent sparse voxel feature map. During exploration, the map expands with newly observed regions and integrates aligned local features through reliability-guided updates, while context-aware decoding produces the current semantic occupancy map. After exploration, cached local features are consolidated as complementary evidence and combined with the completed online representation for multi-scale spatial refinement. On EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet, VEOcc++ achieves 67.12\% IoU and 58.40\% mIoU, establishing new state-of-the-art performance for online incremental mapping. With the local predictor fixed, our online mapping pipeline outperforms VEOcc fusion by 3.70 IoU and 3.74 mIoU points, demonstrating substantial gains from the proposed global mapping design. Offline global map refinement further improves the completed map to 67.79% IoU and 59.37% mIoU using only the observations acquired during exploration. Zero-shot deployment on a mobile robot further demonstrates transferability to unseen environments, onboard feasibility, and scalability to large-scale indoor mapping. Project page: https://wryzju.github.io/VEOccpp/.

</details>


### [57] [RLSplat: Resource-Linear Budget Control for Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27712)
*Injae Kim,Minseok Joo,Jihwan Park,Sehyung Kim,Minseong Bae,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RLSplat 提出一种资源线性的预算可控前馈 3D 高斯泼溅框架，其表示规模、推理延迟与峰值显存均随高斯预算减少而线性下降，且通过区域化预算分配策略与分层近邻注意力解码，在零样本评测中每个预算档位均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预算可控 FF3DGS 方法虽然能在推理时调整高斯数量以满足存储或渲染延迟约束，但忽略了推理本身的成本：它们必须先预测稠密高斯集再剪枝或选择，导致延迟与峰值显存几乎与预算无关（近乎恒定）。因此需要一个真正"资源随预算线性缩放"的框架。

Method: 提出 RLSplat：(1) 不做剪枝/选择，而是学习一个高斯分配策略，将预算分配到局部场景区域，直接生成恰好所需数量的高斯；(2) 采用资源线性的高斯解码，每个高斯仅通过高效的层次化近邻搜索与邻近高空间关联，使解码成本随高斯数线性下降。

Result: 零样本评测中，RLSplat 在每个预算档位都超过先前预算可控方法；即使高斯数量少 4 倍也优于最强基线；在低预算下推理速度最高快 4.1 倍，峰值显存低 9.6 倍。

Conclusion: 通过将预算分配与近邻注意力解码结合，RLSplat 实现了表示规模、延迟与显存对预算的线性响应，显著提升了 FF3DGS 在受限资源场景下的实用性。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (FF3DGS) reconstructs 3D scenes from images in a single forward pass, making it well-suited to applications that must operate within limited resource budgets. To further improve its practicality, recent budget-controllable FF3DGS methods allow the number of Gaussians to be adjusted at inference, so that the output meets a given storage limit or rendering-latency constraint. However, these methods overlook the cost of inference itself: to produce a representation with the target number of Gaussians, they first predict a dense set of Gaussians and then prune or select from it, requiring nearly constant latency and peak memory regardless of the budget. We therefore propose RLSplat, a resource-linear budget-controllable FF3DGS framework whose representation size, inference latency, and peak memory all decrease linearly as the Gaussian budget is reduced. Instead of pruning or selection, RLSplat learns a Gaussian allocation policy that distributes the budget across local scene regions, so that exactly the requested number of Gaussians is generated. These Gaussians are then refined through resource-linear Gaussian decoding, in which each Gaussian attends only to nearby Gaussians found via an efficient hierarchical neighbor search, so that the decoding cost decreases linearly with fewer Gaussians. In zero-shot evaluation, RLSplat outperforms prior budget-controllable methods at every budget, surpassing the strongest baseline even with 4$\times$ fewer Gaussians, while running up to 4.1$\times$ faster with 9.6$\times$ less peak memory at low budgets.

</details>


### [58] [Push-Pull Attentional Anchoring for Diffusion Concept Erasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27719)
*Nattanat Chatthee,Tagon Sompong,Ekapol Chuangsuwanich,Supasorn Suwajanakorn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Push-Pull Attentional Anchoring (PPAA)，在交叉注意力空间中以有界余弦相似度约束锚定擦除强度，实现概念擦除的同时保持非目标生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型带来隐私、有害内容与版权风险，需从预训练模型中移除不良概念。但现有概念擦除方法依赖启发式强度缩放（如 CFG 或负提示缩放），导致非目标生成质量下降与不可控的过度语义漂移。

Method: 在交叉注意力空间中推离目标表示，同时用有界余弦相似度约束锚定擦除尺度；采用相对相似度比值而非绝对差值构造自适应阈值，使其表示语义保留的百分比，从而可用单一数值在多层间统一应用。

Result: 在名人、艺术风格、裸露内容与知识产权等多个概念类别上开展实验，在 Stable Diffusion v1.4 上相较已有方法取得更优的权衡，并证明可适用于 FLUX 等现代架构。

Conclusion: PPAA 通过有界相对相似度约束实现可控的概念擦除，避免启发式强度调参带来的语义漂移，兼顾擦除有效性与非目标生成质量，并具备跨架构适用性。

Abstract: Rapid advances in diffusion models have raised concerns about privacy, harmful content, and copyright violations. Concept erasure methods aim to address these risks by removing undesirable concepts from pretrained models without full retraining. However, existing approaches often degrade non-target generation quality through heuristic strength scaling (e.g., classifier-free guidance or negative prompt scaling), leading to uncontrolled and excessive semantic drift. To address this, we propose Push-Pull Attentional Anchoring (PPAA), a mechanism in the cross-attention space that displaces target representations while anchoring the scale of erasure via bounded cosine-similarity constraints. By using a relative similarity ratio instead of an absolute difference, our adaptive threshold represents a meaningful percentage of semantic retention, facilitating a single value to be applied uniformly across layers. We conduct extensive experiments across multiple concept categories, including celebrities, artistic styles, nudity, and intellectual property. Our method achieves favorable trade-offs against prior approaches on Stable Diffusion v1.4 and demonstrates its applicability to modern architectures such as FLUX.

</details>


### [59] [World2HOSI: A Multi-Expert Harness for Long-Horizon Human-Object-Scene Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27725)
*Jiushu Li,Haoran Wang,Yumeng Liu,Zhiyang Dou,Yuan Liu,Yuexin Ma,Cheng Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 World2HOSI——一个多专家编排框架，从 3DGS 场景与自然语言指令生成连续的长时程人-物-场景交互数据，核心观点是长时程 HOSI 的关键在于编排而非单一更强模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程人-物-场景交互（HOSI）对具身智能、动画与游戏至关重要，但现有 HOI 与 HSI 模型各自只擅长单一技能领域，无法覆盖需要多种交互技能的任务；而训练统一 HOSI 模型又受限于数据稀缺、采集成本高昂。

Method: 构建以持久化场景状态为中心的闭环框架：由解耦的背景与物体资产组成结构化共享场景状态；编排器（harness）在该状态上规划动作阶段，通过专家各自的原生条件接口调用专家，并将每个动作效果回写状态，使每次调用都锚定于不断演化的世界。同时使用有界残差优化保证人体运动物理合理性、用接触驱动物理仿真保证物体操作合理性，并保持交互意图不变。

Result: 实验与感知用户研究表明，在评估的 HOSI 任务上，World2HOSI 取得最佳的任务完成度与交互质量，同时生成的数据更具多样性。

Conclusion: 长时程 HOSI 的突破点在于共享场景状态下的多专家编排，而非单一更强模型；World2HOSI 验证了无需额外训练统一模型即可组合现有专家完成超出各自能力的任务，并作为可扩展的数据生成手段缓解 HOSI 数据瓶颈。

Abstract: Long-horizon human-object-scene interaction (HOSI) is central to embodied AI, animation, and game development. Existing human-object interaction (HOI) and human-scene interaction (HSI) models excel in their respective domains, but none alone covers tasks requiring multiple interaction skills. Training unified HOSI models is hindered by scarce and costly data. We introduce World2HOSI, a multi-expert orchestration harness that generates continuous HOSI data from 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scenes and natural language instructions. Our key insight is that long-horizon HOSI hinges not on a stronger single model but on orchestration. Given a shared, structured scene state and an orchestration harness grounded in it, existing experts can jointly accomplish tasks beyond the reach of any of them alone. World2HOSI realizes this insight as a closed loop around a persistent scene state built from decoupled background and object assets. A harness plans action stages over this state, invokes experts via their native conditioning interfaces, and writes each action's effects back, so that every invocation is grounded in the evolving world. To reduce physical artifacts, bounded residual optimization and contact-driven physics simulation further ensure the physical plausibility of human motion and object manipulation, respectively, while preserving interaction intent. Experiments and a perceptual user study show that World2HOSI achieves the best task completion and interaction quality on the evaluated HOSI tasks, while generating more diverse data.

</details>


### [60] [CILA: Confusion-based Instance-wise Logit Adjustment for Long-Tailed Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27728)
*Jiyoung Lim,Jee-Hyong Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两种互补方法CILA与BNI，用实例级混淆度自适应调整logit，恢复被类级调整抑制的有用梯度，提升长尾类学习与泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据天然类别不平衡，模型偏向多数类。现有logit调整方法仅按类频率做类级调整，忽略实例级混淆，常压制易混淆非目标类的有用信号，导致这些类学习不足、预测不确定性升高、泛化受限。

Method: 1) Confusion-based Instance-wise Logit Adjustment (CILA)：依据目标类与非目标类之间的实例级混淆度自适应调整logit，恢复被类级调整抑制的信息性梯度；2) Batch-adaptation Noise-Injection (BNI)：训练时对目标logit加扰动，把混淆感知更新扩展到已正确分类但接近边界的样本。CILA为与损失无关的即插即用模块。

Result: CILA可无缝集成到现有长尾学习方法中，在多个基准上稳定提升性能。

Conclusion: 实例级混淆感知的logit调整加噪声注入是缓解类级调整缺陷的有效互补方案，并具备良好通用性与可插拔性；代码将开源。

Abstract: Real-world datasets are inherently class-imbalanced, leading models to favor majority classes and struggle with minority ones. Existing logit adjustment methods address this by modifying logits based on class frequencies, but their class-wise adjustments ignore instance-wise confusion and often suppress informative signals from confusing non-target classes. This results in insufficient learning of such classes, increased prediction uncertainty, and limited generalization.
  To address this, we propose two complementary methods. First, \textbf{Confusion-based Instance-wise Logit Adjustment (CILA)} adaptively adjusts logits based on instance-wise confusion between the target and non-target classes, restoring informative gradients that class-wise adjustment suppresses. Second, \textbf{Batch-adaptation Noise-Injection (BNI)} perturbs the target logit during training, extending the confusion-aware update to correctly classified but near-boundary samples that the penalty alone cannot reach. CILA is a loss-agnostic plug-in module that can be seamlessly integrated into existing long-tailed learning methods, and consistently improves performance across multiple benchmarks. Our code will be available at https://github.com/maya0395/CILA.

</details>


### [61] [Revealing Inter-Channel Modality Discriminability: Channel Selective Interaction Fusion Network for Optical-SAR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27757)
*Haojie Li,Yuanfei Huang,Lingfei Song,Jia Li,Lizhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出通道选择性交互融合网络CSIF-Net，用于SAR与光学遥感图像跨模态目标检测，在OGSOD 1.0与M4-SAR数据集上取得水平框与旋转框检测的最优性能，分别提升4.01%和3.18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR与光学图像可提供互补的结构与外观信息，但二者存在显著的跨模态差异，且不加区分的通道交互会引入冗余或冲突特征。由于不同特征通道编码异质语义信息、对跨模态融合的贡献不均，因此需要选择性地强化任务相关通道、抑制冗余通道。

Method: CSIF-Net包含三部分：一是频率分解模块构建频率感知的SAR输入，将结构信息与噪声响应分离；二是跨注意力通道选择模块建模跨模态通道关系，并将特征动态划分为共享成分与模态特有成分子；三是双分支融合模块，对共享特征进行跨模态交互与通道重对齐，同时独立增强SAR特有特征。

Result: 在OGSOD 1.0和M4-SAR数据集上的大量实验表明，CSIF-Net在水平框与旋转框目标检测任务上均达到当前最优性能，相比现有方法分别取得4.01%和3.18%的提升。

Conclusion: 通过通道级选择性跨模态交互，CSIF-Net有效利用SAR与光学信息的互补性并抑制模态干扰，验证了选择性通道融合在跨模态遥感目标检测中的有效性。

Abstract: Synthetic aperture radar (SAR) and optical imagery provide complementary structural and appearance information for remote sensing object detection. However, substantial cross-modal discrepancies and indiscriminate channel interaction inevitably introduce redundant or conflicting features. Since different feature channels encode heterogeneous semantic information and contribute unequally to cross-modal fusion, selectively highlighting task-relevant channels while suppressing redundant ones is essential. To address this issue, we propose the Channel Selective Interaction Fusion Network (CSIF-Net), which performs selective channel-level cross-modal interaction. CSIF-Net first employs a frequency decomposition module to construct frequency-aware SAR inputs, facilitating the separation of structural information from noise-related responses. It then models cross-modal channel relationships through a cross-attention channel selection module and dynamically partitions the features into shared and modality-specific components. Finally, a dual-branch fusion module performs cross-modal interaction and channel realignment for shared features while independently enhancing SAR-specific features, thereby exploiting complementary information and suppressing modality interference. Extensive experiments on the OGSOD 1.0 and M4-SAR datasets demonstrate that CSIF-Net achieves state-of-the-art performance in both horizontal and oriented object detection, surpassing the existing methods by 4.01% and 3.18% gains, respectively.

</details>


### [62] [PixelSR: Pixel-Space Orthogonal Flow Decoupling for Real-World Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27759)
*Xiang Lv,Kai Zhao,Qiang Wang,Qiao Mo,Chao Zhou,Mingwen Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出单阶段像素空间扩散Transformer框架PixelSR，通过像素空间条件扩散与正交流解耦，在真实图像超分中同时提升感知真实感与结构保真度，缓解二者间的跷跷板效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实世界图像超分（Real-SR）中的潜在扩散模型存在"跷跷板效应"：提升感知真实感会损害结构保真度。作者认为这并非扩散模型固有缺陷，而是主流两阶段框架（依赖VAE压缩）的架构约束所致。

Method: 提出单阶段像素扩散Transformer（PixelSR），直接在像素/RGB空间端到端优化，绕开VAE压缩。核心为两项设计：①像素空间条件扩散，融合空间对齐的LR条件、视觉语义引导与轻量纹理精修；②正交流解耦，将非对称流目标分解为低秩语义分量与无噪正交纹理分量，分别送入专用分支，降低相互干扰。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，PixelSR取得了有竞争力的性能，并能有效缓解保真度—真实感之间的跷跷板效应，在恢复感知真实细节的同时忠实保留结构内容。

Conclusion: 消除两阶段VAE架构约束、采用像素空间单阶段扩散并结合流目标解耦，可在Real-SR中实现真实感与保真度的更好平衡，为扩散式超分提供了新的架构思路。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models have recently achieved remarkable progress in Real-world Image Super-Resolution (Real-SR). However, existing methods consistently exhibit a persistent seesaw effect: enhancing perceptual realism compromises structural fidelity. This effect, we argue, is not intrinsic to diffusion models, but largely stems from the architectural constraints of prevailing two-stage frameworks. To address this challenge, we propose PixelSR, a novel single-stage Pixel Diffusion Transformer for Real-SR that operates directly in pixel space. Specifically, PixelSR consists of two core designs: Pixel-space Conditional Diffusion and Orthogonal Flow Decoupling. The former establishes an effective pixel-space restoration framework by integrating spatially aligned LR conditioning, visual semantic guidance, and lightweight texture refinement, thereby bypassing VAE compression and enabling end-to-end optimization directly in RGB space. Complementarily, the latter decomposes the asymmetric flow target into a low-rank semantic component and a noise-free orthogonal texture component. By routing these components to specialized branches, it achieves decoupled optimization with reduced mutual interference. Benefiting from these designs, our PixelSR restores perceptually realistic details while faithfully preserving structural content. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that PixelSR not only achieves competitive performance, but also effectively mitigates the fidelity--realism seesaw effect.

</details>


### [63] [Radar-Based 3D Human Pose Estimation via Motion-Aware Pose Initialization and Observation-Guided Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27774)
*Woo-Jin Jung,Chan-Bin Lim,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段毫米波雷达3D人体姿态估计框架I2R-Pose，并构建RaMoS-Pose数据集，MPJPE分别降低39.50%和15.13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内场景（如智能家居）需要3D人体姿态估计来分析运动与监测状态；毫米波雷达不受光照影响且不采集人体外观图像，适合该任务，但雷达观测常存在部分身体部位信息不足、多部位反射重叠的问题，限制关节定位精度。

Method: 提出I2R-Pose两阶段框架：(1) 运动感知姿态初始化——结合连续雷达观测的时序关系与人体骨架结构学习逐关节特征，弥补关节信息不足并估计初始3D姿态序列；(2) 观测引导精化——用单独提取的雷达观测特征预测逐关节位置修正量，精化初始姿态得到最终序列。框架支持时间-多普勒/时间-距离（TD/TR）图与雷达点云（PC）的单独或融合输入；同时构建RaMoS-Pose数据集，提供同步的TD/TR图、点云与3D关节真值。

Result: 在RaMoS-Pose与公开MVDoppler-Pose数据集上评估，融合输入下相较各自数据集上最佳现有方法，MPJPE在MVDoppler-Pose上降低39.50%，在RaMoS-Pose上降低15.13%。

Conclusion: 时序+骨架结构建模与观测引导的逐关节精化可有效缓解雷达信息不足与反射重叠问题，多模态融合输入进一步提升3D人体姿态估计精度。

Abstract: 3D Human Pose Estimation (3D HPE) is used in indoor environments, such as smart homes, to analyze human movement and monitor human states. Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar is suitable for this task because it is robust to lighting variations and does not capture images of human appearance. However, radar observations often provide insufficient information about certain body parts or contain overlapping reflections from multiple body parts, limiting accurate 3D joint localization. To address these limitations, we propose the Initialization-to-Refinement Pose Framework (I2R-Pose), comprising two stages: Motion-Aware Pose Initialization and Observation-Guided Refinement. In the first stage, joint-wise features are learned by incorporating temporal relationships across consecutive radar observations and human skeletal structure to compensate for insufficient joint information and estimate an initial 3D pose sequence. In the second stage, separately extracted radar observation features predict joint-wise positional corrections that refine the initial pose and generate the final pose sequence. The framework supports individual and fused inputs of time-Doppler/time-range (TD/TR) maps and radar point clouds (PCs). To evaluate performance across indoor activities, we construct the Radar Motion and Spatial Information for 3D Human Pose Estimation (RaMoS-Pose) dataset, providing synchronized TD/TR maps, radar PCs, and 3D joint ground truth (GT). Evaluations on RaMoS-Pose and the publicly available MVDoppler-Pose dataset show that I2R-Pose reduces mean per-joint position error (MPJPE) by 39.50% on MVDoppler-Pose and by 15.13% on RaMoS-Pose with fused inputs, compared with the best-performing existing methods evaluated on each dataset.

</details>


### [64] [Geometry Regularization for Vanilla NeRF via Self-Supervised Cross-Ray Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27776)
*Zongji Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于自监督跨射线一致性的几何优化分支，增强NeRF隐式几何精度，无需深度或法线监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始NeRF的密度场所编码几何存在噪声且不准确，因为形状与辐射的不同组合可解释相同输入图像，存在歧义。

Method: 在原始NeRF上增加几何优化分支：从密度场采样候选表面点，从不同方向生成交于该点的虚拟射线并用NeRF渲染其外观；基于可见性加权的跨射线颜色一致性约束隐式几何；同时惩罚外观预测不一致候选点的不透明度，并鼓励局部法线平滑。

Result: 在八个Replica场景上，相比原始NeRF平均Chamfer距离降低30.8%。

Conclusion: 所提几何约束均源自NeRF预测，无需真实深度或表面法线监督，可有效提升隐式几何质量。

Abstract: Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) achieve high-quality novel-view synthesis by representing a scene as a continuous volumetric radiance field. However, the geometry encoded by the density field of vanilla NeRF can be noisy and inaccurate, since different combinations of shape and radiance may explain the same input images. To reduce this ambiguity, we augment vanilla NeRF with a geometry-optimization branch based on self-supervised cross-ray consistency. Specifically, this branch samples candidate surface points from the density field, generates virtual rays that intersect at each point from different directions, and renders their appearance using the NeRF network. Based on the visibility-weighted color consistency across these rays, we constrain the implicit geometry. We further regularize the implicit geometry by penalizing opacity at candidates with inconsistent appearance predictions and encouraging local normal smoothness. These geometric constraints are derived from the NeRF predictions and require no ground-truth depth or surface-normal supervision. Experiments on eight Replica scenes show that the proposed method reduces the mean Chamfer distance by 30.8% compared with vanilla NeRF.

</details>


### [65] [RoboJudge: Multimodal Language Models as Judges for Embodied Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27782)
*Siyuan Ma,Yu Qi,Cheng Ye,Ruotian Peng,Xinyi Xu,Zitiantao Lin,Zedong Cai,Yijian Huang,Weiyang Liu,Siyuan Wang,Yandong Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboJudge-Bench 用于系统评估多模态大模型（MLLM）作为具身视频生成"裁判"的能力，并基于 RoboJudge-Train 数据（12K 视频、25K 人工审核分数）微调出 RoboJudge-9B，其在基准上取得最高一致性（Pearson 0.719）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型有望成为具身世界模型的基础，但前提是生成结果物理合理且忠实于任务指令。MLLM 日益被用作此类性质的裁判，然而其与人类判断在具身视频上的一致性尚未被系统评估，这一空白需要填补。

Method: 构建 RoboJudge-Bench：围绕 Physical Adherence 与 Instruction Alignment 两个维度，覆盖 8 个数据来源、10 个视频生成器的 800 段人工标注视频，涉及夹爪、人手与灵巧手。基于实验结果暴露的不足，构建 RoboJudge-Train（12K 视频、25K 人工核验分数及模型辅助理由），对 9B MLLM 先监督微调再强化学习，得到 RoboJudge-9B。

Result: 现有裁判与人类评分一致性不均：通用 MLLM 在物理遵循上一致性偏低，面向通用域物理推理的专用视频裁判无法稳定迁移到具身场景。RoboJudge-9B 在 RoboJudge-Bench 上总体一致性最高，Pearson 相关 0.719，较最佳专有基线 Gemini-3.7-Flash 显著提升 0.089；训练还带来 VideoPhy2、Physion-Eval 等外部基准的增益。

Conclusion: 系统评测揭示了现有 MLLM 裁判在具身视频上的局限，而针对性的数据构建与 SFT+RL 训练可显著提升裁判可靠性；RoboJudge-9B 可作为下游评测器，按物理遵循与指令对齐两个维度对视频生成模型进行基准评测。

Abstract: Video generation models are promising foundations for embodied world models, but only if their generated rollouts are physically valid and faithful to task instructions. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to judge these properties, yet their agreement with human judgments on embodied videos has not been systematically evaluated. To address this gap, we introduce RoboJudge-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs as judges of embodied video generation. The benchmark measures two fundamental dimensions, Physical Adherence and Instruction Alignment, across 800 human-annotated videos from 8 source corpora and 10 video generators, spanning grippers, human hands, and dexterous hands. Experiments reveal uneven alignment between existing judges and human ratings: general-purpose MLLMs show lower agreement on physical adherence, while specialized video judges designed for general-domain physical reasoning do not consistently transfer to embodied scenarios. Motivated by these findings, we construct RoboJudge-Train, comprising 12K videos annotated with 25K human-verified scores and model-assisted rationales for Physical Adherence and Instruction Alignment. We fine-tune a 9B MLLM on this dataset with supervised learning followed by reinforcement learning, yielding RoboJudge-9B. Among the model judges evaluated on RoboJudge-Bench, RoboJudge-9B achieves the highest overall agreement, reaching a Pearson correlation of 0.719 with a statistically significant 0.089 gain over Gemini-3.7-Flash, the best-performing proprietary baseline in our evaluation. Training on RoboJudge-Train also yields gains on external benchmarks, including VideoPhy2 and Physion-Eval. We further use RoboJudge-9B as a downstream evaluator to benchmark video generation models according to Physical Adherence and Instruction Alignment.

</details>


### [66] [Decoupled Representation Learning for Face Presentation Attack Detection under Smart Glass Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27800)
*Raghavendra Ramachandra,Narayan Vetrekar,Neha Naik,Rajendra Gad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文构建了基于Ray-Ban Meta智能眼镜的人脸呈现攻击数据库SmaGla-FPAD，并提出解耦表示学习框架DeCoupPAD-FAS++，在域内与跨场景评测中取得攻防平衡的最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人脸呈现攻击检测多在固定相机或手持采集条件下研究，而智能眼镜采集带来自运动、视角变化、背景杂乱、距离变化与光照不可控等更困难场景，缺乏相应数据与方法。

Method: 构建SmaGla-FPAD数据库（室内、室外、车内，共297,000张样本，含真实人脸与硅胶面具攻击）；提出DeCoupPAD-FAS++，通过攻击分支与风格分支互补、伪域监督、对抗抑制及提示引导的语义对齐，将伪造相关证据与采集扰动敏感变化解耦。

Result: 在SmaGla-FPAD上，所提方法在域内与跨场景评测中于攻击拒绝与真实人脸接受之间取得最佳平衡；消融实验证实解耦、伪域监督与提示正则化的贡献。

Conclusion: 智能眼镜采集构成独立且更具挑战的FPAD设定，解耦式表示学习可有效提升该场景下的泛化与判别性能。

Abstract: Face presentation attack detection is typically studied under fixed camera or handheld acquisition, whereas smart glass capture introduces a more challenging setting with ego motion, changing viewpoint, background clutter, distance variation, and less controlled illumination. To address this challenge, we present \emph{SmaGla-FPAD}, a smart glass based face presentation attack database acquired using Ray-Ban Meta Wayfarer Smart Glasses across indoor, outdoor, and in-car settings. The database contains 297,000 sample images with both bona fide and silicone mask based attacks. We further propose \emph{DeCoupPAD-FAS++}, a decoupled representation learning framework that separates spoof related evidence from nuisance sensitive capture variation through complementary attack and style branches, pseudo-domain supervision, adversarial suppression, and prompt guided semantic alignment. Experiments on SmaGla-FPAD show that the proposed method achieves the best balance between attack rejection and bona fide acceptance in both in domain and cross scenario evaluation. Ablation results further confirm the contribution of disentanglement, pseudo-domain supervision, and prompt based regularisation under smart glass capture.

</details>


### [67] [GARRET: Generative Feedback for Region Retrieval in Visual Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27829)
*Weijian Jian,Siyang Chen,Yuguang Yang,Yajun Zhang,Chunyu Xie,Dawei Leng,Yuhui Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GARRET 是一种利用 (page, question, answer) 三元组和可微生成式反馈训练的文档区域级多模态检索框架，无需查询-区域标注，并提出 ViDoRe-V3-Region 基准，在区域检索上超越最强视觉基线 2.94 个点，同时在页级检索上以更少向量保持可比性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉丰富文档上的多模态检索增强生成需要比整页更细粒度的证据，但现有视觉检索器通常以整页为索引和返回单位；而训练区域级检索器往往依赖昂贵的查询-区域标注或噪声较大的模型生成标签。

Method: GARRET 仅使用 (page, question, answer) 三元组，通过可微的生成式反馈训练区域检索。共享的保上下文检索器输出页面与区域的多向量表示；训练时用二者检索分数定义候选区域裁剪上的软路由分布，辅助视觉语言模型分别关注各裁剪、按路由权重聚合并以自回归方式预测问题与答案；梯度经路由分布回传，为区域表示分配查询条件化的信用，无需查询-区域标注。推理时移除辅助模型；并复用上下文化区域表示与页摘要 token 构建紧凑页表示。

Result: 提出 ViDoRe-V3-Region 基准，涵盖八个领域、200,682 个固定候选区域。区域检索宏平均 nDCG@10 达 0.4126，比最强视觉基线高 2.94 个点；页级检索与 ColQwen2.5-v0.2 相当，且每页索引向量数减少 47.8%。

Conclusion: 弱监督的生成式反馈可有效替代区域级标注，实现细粒度区域检索；区域与页级表示共享还能提升索引效率，兼顾细粒度召回与存储成本。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval-augmented generation over visually rich documents requires evidence at a finer granularity than entire pages. Existing visual retrievers typically index or return full pages, whereas training region-level retrievers often requires costly query-to-region annotations or noisy model-generated labels. We introduce GARRET, a region-retrieval framework trained from (page, question, answer) triplets through differentiable generative feedback. A shared context-preserving retriever produces page and region multi-vector representations. During training, their retrieval scores define a soft routing distribution over candidate region crops. An auxiliary vision-language model attends to each crop separately, aggregates crop-specific contexts using the routing weights, and predicts the question followed by the answer under a next-token prediction objective. Gradients through the routing distribution assign query-conditioned credit to the indexed region representations, without requiring query-to-region annotations. At inference, the auxiliary model is removed. GARRET also reuses the contextualized region representations, together with page summary tokens, to construct a compact page representation. We introduce ViDoRe-V3-Region, a corpus-wide benchmark containing 200,682 fixed candidate regions across eight domains. GARRET achieves a macro nDCG@10 of 0.4126, outperforming the strongest visual baseline by 2.94 points. On page retrieval, it remains comparable to ColQwen2.5-v0.2 while indexing 47.8% fewer vectors per page.

</details>


### [68] [LaLa-World: Scaling Latent World Models with Natural Language Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27851)
*Junha Song,Geonmo Gu,Jeongeun Park,Heejeong Nam,Lucas Maes,Byeongho Heo,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LaLa-World，一种语言条件化的潜在世界模型（LWM），通过把数值动作与视觉变化都表述为语言，在仿真、真实机器人轨迹及图像视频数据上联合训练，实现跨域规划与策略微调，训练成本降低 6 倍、规划快 13.9 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 式潜在世界模型通常只能在单一领域或任务上分别训练，难以跨异构数据源学习，而视频生成模型已具备良好的语言条件接口，因此论文希望将二者结合，使 LWM 具备跨域学习与高效规划能力。
