Method: 提出 LaLa-World：第一，将数值动作和视觉变化都用语言描述，从而在异构数据集上联合训练单一未来预测器；第二，把预测器构建在语言模型之上，使其既能理解语言条件又能预测未来潜在状态。预训练后的 LaLa-World 作为 LaLa-Policy 的初始化，联合预测动作与未来视觉表征。

Result: 平均 MPC 规划性能优于按领域/任务分别训练的现有 LWM；加入图像与视频数据后平均规划性能及对动作表述变化的鲁棒性进一步提升；与基于 RAEv2 的视频生成基线相比，规划性能相当，训练成本低 6 倍、规划速度快 13.9 倍；LaLa-Policy 在 LIBERO 和 RoboCasa 上与 Cosmos Policy 表现相当，推理速度最高快 5.5 倍。

Conclusion: 语言条件化的潜在世界模型是跨域学习、想象式规划与下游策略微调的高效有效基座，兼顾性能与计算效率。

Abstract: This paper shows that JEPA-style latent world models (LWMs) can learn across diverse domains and data sources by adopting the language-conditioned interface of video generation models. We establish this approach as an effective and efficient basis for planning with imagination and downstream policy fine-tuning. To this end, we introduce LaLa-World, a Language-conditioned Latent World model that learns from simulation and robot trajectories together with broader image and video data. Two designs make this work. First, we describe both numerical actions and visual changes in language, enabling joint training of a single future predictor across heterogeneous datasets. Second, we build the predictor on a language model, allowing it to interpret language conditions while predicting future latent states. The resulting predictor achieves higher average MPC planning performance than existing LWMs trained separately for each domain or task. Adding image and video data further improves average planning performance and robustness to action phrasing. In a matched comparison with an RAEv2-based video generation model baseline, LaLa-World reaches comparable planning performance with $6\times$ less training cost and $13.9\times$ faster planning. Beyond planning, the pretrained LaLa-World serves as an initialization for LaLa-Policy, which jointly predicts actions and future visual representations. LaLa-Policy performs competitively with Cosmos Policy on LIBERO and RoboCasa, with up to $5.5\times$ faster inference.

</details>


### [69] [Manifold Prior Guided Deep Unfolding for Hyperspectral Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27874)
*Shuqi Liu,Yingkai Zhang,Lin Gu,Tao Zhang,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出以两阶段流形学习为核心的深度展开框架，将无退化的结构先验同时注入投影模块与去噪模块，实现高光谱图像重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度展开式HSI重建方法存在两方面不足：1）数据一致性投影模块采用固定梯度下降步长，忽略HSI的光谱内容自适应性；2）去噪模块或计算复杂度高，或受限于局部感受野，难以高效捕获全局空间-光谱依赖关系。

Method: 提出两阶段流形学习策略获取无退化的结构先验，并将其策略性地注入投影与去噪模块。在数据一致性投影中引入先验引导的光谱权重机制，自适应调制各光谱波段的权重；在去噪模块中提出流形引导的Mamba，通过先验调制实现结构感知的空间-光谱特征提取。

Result: 在合成数据集与真实数据集上的定量与定性对比表明，所提方法显著优于现有最先进方法，同时保持了展开框架的结构可解释性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过流形先验引导的自适应光谱加权与结构感知Mamba去噪，可在保持展开框架可解释性的前提下显著提升HSI重建性能。

Abstract: Hyperspectral Image (HSI) reconstruction has made substantial progress with the deep unfolding framework by decomposing the problem into a projection module and a denoiser module. Nevertheless, existing methods still exhibit limitations of insufficient matching with HSI data. The issues lie in two aspects: 1) the data-consistency projection module applies a fixed gradient descent step while ignoring the spectral content adaptivity of HSI; 2) the denoiser module either suffers from high computational complexity or is limited to the local receptive field, failing to efficiently capture the global spatial-spectral dependencies. In this paper, we propose a deep unfolding framework centered on a two-stage manifold learning strategy to obtain a degradation-free structural prior, which is then strategically injected into both the projection and denoiser modules. In the data-consistency projection, a prior-guided spectral weight mechanism is introduced to adaptively modulate the weight of spectral band. In the denoiser module, a manifold-guided Mamba is proposed to achieve the structure-aware extraction of spatial-spectral features through prior modulation. Quantitative and visual comparisons on synthetic and real-world datasets show that the proposed method significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches, while maintaining the structural interpretability of the unfolding framework. The code is available at https://github.com/SqLiu67/MPG-DU.

</details>


### [70] [Gaze2HOI: Gaze-Aware Intention Disambiguation for Egocentric Hand-Object Interaction Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27877)
*Chuanyang Ma,Chenye Wang,Yixuan Wang,Huiling Zhou,Xingqun Qi,Muyi Sun,Man Zhang,Caifeng Shan,Zhenan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Gaze2HOI，一个利用注视线索的分层式手-物交互预测框架（意图推断→接触落地→耦合手物运动生成），并构建了含细粒度意图标注的EgoAIM基准，在意图推断与运动预测上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接从历史观测预测手-物交互，将意图与接触几何隐含处理；但相似的观测历史可能对应语义或几何上截然不同的未来，存在语义歧义与几何歧义。行为学证据表明注视能为潜在意图提供早期线索，故引入注视信息以消解歧义。

Method: 将预测建模为分层问题：1) 时序注视显著性调制（TGSM）按注视强度与时间新近性加权注视线索，调制VLM视觉表示以降低意图推断的语义歧义；2) 预期接触落地（ACG）将结构化意图映射为显式的接触、支撑与放置场，缓解几何歧义；3) 以这些显式条件驱动扩散模型联合生成物体6-DoF轨迹与双手未来关节运动。同时提出EgoAIM第一人称基准，按手-物关系演化划分四阶段并提供细粒度意图标注。

Result: 大量实验表明Gaze2HOI在意图推断与运动预测上均达到SOTA，在某些交互阶段接近oracle级端到端性能。

Conclusion: 注视信息可作为有效的早期意图先验，配合显式接触几何条件化与分阶段评测，能显著提升主动式手-物交互预测的质量与可解释性。

Abstract: Anticipating how users will interact with their surroundings is essential for proactive systems that provide timely assistance in AR, VR, and embodied applications. Prior work typically forecasts hand-object interactions directly from historical observations, leaving intention and contact geometry implicit. However, similar observation histories can precede semantically or geometrically distinct futures. Inspired by behavioral evidence that gaze can provide early cues for underlying intention, we propose Gaze2HOI, a gaze-aware framework that formulates hand-object interaction forecasting as a hierarchical problem comprising intention inference, contact grounding, and coupled hand-object motion generation. Specifically, Gaze2HOI first weights gaze cues by fixation strength and recency, and uses them to modulate the visual representations of a vision-language model (VLM) through Temporal Gaze Saliency Modulation (TGSM), thereby reducing semantic ambiguity in intention inference. Subsequently, an Anticipatory Contact Grounding (ACG) module maps these structured intentions into explicit contact, support, and placement fields, effectively mitigating geometric ambiguity. Driven by these explicit conditions, a diffusion-based model jointly generates the 6-DoF object trajectories and future articulated motion of both hands. To evaluate Gaze2HOI, we further introduce EgoAIM, a phase-aware benchmark that organizes egocentric hand-object interactions into four phases defined by evolving hand-object relations and provides fine-grained intention annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Gaze2HOI achieves state-of-the-art performance in both intention inference and motion forecasting, even approaching oracle-level end-to-end performance in specific interaction phases. Project page: https://gaze2hoi.github.io/.

</details>


### [71] [S$^3$E-Det: A Strict Spatial-Spectral Equivariant Detector for Multispectral Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27882)
*Peng Zhang,Tingfa Xu,Jinrui Sui,Yuyang Tian,Jianan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 S$^3$E-Det，一个严格空间-谱等变的多光谱目标检测器，通过有序谱建模与变换一致的预测提升检测精度并降低旋转引起的性能波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多光谱目标检测（MOD）检测器虽能学到判别性谱表示，但缺乏统一机制来同时保持谱顺序、在金字塔层级间自适应融合谱-上下文，且在旋转下约束几何响应。

Method: 设计了三个模块：谱顺序感知采样校准（SOSC）通过一阶变化对输入衍生的谱特征施加局部顺序约束；共差等变融合（CDEF）在金字塔各层级自适应聚合谱-上下文；紧凑的几何对齐头（GAH）在统一预测阶段协调不变与等变输出性质。

Result: 在 MODA、HOD3K 和 DroneVehicle 数据集上，S$^3$E-Det 兼具较高检测精度、较小的参数量，并降低了旋转引起的性能变化。

Conclusion: 将有序谱建模与变换一致的预测耦合，可有效提升多光谱目标检测的精度与几何鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multispectral object detection requires detectors to exploit material-dependent spectral responses while maintaining consistent localization under geometric variations. Existing MOD detectors learn discriminative spectral representations, yet lack a unified mechanism that preserves spectral order, adapts spectral-context fusion across pyramid levels, and regulates geometric responses under rotation. To address these limitations, we propose S$^3$E-Det, a strict spatial-spectral equivariant detector that couples ordered spectral modeling with transformation-consistent prediction. Spectral Order-aware Sampling Calibration (SOSC) imposes local order constraints on input-derived spectral features through first-order variation, while Common-Differential Equivariant Fusion (CDEF) adapts spectral-context aggregation across pyramid levels. A compact Geometric Alignment Head (GAH) reconciles invariant and equivariant output properties within a unified prediction stage. Across MODA, HOD3K, and DroneVehicle, S$^3$E-Det combines high detection accuracy with a compact parameter footprint and reduced rotation-induced variation. Code is available at https://github.com/Riiluo/S3E-Det.

</details>


### [72] [SGLDet: Spectral-aware Global-Local Dynamic Modeling for Multispectral Aerial Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27883)
*Peng Zhang,Shuaihao Han,Tingfa Xu,Jianan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SGLDet，一个光谱感知的全局-局部动态建模框架，用于多光谱航空目标检测，包含三个协同模块，在MODA、HOD3K、DroneVehicle上取得优异性能且计算开销有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多光谱航空检测方法主要通过光谱-空间解耦与目标感知的光谱-空间融合提升光谱利用，但存在两个未被充分探索的关键问题：(1) 大场景覆盖导致严重的前景-背景不平衡，阻碍可靠的目标识别；(2) 航空目标在尺度、朝向和结构上存在显著局部变化，妨碍准确定位。作者观察到目标语义由全局光谱一致性与场景上下文共同决定，而精确定位需要以输入光谱与空间属性为条件的动态特征调制。

Method: SGLDet由三个协同组件构成：1) 光谱驱动的全局动态建模模块(Spectral-driven Global Dynamic Modeling)，通过相似度引导的稀疏匹配聚合全场景中光谱相关的特征；2) 多尺度全局上下文集成模块(Multi-scale Global Context Integration)，构建紧凑的场景记忆用于场景引导的上下文推理；3) 光谱感知的输入依赖校准聚合模块(Spectral-aware Input-dependent Calibration Aggregation)，采用专家核聚合并配合场景引导的光谱-空间校准，实现自适应局部特征调制。

Result: 在MODA、HOD3K与DroneVehicle三个数据集上的大量实验表明，SGLDet取得了更优的检测性能，同时计算成本具有竞争力。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过全局光谱一致性建模与输入依赖的局部动态调制相结合，SGLDet有效缓解了前景-背景不平衡与目标局部变化带来的识别和定位难题，验证了全局-局部协同动态建模在多光谱航空目标检测中的有效性。

Abstract: Multispectral aerial object detection aims to exploit spectral-spatial cues to address object detection challenges in complex aerial scenes. Existing methods mainly focus on spectral-spatial decoupling and object-aware spectral-spatial fusion to improve spectral utilization. However, two critical issues remain underexplored: 1) severe foreground-background imbalance caused by large scene coverage hinders reliable object recognition; 2) aerial objects exhibit substantial local variations in scale, orientation, and structure, which hinder accurate localization. To address these issues, we propose SGLDet, a spectral-aware global-local dynamic modeling framework for multispectral aerial object detection, which is motivated by the observation that object semantics are jointly determined by global spectral consistency and scene context, while precise localization requires dynamic feature modulation conditioned on the input spectral and spatial properties. To realize these properties, SGLDet consists of three coordinated components: a Spectral-driven Global Dynamic Modeling module that aggregates spectrally related features across the scene via similarity-guided sparse matching; a Multi-scale Global Context Integration module that constructs a compact scene memory for scene-guided contextual reasoning; and a Spectral-aware Input-dependent Calibration Aggregation module that performs expert-kernel aggregation with scene-guided spectral-spatial calibration to enable adaptive local feature modulation. Extensive experiments on MODA, HOD3K, and DroneVehicle demonstrate that SGLDet achieves superior performance with competitive computational cost. Code is available at https://github.com/Riiluo/SGLDet.

</details>


### [73] [EndoWorld-JEPA: Motion-Guided Global-Local Latent World Modeling for Endoscopic Video Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27890)
*Jie Wang,Shilong Yang,Tao Zhou,Juan Yu,Rong Qu,Jianfeng Ren,Zhicheng Zhang,Linlin Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 EndoWorld-JEPA，一种面向内窥镜视频的潜空间世界模型预训练框架，用观测到的光流运动替代动作/位姿条件，通过全局与运动局部的双预测器学习可预测的视觉—运动潜在动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内窥镜视频中存在由镜头位移、可变形组织和器械交互引起的状态转移，但现有预训练方法隐式处理这些动态，缺乏对运动因果结构的建模。受动作条件联合嵌入预测世界模型启发，且内窥镜光流同时包含共享的图像平面位移与局部残差运动，因此可将被动视频预训练重新表述为因果运动引导的潜状态预测。

Method: 提出 EndoWorld-JEPA：以当前状态之前观测到的运动作为条件预测下一潜状态，无需动作记录、标定位姿或机器人运动学。核心为 Optical Motion-guided 模块，将主导位移与残差运动分离，生成有序的时间运动条件与空间局部运动权重。全局预测器与运动局部预测器协同：时间条件调制两个潜转移，局部权重定义辅助的运动局部状态，将预测从场景级演化扩展到空间选择性的动态。采用逐块运动加权突出局部变化并保留解剖上下文，并用过去运动窗口随机丢弃（past-motion-window dropout）整体屏蔽运动条件以降低对不可靠运动的依赖。在表征空间学习可预测的视觉—运动动态，而不重建随机外观伪影。

Result: 同一预训练检查点在四个下游基准上均取得强迁移性能，验证了运动引导的全局—局部潜预测作为内窥镜视频预训练范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 以运动为条件的全局—局部潜空间预测能够有效捕捉内窥镜视频中可预测的视觉—运动动态，是一种通用且可迁移的内窥镜视频表示学习预训练范式。

Abstract: Endoscopic video contains state transitions induced by camera displacement, deformable tissue, and instrument interaction, yet existing pre-training methods treat dynamics implicitly. Inspired by action-conditioned joint-embedding predictive world models and by the observation that endoscopic optical flow contains both shared image-plane displacement and localized residual motion, we reformulate passive endoscopic video pre-training as causal motion-guided latent-state prediction. We present EndoWorld-JEPA, to our knowledge the first latent world-model pre-training framework designed for general-purpose endoscopic video representation learning. In place of recorded actions, calibrated poses, or robot kinematics, the model conditions prediction of the next latent state on motion observed up to the current state. Its Optical Motion-guided module separates dominant displacement from residual motion and converts the resulting evidence into an ordered temporal motion condition and spatial local-motion weights. Complementary global and motion-local predictors use these outputs: the temporal condition modulates both latent transitions, whereas the local weights define an auxiliary motion-local state that extends prediction from scene-level evolution to spatially selective dynamics. Patch-wise motion weighting emphasizes localized change while retaining anatomical context, and past-motion-window dropout randomly withholds the complete motion condition to reduce dependence on unreliable motion. This global--local formulation learns predictable visual--motion dynamics in representation space without reconstructing stochastic appearance artifacts. Evaluation shows that a common pre-trained checkpoint achieves strong transfer across four downstream benchmarks, supporting motion-guided global--local latent prediction as an effective pre-training paradigm for endoscopic video.

</details>


### [74] [ICIBS: Increasing the Context for Ice Boundary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27912)
*Marcel Dreier,Dakota Pyles,Nora Gourmelon,Moritz Koch,Fei Wu,Thorsten Seehaus,Matthias Braun,Andreas Maier,Vincent Christlein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对山地冰川与冰盖冰厚雷达数据中冰底界面难以自动圈定的问题，提出在深度学习模型中融入额外空间与地理空间上下文信息的多种扩展方法，在 IceAnatomy 基准上取得新最优结果，并展现出对未见数据的良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探地雷达生成的无线电回波探测数据可用于测量冰厚、评估冰量与预测冰体演变，但人工标注界面耗时巨大（调查区域可达数百公里）。已有的自动方法在圈定冰底方面表现不佳，尤其在温性冰川中：高含水量导致雷达信号散射与衰减加剧，冰底反射微弱、模糊，数据噪声大，对应冰底的视觉特征不明确。

Method: 在深度学习模型中引入额外的上下文信息以辅助预测，具体包括两类改进：一是融入额外的空间上下文信息，二是融入地理空间（geo-spatial）上下文信息，从而在微弱、歧义的回波中提供判别依据。

Result: 在 IceAnatomy 基准数据集上取得新的最优性能：单一集成系统中 FAU 子集平均误差为 10.9 m，CReSIS 子集平均误差为 43.4 m。此外，方法对先前未见数据泛化良好，在来自 Patagonia 的新观测数据上比基线方法优 49.6%。

Conclusion: 将空间与地理空间上下文信息纳入深度网络，可有效缓解温性冰川雷达数据噪声大、冰底反射模糊的问题，显著提升冰底界面自动圈定的精度与跨区域泛化能力，有助于加速冰厚数据的规模化标注。模型代码与新数据集均已公开。

Abstract: Mountain glaciers and ice sheets are undergoing rapid loss of ice mass. To assess their current volume and ice-thickness distribution, and to accurately forecast their future, ground-penetrating radar systems are employed to measure ice thickness. These instruments produce radio-echo sounding data that reveal the internal structure of the ice and its boundaries at the surface and beneath the ice body. However, delineating these interfaces is time-consuming as the surveyed area can span several hundred kilometers. To accelerate the labelling process, automated methods have emerged, but they often struggle to delineate the ice bottom, particularly in temperate glaciers. The high water content inherent in temperate ice increases both scattering and attenuation of the radar signal, resulting in weak and ambiguous reflections at the ice bottom and generally noisier data. As a result, the visual features corresponding to the ice bottom become ambiguous. To address this issue, we propose several extensions to deep learning models that incorporate additional contextual information into the prediction. Specifically, we integrate modifications that incorporate additional spatial and geo-spatial contextual information into the network. With these enhancements, we achieve a new state-of-the-art performance on the IceAnatomy benchmark dataset, with mean errors of 10.9 m for the FAU subset and 43.4 m for the CReSIS subset in a single combined system. We also demonstrate that our method generalises well to previously unseen data, outperforming the baseline by 49.6 % on new campaign data from Patagonia. The model code and the new dataset are available at https://github.com/ki7077/ICIBS/tree/main and https://zenodo.org/records/23392580.

</details>


### [75] [World Models with Configurable Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27914)
*Yijun Shen,Delong Chen,Xianming Hu,Kai Zhang,Pascale Fung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ConfigWM，用语言任务描述作为配置信号，将视觉嵌入映射到任务特定状态空间，实现可配置感知，从而在含干扰的噪声环境中保持稳健的世界建模与规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实观测含噪声并充满任务无关信息，而现有世界模型的编码器通常是非自适应的，导致状态表示易受无关信息干扰，无法按不同任务需求选择性过滤观测。

Method: 给定当前任务的语言描述，ConfigWM将其作为配置信号，引导视觉嵌入映射到任务特定的状态空间，选择性地保留与任务相关的信息、抑制无关变化；随后在该任务特定状态空间中学习动作条件动态用于规划。

Result: 在干扰程度递增的环境中评估：现有方法规划成功率显著下降，而ConfigWM相对稳定，在多种环境上平均比最强竞争方法高22.3%；物理量探针提升+0.365 Pearson r，干扰到干净检索提升+75.5%。

Conclusion: ConfigWM学到的状态表示能保留任务相关信息并抵御干扰，支持噪声世界中的可靠世界建模与规划。

Abstract: Real-world observations are noisy and often contain excessive task-irrelevant information. Effective world modeling and planning need to abstract observations into world state representations while filtering out irrelevant information. However, encoders in existing world models are typically non-adaptive, leading to state representations that are easily distracted by task-irrelevant information. As a result, the model may not selectively filter observations to cater to different task demands. To address this problem, we introduce ConfigWM, which realizes configurable perception through task-conditioned state extraction. Given a language description of the task at hand, ConfigWM uses it as a configuration signal to steer how visual embeddings are mapped into a task-specific state space, selectively preserving information relevant to the current task while suppressing task-irrelevant variations. Action-conditioned dynamics are then learned in this task-specific state space for planning. We evaluate ConfigWM in environments with increasing levels of distraction. While existing methods exhibit substantial declines in planning success rate as the environments become increasingly noisy, ConfigWM remains comparatively stable and outperforms the strongest competing method by 22.3% on average across diverse environments. Consistent gains in physical-quantity probing (+0.365 Pearson r) and distracted-to-clean retrieval (+75.5%) further indicate that ConfigWM learns states that preserve task-relevant information while remaining robust to distractors, supporting reliable world modeling and planning in noisy worlds. Project Page: https://configwm.github.io

</details>


### [76] [In PRAISE of Attributes: Grounding Retrieval Geometry with Query-Conditioned Subspaces for Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27944)
*Rajarshi Bhattacharya,Aryan Shukla,Shakeeb Murtaza,Christian Desrosiers,Jose Dolz,Maguelonne Heritier,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PRAISE，一个面向VLM行人重识别的查询侧测试时自适应框架，用可迁移属性提示库构建属性条件子空间来重塑查询-图库相似度，在I2I与T2I基准上稳定提升检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像到图像ReID中视角、光照、遮挡和背景变化会让干扰样本比真实匹配更相似；文本到图像ReID中描述不完整或含糊会丢失关键视觉证据。VLM中的服装颜色、携带物等属性提示虽提供细粒度线索，但模板化提示存在偏差、相关性和校准不良问题，直接把属性提示相似度当作检索分数不可靠。

Method: 提出PRAISE框架：不再将提示相似度当作分数，而是用可迁移的属性提示库选出最兼容的属性提示，构造查询相关的低秩属性条件子空间（ACS），借此形成受控的属性条件几何，用于重塑查询-图库亲和度，并在无身份标签、无属性标签的情况下指导轻量级推理时更新。

Result: 在多个I2I和T2I ReID基准上，PRAISE一致提升基于VLM的检索骨干网络性能，并优于通用TTA与ReID专用TTA基线。

Conclusion: 将属性提示从“打分工具”转为“构造查询条件子空间的几何先验”，是提升VLM-ReID测试时适应性的有效途径。

Abstract: Person re-identification (ReID) ranks an image gallery using either an image query or a textual query. In image-to-image (I2I) ReID, viewpoint, illumination, occlusion, and background changes can make distractors appear more similar than the true match. In text-to-image (T2I) ReID, incomplete or ambiguous descriptions may omit key visual evidence. We study this problem for vision-language model (VLM) based ReID, where attributes such as clothing color and carried objects provide useful fine-grained matching cues. However, directly using attribute-prompt similarities as retrieval scores is unreliable because templated prompts can be biased, correlated, and poorly calibrated. We therefore propose \textbf{PR}ompt-guided \textbf{A}ttribute-based \textbf{I}nference-time \textbf{S}imilarity \textbf{E}nhancement (\texttt{PRAISE}), a query-side test-time adaptation framework for VLM-based ReID. Instead of treating prompts as scores, \texttt{PRAISE} uses a transferable attribute-prompt bank to construct an \emph{Attribute-Conditioned Subspace} (ACS): a query-dependent low-rank subspace induced by the most compatible attribute prompts. ACS provides a controlled attribute-conditioned geometry for reshaping query--gallery affinities and guiding lightweight inference-time updates without identity or attribute labels. Experiments across multiple I2I- and T2I-ReID benchmarks show that \texttt{PRAISE} consistently improves VLM retrieval backbones and outperforms both generic and ReID-specific TTA baselines.

</details>


### [77] [Decoupling Action from Egocentric Observation for World Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27946)
*Yue Ma,Pengjie Song,Xinyu Wang,Yi He,Zeqian Long,Fangneng Zhan,Kaichen Zhou,Hongyu Liu,Hongfa Wang,Peihao Li,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EgoACT，一个无需额外几何估计器的测试时第一人称动作迁移框架，通过在视频扩散去噪过程中进行速度引导的结构锚定与稀疏对应校准，实现语义保真且物理合理的交互视频生成，并建立EgoActionBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一人称动作迁移方法分为两类：基于条件引导的视频生成依赖显式几何控制（手部姿态或网格），会引入几何估计误差，导致新场景中交互物理合理性差；而运动迁移方法虽直接提取运动模式，但运动级特征无法编码丰富的接触动力学，常导致手部结构崩塌与接触布局不合理。因此需要一种规避几何估计、同时保留接触动力学的方法。

Method: EgoACT为测试时框架，直接作用于视频扩散模型的去噪过程，无需额外几何估计器。其包含两个组件：(1) 速度引导的结构锚定（Velocity-guided Structure Anchoring），在去噪早期阶段稳定与参考一致的手-物结构；(2) 稀疏对应校准（Sparse Correspondence Calibration），在去噪中后期细化可靠的局部对应关系。

Result: 实验表明，EgoACT在动作保持、视觉质量与手-物交互合理性上优于强基线，生成的交互视频在时序一致性与物理合理性方面表现更佳；同时论文建立了EgoActionBench，用于在多样化第一人称操作场景下评估动作保持、视觉质量与手-物合理性。

Conclusion: 通过在扩散去噪过程中联合施加结构锚定与对应校准，可在不依赖显式几何估计的前提下同时保留可迁移的动作语义并提升时序连贯性与交互真实感，为可扩展的世界模拟、具身策略学习与机器人数据生成提供支持。

Abstract: Egocentric action transfer aims to decouple the semantic action from egocentric observations and reproduce them in new visual contexts, enabling scalable world simulation, embodied policy learning, and robotic data generation. Existing approaches to transferring actions rely on condition-guided video generation, which converts the observation video into explicit geometric controls (e.g., hand poses or meshes) to drive synthesis. However, these methods introduce geometric estimation errors, making it difficult to preserve physically plausible interactions when transferred to new scenes. Alternatively, motion transfer methods directly extract motion patterns from the observation video, yet motion-level features alone cannot encode rich contact dynamics, frequently leading to severe hand structural collapse and implausible contact layout. To address both limitations, we present EgoACT, a test-time framework for egocentric action transfer that operates directly on the denoising process of video diffusion models without requiring additional geometric estimators. EgoACT uses Velocity-guided Structure Anchoring to stabilize reference-consistent hand-object structure in the early denoising stage, and Sparse Correspondence Calibration to refine reliable local correspondences in the mid-to-late denoising stages. Together, these two components preserve transferable action semantics while improving temporal coherence and interaction realism. We further establish EgoActionBench, a benchmark for evaluating action preservation, visual quality, and hand-object plausibility across diverse egocentric manipulation scenarios. Experiments show that EgoACT generates more coherent and physically plausible interaction videos than strong baselines.

</details>


### [78] [Edit in Sync: Unified End-to-End Audio-Visual Editing for Human-Centric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27947)
*Ziye Yuan,Minghong Cai,Xu He,Ruchang Yao,Xiao Fu,Kaixin Ding,Ming Zhang,Xiangyu Du,Tianpeng Lan,Da Xie,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AVE，首个统一的端到端音视频联合编辑框架，无需空间掩码即可在同一模型中完成参考引导的人物替换、插入与删除，并构建了含 10 万配对样本的音视频编辑数据集 AVE-Dataset。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以人为中心的音视频编辑需要口型、手势与表情和语音精确对齐。现有方法将视频与音频分成独立流水线处理，缺乏跨模态交互；且多依赖基于掩码的修复，导致口型—语音同步差、流水线误差累积以及掩码尺寸的两难问题。

Method: 提出统一端到端框架 AVE，同时建模音频与视频模态，在无空间掩码条件下完成参考引导的人物替换、插入和删除；设计数据合成流水线并构建 AVE-Dataset（首个含外观与音色参考的 10 万配对音视频编辑数据集）；采用合成到真实的重建策略结合音频预热训练，以突破合成数据局限并缓解音视频 token 不平衡。

Result: 在编辑真实感、时序一致性以及音视频对齐三项指标上，AVE 均优于模态分离式的音视频编辑流水线。

Conclusion: 统一的端到端音视频联合建模可有效替代掩码式分离流水线，在无掩码条件下实现高质量、对齐良好的以人为中心音视频编辑。

Abstract: Human-centric audio-visual editing requires precise alignment of lip movements, gestures, and expressions with speech. Existing methods process inputs through independent video and audio pipelines, preventing cross-modal interaction between visual editing and audio dubbing. Many of these pipelines further rely on mask-based inpainting. As a result, they suffer from poor lip-audio synchronization, cumulative pipeline errors, and an inherent mask-size dilemma. We present AVE, the first unified end-to-end framework that jointly performs reference-guided person swapping, insertion, and deletion across both audio and video modalities without requiring spatial masks. To overcome data scarcity, we design a synthesis pipeline and introduce AVE-Dataset, the first audio-visual editing dataset to provide 100K pairs with appearance and voice references. By combining Synthetic-to-Real reconstruction with preliminary audio warm-up, our framework transcends synthetic pipeline limits and mitigates audio-video token imbalance. AVE outperforms modality-separate audio-visual editing pipelines in editing realism, temporal consistency, and audio-visual alignment.

</details>


### [79] [From Proper Scoring Rules and Edge Biases to Mumford-Shah Models: Robust Unsupervised Hyperspectral Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27958)
*Fabian Conrad,Benjamin Berkels*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个特征无关的无监督高光谱图像分割框架，基于分段常数Mumford-Shah泛函，将严格正常评分规则与参数概率分布族转化为指示函数，重点采用重尾椭圆分布（负对数似然与beta散度评分）以应对异常值，并通过变分法与统计学的结合提出参数估计的鲁棒步骤，同时引入包含先验边界信息的边缘偏置项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱图像逐像素分类意义重大，但标注数据稀缺且获取耗时，因此无监督分割方法十分重要。同时，光谱变异性、混合像素、噪声以及相邻波段间的高相关性使该任务充满挑战。

Method: 构建基于分段常数Mumford-Shah泛函的无监督分割框架，将严格正常评分规则与参数概率分布族转化为Mumford-Shah泛函中的指示函数，自然推广了若干已有的Mumford-Shah分割模型；聚焦重尾椭圆分布，使用负对数似然评分和基于beta散度的评分处理高光谱中的重尾异常值；利用变分法与统计学的相互作用提出估计重尾椭圆分布统计参数的鲁棒步骤；并将包含先验边界信息的边缘偏置同时融入数据保真项与周长项。

Result: 数值实验表明，在Indian Pines、Kennedy Space Center、Pavia University和Salinas四个标准基准的全场景评估中，该方法在其中三个上取得了完全无监督方法中最高的总体精度，并优于作者此前基于Mumford-Shah的模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的特征无关、基于Mumford-Shah泛函的无监督高光谱分割框架，通过重尾椭圆分布建模、鲁棒参数估计与边缘偏置先验，在多个标准数据集上达到无监督方法中的领先性能，验证了将统计评分规则与变分分割框架结合的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Hyperspectral images provide a rich representation of the underlying spectrum for each pixel, allowing for a pixel-wise classification/segmentation into different classes. As the availability of labeled hyperspectral images is limited and the acquisition of labeled training data is time-consuming, unsupervised segmentation methods are important in hyperspectral image segmentation. Spectral variability, mixed pixels, noise as well as high correlation between neighboring spectral bands make this task challenging. We present a feature-agnostic, unsupervised hyperspectral segmentation framework based on the piecewise-constant Mumford-Shah functional. Our framework turns a strictly proper scoring rule and a family of parametric probability distributions into an indicator function for the Mumford-Shah functional, and we naturally generalize some previous Mumford-Shah based models for image segmentation this way. Our focus will be on heavy-tailed elliptical distributions, the negative log-likelihood score and the score based on the beta-divergence to deal with the heavy outliers in hyperspectral imaging. Using the interplay between the calculus of variations and statistics, we also propose a robustness step to estimate the statistical parameters of these heavy-tailed elliptical distributions. In addition, an edge bias, which contains prior boundary information, is incorporated into both the data fidelity term and the perimeter term of the Mumford-Shah functional. Numerical experiments show that, to the best of our knowledge, our method achieves the highest overall accuracy among fully unsupervised methods on three of the four standard benchmarks (Indian Pines, Kennedy Space Center, Pavia University and Salinas) evaluated on the full scenes. It also improves upon our previous Mumford-Shah based model.

</details>


### [80] [Efficient SAM3 Adaptation for Multi-Class Semantic Segmentation via Dense Competitive Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27960)
*Wenbin Liao,Hao Zhu,Yike Ma,Hao Jiang,Feng Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 DCR-SAM 框架，解决 SAM3 在多概念同时分割时语义重叠导致的误分类、冗余掩码与推理延迟问题，性能提升 2.3%~10.7%，推理较原生 SAM3 加速 33 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM3 在 Promptable Concept Segmentation 中表现良好，但仅擅长单概念分割。真实场景常需同时分割多个概念：语义重叠概念会引发语义误分类与冗余掩码预测；且概念数量增加时，独立推理范式带来难以接受的推理延迟。

Method: 提出 DCR-SAM。其一，解耦跨模态交互以缓解推理延迟；其二，动态对齐文本概念与视觉表示，并引入密集竞争头（dense competition head）显式施加类间竞争以矫正语义误分类；其三，使用可学习背景 token 动态吸收非目标物体，从而抑制重叠预测、生成高保真掩码。

Result: 在多个基准上：相较 SOTA 方法性能提升 2.3%~10.7%；相比原生 SAM3 实现 33 倍推理加速；在多样场景下表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦跨模态交互、文本-视觉动态对齐、密集竞争头与可学习背景 token 的协同设计，DCR-SAM 同时改善了多概念分割的精度与效率，可作为 SAM3 在多概念密集场景下的有效扩展方案。

Abstract: The Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM3) has achieved significant progress in Promptable Concept Segmentation (PCS) by processing short noun phrases to generate segmentation masks with unique instance identifiers. While SAM3 excels at single-concept segmentation, it falls short in complex, real-world environments where multiple concepts must be segmented simultaneously. In particular, SAM3 typically fails to simultaneously process concepts with semantic overlap, leading to semantic misclassifications and redundant mask predictions. Moreover, as the number of concepts increases, the independent inference paradigm introduces prohibitive inference latency. To address these limitations, we propose the Dense Competitive Representations SAM (DCR-SAM) framework. By decoupling cross-modal interactions, the proposed method alleviates the inference latency. To resolve semantic misclassifications, DCR-SAM dynamically aligns textual concepts with visual representations and incorporates a dense competition head to enforce explicit inter-class competition. Furthermore, a learnable background token is applied to absorb non-target objects dynamically. These mechanisms effectively suppress overlapping predictions and generate high-fidelity masks. Extensive experimental evaluations across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed framework: (1) achieves a 2.3% $\sim$ 10.7% performance improvement over state-of-the-art methods, (2) delivers a 33$\times$ inference speedup compared to the native SAM3, and (3) exhibits strong robustness across diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [81] [Media Intelligence Platform: Multimodal Detection of Synthetic and Manipulated Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27974)
*David Fischinger,Marcel Hasenbalg,Silvia Poletti,Davide Antonutti,Mina Schütz,Lam Pham,Justin Ilyes,Péter Rjabcsenkó,Simon Freitter,Martin Boyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出媒体智能平台（MIP），一个基于Web的统一多模态媒体取证框架，集成了假视频检测、人脸形变检测、AI生成图像检测、音频深度伪造检测、局部图像篡改检测和文本分析等专用模块，在多个基准数据集上验证了跨模态的可靠检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的快速发展使合成人脸、伪造视频、AI生成图像、虚假音频和局部编辑图像等被操纵内容大幅增加。然而现有检测方法通常只针对单一篡改类型设计，难以应对真实场景中异构媒体来源并存的情况，因此需要一个统一的多模态取证系统。研究动机是填补"单一类型检测器"与"现实多模态混合威胁"之间的鸿沟。

Method: 作者设计并实现了媒体智能平台（MIP）：一个基于Web的统一框架，将六类专用检测模块整合到同一系统中，包括（1）假视频检测、（2）人脸形变（morphing）检测、（3）AI生成图像检测、（4）音频深度伪造检测、（5）局部图像篡改检测、（6）文本分析。其核心方法是模块化架构：各模块相对独立，便于灵活接入异构模型，并支持未来针对新兴篡改技术的扩展；同时提供面向取证调查的用户友好界面，并公开发布演示系统。

Result: 在多个代表性基准数据集上的实验表明，该平台在多种媒体模态上均取得可靠的检测性能，验证了统一多模态框架的可行性；同时平台提供了实用且易用的取证交互界面，并随论文发布公开演示版本以支持可复现性与社区评测。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，统一的多模态系统在真实环境中开展全面深度伪造检测具有较大潜力；模块化设计使系统可持续扩展以适应新出现的篡改手段；公开演示版本有助于推动可复现研究和社区基准评估。摘要未给出具体的量化指标、各模块所用模型细节及跨模态融合方式，这些可作为后续阅读全文的重点。

Abstract: The rapid progress of generative artificial intelligence has led to a significant increase in manipulated multimedia content, including synthetic faces, forged videos, AI-generated images, fake audio, and locally edited images. Existing detection approaches are typically designed for a single manipulation type, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios involving heterogeneous media sources. In this work, we present the Media Intelligence Platform (MIP), a unified web-based framework for multimodal media forensics integrating specialized modules for fake video detection, face morphing detection, AI-generated image detection, audio deepfake detection, localized image manipulation detection, and text analysis. Its modular architecture enables flexible integration of heterogeneous models and supports future extensions for emerging manipulation techniques. Experimental results on representative benchmark datasets demonstrate reliable detection performance across multiple media modalities while providing a practical and user-friendly interface for forensic investigations. The platform highlights the potential of unified multimodal systems for comprehensive deepfake detection in real-world environments, and is accompanied by a public demonstrator released with this workshop submission to support reproducibility and community evaluation.

</details>


### [82] [Beyond the Frame: Redundancy, Not Trajectory, Carries the Privacy Leak in Video Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27982)
*Kavyansh Tyagi,Paolo Russo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有逐帧泄露评估严重低估了视频分割遗忘学习中的信息泄露；跨帧冗余才是可恢复信号的来源，据此提出的PACE直接抑制冗余，大幅降低池化攻击者的AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频分割的类别遗忘目标是让被删除类别无法从部署模型输出中重建。现有泄露评估算法逐帧检查，作者发现这种评估方式大幅低估了真实泄露程度，因此需要揭示这一被忽视的威胁并给出相应防御。

Method: 首先用池化攻击者（对约两秒片段内模型输出场取平均）重新评估泄露；用时间打乱对照实验（time-shuffled control）验证信号来源是跨帧冗余而非物体轨迹；随后提出PACE遗忘方法：使用严格置换不变的池化对抗判别器直接抑制冗余，并配合双向时间一致性目标。

Result: 池化攻击者可以在VSPW上对所有被评估方法、在Virtual KITTI 2上对多数方法恢复出遗忘类别，包括逐帧预测与retrain oracle视觉上无法区分的方法。PACE在VSPW上将池化攻击者AP降至18.79（person）与12.10（car），在Virtual KITTI 2上降至9.11（car），后者在保持可用效用的判定安全方法中抑制效果最强；同时在VSPW car上保留19.8 mIoU，而达到相当擦除效果的基线最多仅8.3。

Conclusion: 逐帧泄露评估不足以刻画视频分割遗忘学习的真实泄露，跨帧冗余是关键泄露通道；通过池化对抗判别与双向时间一致性直接压制冗余的PACE，能在判定安全前提下显著降低池化攻击者成功率，并在擦除与效用之间取得更优权衡。

Abstract: Class unlearning for video segmentation aims to ensure that a removed category cannot be reconstructed from a deployed model's outputs. Current leakage evaluation algorithms inspect one frame at a time, and we show that this understates leakage by a wide margin: an attacker that averages a model's output field over a two-second clip recovers the forgotten class from every method we evaluate on VSPW and most on Virtual KITTI~2, including models whose per-frame predictions are visually indistinguishable from a retrain oracle (a model trained from scratch without the forgotten class). We attribute the recoverable signal to cross-frame redundancy rather than object trajectory, establish this with a time-shuffled control that leaves leakage intact, and introduce PACE, an unlearning method that suppresses redundancy directly using an exactly permutation-invariant pooled adversarial discriminator and a two-sided temporal consistency objective. Across VSPW and Virtual KITTI~2, PACE lowers pooled attacker AP to $18.79$ (\texttt{person}) and $12.10$ (\texttt{car}) on VSPW and $9.11$ (\texttt{car}) on Virtual KITTI~2, achieving on the latter the strongest suppression among verdict-safe methods retaining usable utility, while retaining $19.8$ mIoU on VSPW \texttt{car} against at most $8.3$ for any baseline achieving comparable erasure.

</details>


### [83] [Recast the Past: Stable Long Video Generation with Full Context Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27986)
*Yukuo Ma,Cong Liu,Junke Wang,Junqi Liu,Haibin Huang,Zuxuan Wu,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReCam 将历史潜变量的局部时空块聚合为向量并直接投影到 DiT 隐藏空间，作为可经原生自注意力访问的记忆 token，配合双指针去噪策略，在长视频生成中以更少 token 保留完整历史，取得 VBench-Long 80.1 的最高总分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程视频生成需要保留不断扩张的历史信息，但直接在增长的历史 token 序列上做条件建模代价高昂。现有方法往往限制或压缩历史上下文，可能丢失未来生成所需的信息。

Method: 提出 ReCam：1）将历史潜变量的局部时空块分组为向量，直接投影进 Diffusion Transformer 的高维隐空间，使模型通过原生自注意力学习使用这些记忆 token，在降低 token 数量的同时保留对完整历史的块级访问能力；2）引入双指针去噪，对近期上下文帧与目标帧采用不同的噪声调度，以缓解自回归滚动中的时间漂移。

Result: 在对比方法中，ReCam 取得最高的 VBench-Long 总分 80.1，并在 R2M-Bench 上具备有竞争力的静态场景重访性能；在离屏主体重现的全部四项一致性指标上均领先，其中服装一致性为 4.09，而最佳基线为 3.03。

Conclusion: 通过块级记忆 token 投影与双调度去噪，ReCam 在不压缩历史信息的条件下实现了高效的长视频条件建模，并在综合质量、场景重访与主体一致性上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Long-horizon video generation requires retaining an ever-expanding history, but conditioning on its growing token sequence is costly. Existing methods restrict or compress historical context, potentially losing information needed for future generation. This work proposes ReCam, which groups local spatiotemporal blocks of historical latents into vectors and projects them directly into the high-dimensional hidden space of a Diffusion Transformer. The model learns to use the resulting memory tokens through native self-attention, reducing token count while retaining block-level access to the full history. To mitigate temporal drift during autoregressive rollout, we further introduce dual-pointer denoising with separate noise schedules for recent context and target frames. Among the evaluated methods, ReCam achieves the highest VBench-Long overall score of 80.1 and competitive static-scene revisitation performance on R2M-Bench. It also leads on all four consistency metrics for off-screen subject reappearance, including clothing consistency of 4.09 versus 3.03 for the best baseline.

</details>


### [84] [Matrix-Game 4.0: Harnessing Video Models for Real-Time Playable Game Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27999)
*Jihai Zhang,Runjia Qian,Kaiqin Kong,Biao Jiang,Puyi Wang,Siru Chen,Tianle Li,Guanjie Chen,Junyi Wu,Chubin Chen,Yuanhang Li,Kaichen Huang,Haobo Zhang,Yangguang Li,Wei Yu,Guibin Chen,Yu Cheng,Yahui Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可执行游戏框架与 Matrix-Game 4.0，将游戏执行与视觉生成解耦，实现单卡 480p/60FPS、按键到显示延迟 112ms 的可玩世界生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从"可控视频生成"迈向"真正可玩的世界"需要三要素：持久的世界状态、可执行的游戏规则、低延迟交互，而纯视频生成模型难以同时满足。

Method: 分离游戏执行与视觉生成：轻量游戏引擎维护世界状态并执行交互规则；多模态编码智能体将玩家想法转译为游戏代码与动态场景描述；基于自回归视频模型的渲染器，在对齐的低多边形与高保真视频对上训练，并以动态描述与记忆参考为条件，把低多边形代理渲染成精细画面；渲染器蒸馏为四帧块、1–2 步去噪的因果生成器，配合推理优化。

Result: 单张 RTX5090 上实现 480p、60FPS 游戏体验，按键到显示延迟 112ms；单步生成稳态吞吐 83FPS，峰值显存 18.2GiB；固定提示下 RTX5080 达 67FPS、RTX5070Ti 达 58FPS。

Conclusion: 展示了具备引擎强制机制的可定制射击与驾驶游戏，并开源代码、模型权重与低多边形游戏资产，支持可玩游戏世界的研究与创作。

Abstract: Moving from controllable videos to genuinely playable worlds requires persistent world state, executable game rules, and low-latency interaction. We outline a roadmap for the next generation of Matrix-Game that separates game execution from visual generation through an executable game harness, and introduce Matrix-Game 4.0 as the first system built on this design. A lightweight game engine maintains world state and executes interaction rules, a multimodal coding agent translates player ideas into game code and dynamic scene captions, and a renderer based on autoregressive video model transforms engine-rendered low-poly proxies into detailed visuals, conditioned on dynamic captions and memory references. This framework provides continuous guidance for visual generation and enables natural-language creation and modification of scenes and gameplay. Our renderer is trained on aligned low-poly and high-fidelity video pairs and distilled into a causal generator that generates in four-frame chunks with one or two denoising steps. Together with targeted inference optimizations, Matrix-Game 4.0 enables 480p, 60 FPS gameplay on a single NVIDIA RTX5090 with a key-press-to-display latency of 112ms. Single-step generation achieves 83FPS steady-state throughput with 18.2GiB of peak GPU memory usage. With fixed prompts, steady-state throughput also reaches 67FPS on an RTX5080 and 58FPS on an RTX5070Ti. We demonstrate customizable shooter and driving games with engine-enforced mechanics, and release code, model weights, and low-poly game assets to support research and creation of playable game worlds.

</details>


### [85] [Beyond the Evidence Boundary: Behavioral Mirage Under Progressive Degradation in Medical VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28000)
*Edric Castel C. Hao,Rez Samantha Z. Floresca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基线锚定的评估框架，用渐进图像退化追踪原本可靠的模型-样本对何时"失效"，并把在此"经验证据边界"上仍坚持作答的行为定义为"mirage（海市蜃楼）"；发现这种持续承诺无法被输出熵与弃答行为稳定反映，但可由残差流探针高精度解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型（VLM）通常在干净输入上评估，而真实临床场景存在渐进式证据丢失；已有鲁棒性研究表明性能下降时置信度仍可能很高，但未能界定"原本可靠的模型-样本对"从何时起不再可靠。

Method: 1) 构建基线锚定框架：以基线正确（baseline-correct）样本为起点，施加渐进图像退化并追踪其轨迹，确定经验证据边界；2) 将边界处"无论对错仍持续作答"定义为 mirage；3) 在 3 个 VLM、3 个医学数据集上分析（仅 MedGemma 基线正确样本量足够）；4) 利用 VQA-RAD 问题文本可变的特性，检验纯语言可答性与基线正确性、边界处承诺的关联；5) 在残差流上训练线性探针，用嵌套交叉验证解码"承诺 vs 弃答"。

Result: VQA-RAD 上纯语言可答性与基线正确性相关（AUROC 0.677），也与边界处承诺相关（AUROC 0.653–0.670）；MedGemma 越界后行为高度异质，包含直接 mirage、正确恢复、mirage 消解、持续弃答与振荡，各数据集-模态组合的 mirage 率为 16.0%–100.0%；尽管如此异质，残差流探针在边界处解码承诺/弃答的 AUROC 达 0.895–0.994（嵌套交叉验证）。

Conclusion: 渐进证据丢失会产生持续承诺，而输出熵与弃答行为并不能一致地反映这一现象；据此倡导边界感知评估、选项顺序控制，以及面向部分观测下可靠医学基础模型的更优弃答机制。

Abstract: VLMs for medical image interpretation are typically evaluated on clean inputs, despite the progressive evidence loss that can occur in high stakes clinical settings. Existing robustness studies show that performance can degrade while confidence remains high, but do not establish when a previously reliable model-case pair ceases to behave reliably. We introduce a baseline-anchored framework that traces baseline-correct cases through progressive image degradation to identify an empirical evidence boundary, and define mirage as continued commitment at this boundary, regardless of correctness. Across three VLMs on three medical datasets, only MedGemma provides a sufficiently large baseline-correct pool for trajectory analysis. On VQA-RAD, the only dataset in which the question text varies, language-only answerability is associated with baseline correctness (AUROC 0.677) and with commitment at the boundary (AUROC 0.653 to 0.670). MedGemma's post-boundary behavior is heterogeneous, including direct mirage, correct recovery, mirage dissolution, consistent abstention, and oscillation, with mirage rates of 16.0 to 100.0% across dataset-modality pairs. Despite this behavioral heterogeneity, residual-stream probes decode commit-versus-abstain behavior at the boundary with AUROC of 0.895 to 0.994 under nested cross-validation. These results show that progressive evidence loss can produce persistent commitment that output entropy and abstention behavior do not consistently reflect, motivating boundary-aware evaluation, option-order controls, and improved abstention mechanisms for reliable medical foundation models operating under partial observation.

</details>


### [86] [Differentiable Task-Driven Coded Aperture Camera Design with 3D Gaussian Scene Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28004)
*Chengyang Yan,Donald G. Dansereau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个可微分框架，将复杂相机系统与下游感知任务端到端联合优化；借助3D Gaussian Splatting构建可微成像过程，设计单目与立体两种编码孔径散焦深度相机，性能优于现有编码孔径设计与非可微相机设计基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机设计显著影响感知性能，但目前多由领域专家通过高成本试错完成，且与所服务任务割裂。已有联合设计方法存在局限：可微仿真仅限预先采集的2D图像，只能优化光学或图像处理流水线；非可微模拟器配合无导数或强化学习优化，则随设计参数量增加而难以扩展。

Method: 构建面向复杂相机系统与下游感知任务的可微分联合设计框架。以真实采集重建的3D Gaussian Splatting表示场景，提供可微的图像形成过程，从而支持对先前可微方法难以处理的庞大设计空间进行梯度优化。应用该框架设计两种编码孔径散焦深度相机（单目与立体配置），对编码孔径掩膜、相机参数与深度估计网络进行端到端联合优化。

Result: 实验表明，该可微方法优于现有编码孔径设计方法与不可微相机设计基线。

Conclusion: 该工作为自主系统中的自动化、原理化相机设计迈出了一步，说明基于3DGS的可微联合优化能够有效扩展至复杂相机系统的端到端设计。

Abstract: Camera design heavily influences perception task performance, yet cameras are typically designed by domain experts through costly experimentation, in isolation from the tasks they serve. Existing joint-design methods either rely on differentiable simulation limited to pre-captured 2D images, restricting them to optics or image-processing pipeline design, or use non-differentiable camera simulators optimised with derivative-free or reinforcement-learning methods that scale poorly with the number of design parameters. We present a differentiable framework for jointly designing complex camera systems and downstream perception tasks. We use it to design two coded-aperture depth-from-defocus cameras, a monocular and a stereo configuration, by jointly optimising the coded aperture mask, camera parameters, and a depth estimation network end-to-end. Our framework represents scenes with 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructed from real-world captures, providing a differentiable image formation process for gradient-based optimisation over design spaces intractable for prior differentiable approaches. Results demonstrate that our differentiable method improves over existing coded aperture design and non-differentiable camera design baselines, offering a step forward in automated and principled camera design for autonomous systems.

</details>


### [87] [AdaptMIL: Domain Adaptation for Multiple Instance Learning in Digital Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28007)
*Ben Vardi,Dana Schonberger,Yuval Friedmann,Zohar Yakhini,Iris Barshack,Alexander Loebel,Ariel Shamir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AdaptMIL，一种将域适应整合进多实例学习（MIL）的两阶段训练策略，用于提升病理基础模型在医院间分布偏移下的跨院表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型（PFM）通过 MIL 支持全切片图像的多种组织病理任务，但存在明显的跨医院分布偏移敏感性：任务头在一个医院数据上训练、在另一医院数据上使用时性能常下降。作者还发现，直接在 MIL 训练中施加域适应目标会干扰 MIL 聚合学习，甚至不如标准训练。

Method: AdaptMIL 采用两阶段训练流程，将表示层面的域对齐学习与 MIL 聚合学习解耦，从而避免域适应目标对 MIL 聚合的干扰。

Result: 在多个 WSI 数据集、任务、PFM、MIL 架构与域适应目标上的实验中，AdaptMIL 提升了跨医院性能，并优于其他适应方法。

Conclusion: 通过解耦域对齐与 MIL 聚合，AdaptMIL 有效缓解了跨医院分布偏移导致的性能退化；代码将在论文发表后开源。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models (PFMs) support a wide range of histopathology tasks on whole-slide images through multiple instance learning (MIL). Despite recent progress, these models remain sensitive to cross-hospital distribution shifts: performance often degrades when a task head (e.g., classification) is trained on data from one hospital and then used on data from another hospital. To address this challenge, we propose AdaptMIL, a training strategy for integrating domain adaptation into MIL training. AdaptMIL is motivated by our finding that directly applying domain adaptation objectives during MIL training can interfere with MIL aggregation learning, often resulting in performance degradation over standard training. AdaptMIL resolves this by decoupling representation-level domain alignment learning from MIL aggregation learning through a two-stage training procedure. We show that AdaptMIL improves cross-hospital performance and outperforms alternative adaptation methods across experiments spanning multiple WSI datasets, tasks, PFMs, MIL architectures, and domain adaptation objectives. AdaptMIL's code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [88] [P$^3$-VLM: A Point-based Alternative for Grounded 3D Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28018)
*Anna-Maria Halacheva,Jan-Nico Zaech,Sombit Dey,Luc Van Gool,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文揭示3D视觉语言模型基准中普遍存在"语义泄漏"：框提示携带类别信息，形成捷径；作者提出点提示协议并构建无检测器、以场景为中心的三通路模型P³-VLM，在点基定位字幕任务上取得52.1%的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D VLM的定位评测与提示协议高度依赖边界框作为空间提示或表示基元，而框本身高度包含物体类别信息，使模型可以走捷径忽略视觉信息。作者用一个"盲"LLM（仅输入框坐标）验证了这一点，因此在ScanNet Nr3D上盲模型的准确率高达36.4%，说明评测存在语义泄漏。

Method: 提出以单个3D点替代边界框的"点基定位提示"协议，用以量化并削弱该捷径（在该协议下盲LLM准确率从36.4%降至18.0%，LL3DA等3D VLM同样下降）。在此基础上提出P³-VLM：一种三通路（位置感知、任务感知、上下文感知）3D VLM，架构上重新设计，保持无检测器、以场景为中心，并适配3D高斯泼溅（3D GS）输入，完全不依赖边界框。

Result: P³-VLM在点基定位设置下的位置定位字幕任务上达到52.1%的SOTA准确率；同时在多种设置下取得一致提升，包括鲁棒性、分布外（OOD）泛化，以及非定位、以场景为中心的3D VQA基准上的表现。

Conclusion: 边界框提示会诱发语义捷径、削弱视觉-空间推理；改用点提示并配合三通路无检测器架构，可显著降低对框捷径的依赖，获得更强的视觉-空间推理能力与更好的泛化表现，并为3D定位评测协议提供更严谨的范式。

Abstract: Examining existing benchmarks for grounding in 3D VLMs reveals a pervasive "semantic leakage" in the evaluation and prompting protocols: a heavy reliance on bounding boxes as spatial prompts or representation primitives. Because bounding boxes are highly informative of object categories, they introduce a shortcut for the models to neglect visual information during 3D VLM training. We demonstrate this with a "blind" LLM that, prompted only with the box coordinates, can perform surprisingly well. To further analyze this shortcut, we propose point-based grounding prompting - querying models with a single 3D point instead of a box. Under this protocol, the accuracy of the blind LLM drops from 36.4% to 18.0% on ScanNet's Nr3D, and similarly degrades for 3D VLMs (e.g. LL3DA).
  Building on this insight, we introduce P$^3$-VLM, a novel tri-pathway 3D VLM that supports point-based prompts and achieves 52.1% state-of-the-art accuracy on location-grounded captioning under this setting. To achieve this, we redesign the architecture while keeping it detector-free, scene-centric, and suitable for 3D GS inputs. P$^3$-VLM utilizes three complementary pathways (location-, task-, and context-aware), enabling strong reasoning about scenes without any bounding box dependencies. The key benefit of point-based learning is a consistent improvement across settings: P$^3$-VLM achieves state-of-the-art performance under point-based grounding while also improving robustness, OOD generalization, and performance on ungrounded scene-centric 3D VQA benchmarks, reflecting its reduced reliance on bounding-box shortcuts and resulting stronger visual-spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [89] [Spherical Geometry-Aware Panoptic Scene Graph Generation for Equirectangular Panoramas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28033)
*Zhe Wu,Xiaoyan Fei,Xinyi Jiang,Yanxu Wei,Junming Rao,Yihong Yan,Xuewei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段球面几何感知框架，融合重叠透视视角预测以解决全景图PSGG的畸变、纬度相关像素面积与接缝问题，并构建340张高分辨率全景数据集，显著提升PQ与mR@50。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景全景场景图生成（PSGG）需在等距柱状投影（ERP）全景图上用分割掩码与有向关系表示场景实体，但ERP引入畸变、随纬度变化的像素面积以及人为接缝，直接在这些投影图上做PSGG效果受限。

Method: 两阶段球面几何感知框架：第一阶段基于观测可靠性、球面重叠度与角度邻近性融合多个重叠透视视角的预测；第二阶段关系推理结合球面面积感知聚合、加权无关系监督与双经度分数融合。同时构建含340张高分辨率全景、10,571个节点、6,632条有向关系的数据集。

Result: 相比对应基线，在视角匹配条件下球面融合使PQ提升36.03%；在PredCls设置（真值掩码与物体标签）下关系推理使mR@50提升11.20%；完整框架在SGDet设置（预测全景分割）下mR@50提升52.99%。

Conclusion: 结果表明球面几何建模对全景PSGG具有显著价值，可有效缓解ERP畸变、纬度面积偏差与接缝问题，并配套提供了新的全景PSGG数据集。

Abstract: Panoptic scene graph generation (PSGG) represents scene entities with segmentation masks and directed relations. Equirectangular projection (ERP) panoramas challenge PSGG through distortion, latitude-dependent pixel areas, and an artificial seam. We propose a two-stage spherical geometry-aware framework that fuses overlapping perspective-view predictions using observation reliability, spherical overlap, and angular proximity. Relation reasoning combines spherical-area-aware aggregation, weighted no-relation supervision, and dual-longitude score fusion. We also construct a dataset of 340 high-resolution panoramas with 10,571 nodes and 6,632 directed relations. Against corresponding baselines, spherical fusion improves PQ by 36.03% under matched views, while relation reasoning improves mR@50 by 11.20% under PredCls with ground-truth masks and object labels. The full framework improves mR@50 by 52.99% under SGDet with predicted panoptic segments. These results demonstrate the value of spherical geometry for panoramic PSGG.

</details>


### [90] [A Process-Informed, Photorealistic Digital Twin for Vision-Based Inline Inspection of C-SMC Strip Deposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28050)
*Patrick Flore,Fabian Röder,Kevin Chen,Andreas Gebhard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个融合刚体沉积仿真与偏振渲染的碳纤维SMC条带铺放数字孪生，自动生成带精确多任务标注的合成图像数据集，验证了纯合成数据训练检测模型可实现较高精度且合成-真实差距与真实采集批次间差异同量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SMC制造在线质量保证受限于缺乏大规模、标注准确的图像数据集，人工标注重叠条带等复杂场景的联合分布几乎不可行，且现有数据集难以提供多格式一致标签。

Method: 构建过程驱动的逼真数字孪生：用刚体仿真再现条带沉积运动学（沉降、摩擦、传送带运动），并用物理偏振渲染模型生成真实外观；同步输出定向包围盒、点标注、密度图及偏振图（AoLP/DoLP）等一致标签；生成16000余张图像、3300帧，并用YOLOv11、CSRNet及定向检测器在合成数据上训练、在真实图像上评估结构与纹理分布差异。

Result: 合成数据复现近各向同性纤维取向（轴向集中度r<0.05），与商用SMC沉积态一致；纯合成训练下YOLOv11与CSRNet检测mAP50最高达0.91，定向检测器mAP50=0.83；真实图像上合成-真实的边缘纹理KS距离0.46、分布重叠0.56，与同一材料两批真实采集之间的差异（0.41、0.57）同量级。

Conclusion: 该数字孪生可提供人工难以获得的精确可扩展真值，显著降低基于相机的纤维取向与体积含量在线检测的标注负担，是SMC在线质量保证的可行数据方案。

Abstract: Inline quality assurance for Sheet Molding Compound (SMC) manufacturing is hindered by the lack of large-scale, accurately labeled image datasets for training machine-learning models. This work presents a process-informed, photorealistic digital twin for carbon-fiber SMC strip deposition. A rigid-body simulation reproduces the deposition kinematics (settling, friction, belt motion), while a physically grounded polarization-rendering model produces realistic appearance, so that every image carries exact, mutually consistent labels in synchronized formats: oriented bounding boxes, point annotations, density maps, and polarization renderings (Angle and Degree of Linear Polarization). Because the joint distribution of many overlapping strips cannot be specified analytically, simulation yields exact, scalable ground truth that manual annotation cannot provide. The twin produces over 16,000 images across 3,300 frames, and the synthetic data replicate near-isotropic fiber orientation (axial concentration r < 0.05) consistent with as-deposited commercial SMC. Trained purely on synthetic data, YOLOv11 and CSRNet reach detection mAP50 up to 0.91, with a dedicated oriented-bounding-box detector confirming the oriented labels are directly trainable (mAP50 = 0.83); on real manufacturing images, the synthetic-to-real difference in edge and texture structure (Kolmogorov-Smirnov distance 0.46, distribution overlap 0.56) is of the same magnitude as the difference between two real acquisition series of the same material (0.41, overlap 0.57). The approach substantially reduces the labeling burden for camera-based inline inspection of fiber orientation and volume content.

</details>


### [91] [Assessing Representation Semantics Under Language Pretraining in Fine-Grained Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28051)
*Md Raisul Kibria,Sébastien Lafond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个轻量的"可解释性驱动"评估框架，在CUB-200上用受控两阶段协议训练两个最终都优化同一分类任务的编码器（分别由纯分类监督与图像描述生成监督预训练），通过注意力rollout与梯度加权rollout生成显著性图，并用5个指标（保真性与定位性）比较所得表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉模型评估主要依赖下游准确率，难以刻画监督信号如何塑造细粒度、低数据场景下的语义视觉表示的结构性差异，因此需要一个能直接观测并表示层面差异的比较框架。

Method: 在CUB-200上采用受控两阶段协议：两编码器最终均针对同一分类任务优化，使比较具有直接性；用attention rollout与gradient-weighted rollout生成显著性图；用5个指标评估，覆盖保真性（正/负扰动）与定位性（关键点强调、背景归因、Earth Mover's Distance）；并跨随机种子与多种解释方法重复；另在Oxford-IIIT Pet上做消融以检验趋势一致性。

Result: 跨随机种子与解释方法，描述生成监督得到的表示对显著区域移除更敏感、与标注部件对齐更好、对背景依赖更少；而纯分类监督的表示对低显著区域的移除略更鲁棒。Oxford-IIIT Pet上的消融在关键指标上呈现一致趋势。

Conclusion: 该框架使监督导致的表示差异可观测、可量化，超越单一下游准确率；为系统评估学习到的表示、比较不同训练与迁移学习策略提供了基础。

Abstract: Understanding how supervision signals shape semantic visual representations remains challenging, particularly in fine-grained, low-data settings. Standard evaluations of vision models rely on downstream accuracy, which often fails to capture structural differences in learned representations. We propose a compact explainability-driven framework for comparing encoders trained with classification-only and caption-generation supervision on CUB-200. Using a controlled two-stage protocol in which both encoders are ultimately optimized for the same classification task, we enable a direct comparison of the resulting representations. Saliency maps are generated using attention rollout and gradient-weighted rollout and evaluated with five metrics covering faithfulness (positive and negative perturbation) and localization (keypoint emphasis, background attribution, and Earth Mover's Distance). Across random seeds and explanation methods, caption supervision yields representations that are more sensitive to the removal of salient regions, better aligned with annotated object parts, and less reliant on background regions, whereas classification supervision produces representations that are slightly more robust to the removal of low-saliency regions. An ablation study on the Oxford-IIIT Pet dataset shows consistent trends across key metrics. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework makes supervision-induced representational differences observable and quantifiable beyond downstream accuracy alone. More broadly, it provides a systematic basis for assessing learned representations and comparing alternative training and transfer-learning strategies.

</details>


### [92] [Auditing Where Concept Bottleneck Accuracy Comes From on CUB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28059)
*Sascha Saralajew,Eleonora Poeta,Eliana Pastor,Ammar Shaker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文审计CBM/CEM在CUB-200-2011上的准确性来源，发现其性能主要来自非局部特征与内部嵌入中的类别信息泄漏，而非暴露的概念分数；建议任务与概念准确率应作为辅助而非主导评测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈模型宣称可解释可纠错，但CUB基准上仅用任务与概念准确率衡量，无法判断标签是否真的从暴露的概念分数预测而来，也无法判断概念是否由对应视觉证据支撑，因此需要审计其准确性的真实来源。

Method: 通过标注噪声分析（类级训练标签与逐图标注一致性、逐图标注训练的分类器精度）、泄漏探针（能否从概念分数重建CEM预测）、识别类别信息进入概念分数的四个设计轴（感受野、共享vs概念特定表征、范数依赖打分、任务监督），并进行消融实验（ResNet50改为局部BagNet块）、定位与遮挡实验。

Result: 类级标签与逐图标注仅约82%一致，逐图标注训练的模型仅约37%物种准确率；CEM预测无法由概念分数重建，缺失的类别信息存于内部嵌入。限制为局部BagNet块后，从概念分数可恢复的准确率由72%降至43%，接近真实标注可支撑水平，而联合训练又将其提高但牺牲概念准确率。局部性使归因可测但不保证成立：类级标签下局部概念分数用头部作为身体、翅膀、尾部概念的代理，CEM概念分数对遮挡其支撑部位几乎无响应。

Conclusion: 任务与概念准确率应支撑而非主导CBM评测；鉴于标注噪声，CUB上的准确率差距不应被过度解读。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) promise predictions that humans can inspect and correct through concepts such as "curved beak". Yet on CUB-200-2011, their standard benchmark, progress is measured like that of ordinary classifiers: by task and concept accuracy. Neither reveals whether the label is predicted from the exposed concept scores, or whether a concept is supported by visual evidence of that concept rather than by co-occurring features such as cues to the species as a whole. We audit where the accuracy of CBMs and Concept Embedding Models (CEMs) on CUB comes from. The CUB annotations are too noisy to explain it: class-level training labels agree with per-image annotations on only ~82% of image--concept pairs, and classifiers trained on per-image annotations reach only ~37% species accuracy. Leakage probes show that CEM predictions cannot be reconstructed from their concept scores; the missing class information sits in internal embeddings. We identify four design axes through which class information can enter concept scores---receptive field, shared versus concept-specific representations, norm-dependent scoring, and task supervision. An ablation shows that most of it enters through non-local features: restricting a ResNet50 backbone to local BagNet patches lowers the accuracy recoverable from concept scores from 72% to 43%, close to what ground-truth annotations support, while joint training raises it again at the cost of concept accuracy. Localization and occlusion show that locality makes grounding measurable but does not guarantee it: under class-level labels, local concept scores use the head as a proxy for body, wing, and tail concepts, and CEM concept scores barely respond to occluding their supporting part. Task and concept accuracy should thus support, not drive, the evaluation of CBMs, and given the noisy annotations, CUB accuracy gaps should not be overinterpreted.

</details>


### [93] [MoDDiT: Simple Token Routing in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28086)
*Jarek Liesen,Matthew T. Jackson,Benjamin Ellis,Shimon Whiteson,Jakob N. Foerster*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoDDiT 通过对 token 在 MLP 与自注意力中分别进行动态路由，实现扩散 Transformer 的计算稀疏化分配，最低仅处理 12.5% 的 token，在降低训练与推理成本的同时提升生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时视频生成、交互式世界模型与端侧图像合成都接近落地，Diffusion Transformer 样本质量优秀，瓶颈已转移到模型吞吐量。自然图像本身具有稀疏性（大量背景区域对场景内容贡献小、易于去噪），但 DiT 对所有图像 token 采用静态、均匀的处理方式，未能利用这一稀疏性。

Method: 提出 MoDDiT，一种简单的架构改动：让 token 在 MLP 和自注意力两类操作中分别独立学习路由，从而在 MLP 中实现随 token、图像和去噪时间步而变化的动态计算分配。

Result: 从头训练图像生成模型时，MoDDiT 显著降低训练与推理计算成本，最少仅需处理 12.5% 的 token；提高稀疏度与模型深度可进一步降本并优于未路由基线。学到的分配具有可解释性，揭示了计算量如何随视觉内容与噪声水平变化；在视频生成中，分配与运动相关；还可将稠密双向视频模型蒸馏为稀疏自回归学生模型。

Conclusion: MoDDiT 以极简的架构改动有效利用自然数据的稀疏性，实现动态计算分配，兼顾效率与生成质量，并具备良好的可解释性与向视频生成、模型蒸馏场景的可扩展性。

Abstract: Real-time video generation, interactive world models, and on-device image synthesis are almost a reality. Diffusion Transformers (DiT) achieve excellent sample quality, shifting the bottleneck to the throughput of the models themselves. Natural images are inherently sparse, as large portions of an image are often background, contribute little to the scene content, and are easy to denoise. DiTs fail to exploit this sparsity, as they process image tokens statically and uniformly. We propose MoDDiT, a simple architecture modification that learns to route tokens through both MLP and self-attention operations. By routing tokens through both blocks independently, MoDDiT allows for dynamic compute allocation in MLPs that varies across tokens, images, and denoising timesteps. Using MoDDiT to train image generation models from scratch significantly reduces computational costs of training and inference, processing as few as 12.5% of the tokens. By increasing sparsity and model depth, MoDDiT reduces costs and improves generation performance over unrouted baselines. The learned allocation is interpretable, revealing how computation varies with visual content and noise level. We also use MoDDiT to train video generation models, where allocation correlates with motion, and to distill a dense bidirectional video model into a sparse autoregressive student.

</details>


### [94] [WildAIGC-Bench: Can AI Image Detectors Handle the Wild? A Benchmark of Generated and Edited Images in Online Circulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28087)
*Zijian Yu,Hangwei Zhang,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 WildAIGC-Bench：从新闻网站、事实核查来源与社交媒体滚动采集、经人工核验的 407 张真实场景 AI 生成/编辑图像基准，用于检验检测器在压缩、截图、翻拍等在线条件下的召回能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与新闻事件和网络说法绑定的 AI 生成图像可能误导受众、助长欺诈或引发公共争议。现有基准多来自受控采集，缺乏时效性与真实在线退化条件，导致检测器在真实场景下的表现被高估。

Method: 构建持续采集流水线：定期从新闻网站、事实核查源与相关社交媒体发现样本，经自动筛选与初步标注后，由人工依据图像级证据复核；每张图像附带来源网页链接与证据摘要，并保留压缩、截图、翻拍等线上真实状态。

Result: 基准含 407 张经核验的 AI 生成或编辑图像。评测专用视觉检测器、多模态大模型及简单的 Perception Encoder 基线，相对既有基准出现明显的召回差距。

Conclusion: WildAIGC-Bench 兼具实用检测挑战与可扩展的数据来源核查基础，支持持续扩充数据与基于来源的验证；数据集已开源发布。

Abstract: AI-generated images linked to news events and online claims can mislead audiences, enable fraud, or cause public controversy. We introduce WildAIGC-Bench, built through recurring collection across news websites, fact-checking sources, and related social media content. The pipeline combines broad coverage, timely updates, and human-verified labels through scheduled discovery, automatic screening and preliminary annotation, and human review of image-specific evidence. The benchmark contains 407 verified AI-generated or edited images, each accompanied by a source webpage link and an evidence summary. Images retain the conditions encountered online, including compression, screenshots, and recapture. Evaluation of dedicated visual detectors, multimodal large language models, and a simple Perception Encoder baseline reveals substantial recall gaps relative to existing benchmarks. WildAIGC-Bench provides both a practical detection challenge and a basis for continued data expansion and source-based verification. The dataset is released at https://github.com/YuZijian/WildAIGC-Bench.

</details>


### [95] [LatticeRank: Spacing-Ranked Consistency for Native-Grid 3D Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28091)
*Jingjia Yan,Yue Zhuo,Zhonghua Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 LatticeRank——仅在训练期使用的框架，在物理空间对齐不同体素间距下的预测后验，并按体素间距排序分配一致性监督（聚焦最粗网格），推理时无需集成，在 MSD Task04 上显著提升粗网格 Dice。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体素间距变化不仅改变分辨率，还改变离散采样格、张量几何与可用解剖证据。常规流程将图像重采样到统一网格，而直接以原始网格部署时需模型自身容忍这些变化。作者观察到候选网格并非可交换视图：粗网格丢失更多结构信息，但标准一致性目标对它们赋予相同权重，这一不匹配构成研究动机。

Method: 提出 LatticeRank：训练期框架，将各候选网格的预测后验映射到物理空间进行对齐；依据请求的体素间距排序分配一致性监督，使辅助损失集中作用于最粗的候选网格。推理时仅做单次网络前向，不使用候选分支或集成。另设计对照阶梯实验，分离网格暴露、后验一致性与间距排序分配三者的贡献。

Result: 在 MSD Task04 上，LatticeRank 于五个随机种子下将粗网格 Extra Dice 相比均值一致性提升 0.03436，同时满足预设的参考网格非劣效性标准。阶梯实验显示：网格暴露带来最大恢复，后验一致性带来二次增益，间距排序分配给出最佳的直接网格结果。

Conclusion: 请求的体素间距为直接网格一致性学习提供了一种简单、与模型无关的排序信号；按间距加权的一致性监督可有效提升变间距下的三维分割性能，且不增加推理开销。

Abstract: Variable voxel spacing changes more than image resolution: it alters the discrete sampling lattice, tensor geometry, and anatomical evidence available to a 3D segmentation network. While conventional pipelines restore images to a canonical grid, direct-grid deployment requires the model itself to tolerate these changes. We observe that candidate grids are not exchangeable views -- a coarse grid removes more structural information than a mildly resampled one -- yet standard consistency objectives assign them equal influence. We therefore propose LatticeRank, a training-only framework that aligns candidate posteriors in physical space and allocates consistency supervision by voxel-spacing order: among endpoint-matched candidates, the auxiliary loss concentrates on the coarsest requested grid, while inference retains a single network forward with no candidate branch or ensemble. On MSD Task04, LatticeRank improves coarse-grid Extra Dice over mean consistency by 0.03436 across five seeds while meeting the prespecified reference-grid noninferiority criterion. A controlled ladder shows that grid exposure provides the largest recovery, posterior consistency adds a second gain, and spacing-ranked allocation yields the strongest direct-grid result; requested spacing thus provides a simple, model-independent ordering signal for direct-grid consistency learning.

</details>


### [96] [Reliability-Guided Vision-Language Decoding with Text-Aware Skip Refinement for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28118)
*Sivan Talaei,Fatemeh Daneshfar,Mustaqeem Khan,Abdulhady Abas Abdullah,Mourad Oussalah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RELiF 视觉-语言分割框架，通过可靠性引导的解码机制有选择地融合医学文本，在 X 线、CT 与内镜分割任务上取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割在目标视觉区分度低、结构不规则或跨模态差异大时仍然困难；医学文本可缓解歧义，但无差别融合可能引入不可靠的引导信息。

Method: 提出 RELiF 框架，包含可靠性引导融合门控（RFG）自适应控制跨模态更新，以及文本引导跳跃精炼适配器（TSRA）利用文本引导精炼高分辨率特征；并基于可靠性响应导出不确定性表示。

Result: 在放射影像、CT 与内镜分割任务上表现具竞争力，适应多样成像特性与目标外观；额外分析考察了效率与稳定性；可靠性响应导出的不确定性表征与预测难度相关，分割错误像素处出现更高不确定性值的频率更高。

Conclusion: 可靠性引导的文本融合是避免不可靠跨模态引导的有效途径，且其派生的不确定性可作为预测难度的指示信号。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation remains challenging when targets exhibit limited visual distinction, irregular structure, or substantial variation across imaging modalities. Medical text can reduce this ambiguity, but indiscriminate fusion may introduce unreliable guidance. We present RELiF, a vision--language framework that integrates text through reliability-guided decoding. Its Reliability-guided Fusion Gate (RFG) adaptively controls cross-modal updates, while the Text-guided Skip Refinement Adapter (TSRA) refines high-resolution features using text guidance. RELiF is evaluated on radiographic, CT, and endoscopic segmentation tasks, where it shows competitive performance across diverse imaging characteristics and target appearances. Additional analyses examine efficiency and stability. The complementary uncertainty representation derived from the reliability response also shows an association with prediction difficulty, with higher uncertainty values occurring more frequently in pixels associated with segmentation errors. Code: https://github.com/SeivanTalaie/RELiF.

</details>


### [97] [PointForward V2: Point-Aligned Interaction for Feedforward Driving Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28120)
*Xianqi Wang,Cheng Chi,Yuechuan Pu,Hongcheng Luo,Junda Cheng,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Bing Wang,Xin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PointForward V2 通过引入场景结构化寄存器（静态场景寄存器 + 动态实例寄存器）在提升的 3D 点查询之间实现可扩展的时空交互，避免全局注意力，在三个驾驶数据集上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈驾驶场景重建方法（PointForward）将图像观测提升为稀疏 3D 查询，但这些查询基本独立处理，缺乏跨空间与时间的信息交换；而对数万个点查询做直接全局注意力在计算上不可行。

Method: 核心思想是以紧凑的场景结构化寄存器作为中介实现查询交互。静态背景方面，逐帧 Static Scene Registers 压缩点信息、跨帧通信并将聚合上下文回传查询；每个动态实例在不同帧的查询被对齐到物体中心的规范化空间，并通过实例专属的 Dynamic Instance Registers 交互。此外采用结合对齐空间坐标与原始时间戳的 4D 位置编码，使交互空间对齐同时保持时间可区分。

Result: 在 Waymo、nuScenes 和 Argoverse 2 三个大规模驾驶基准上均取得最先进性能，并实现了无需稠密全局注意力的可扩展时空推理。

Conclusion: 将点对齐表示推进为点对齐交互（point-aligned interaction）是有效且可扩展的方向；借助寄存器中介与 4D 位置编码，可在稀疏 3D 查询上直接完成时空推理。

Abstract: Feedforward reconstruction of dynamic driving scenes requires integrating observations across multiple views and time steps into a coherent 3D representation. PointForward established state-of-the-art feedforward driving reconstruction by lifting image observations into sparse 3D queries, demonstrating the effectiveness of point-aligned scene representations. However, these queries are largely processed independently, limiting information exchange across space and time, while direct global attention among tens of thousands of point queries is computationally prohibitive. In this work, we present \textbf{PointForward V2}, which advances point-aligned representation to point-aligned interaction. Our key idea is to mediate query interaction through compact, scene-structured registers. For the static background, frame-wise Static Scene Registers compress point information, communicate across frames, and propagate the aggregated context back to the queries. For each dynamic instance, queries from different frames are aligned in an object-centric canonical space and interact through instance-specific Dynamic Instance Registers. We further employ 4D positional encoding that combines aligned spatial coordinates with original timestamps, enabling spatially aligned yet temporally distinguishable interactions. In this way, PointForward V2 enables scalable spatio-temporal reasoning directly among lifted 3D queries without dense global attention. Experiments on Waymo, nuScenes, and Argoverse 2 demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across large-scale driving benchmarks.

</details>


### [98] [GAMF-Net: Grouped Availability-Aware Multimodal Fusion for Smartphone Skin Lesion Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28129)
*Yue Zhuo,Jingjia Yan,Fangyu Liu,Lingjun Liu,Zongjun Chen,Zhisheng Luo,Zhonghua Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GAMF-Net：将10个临床变量分为人口学、病史风险、皮损表现三组，各组联合编码取值与可用性掩码；图像分支给基础logits，各临床组产生图像条件下的类别级残差证据，仅在可用组上做可用性归一化等权融合，全部缺失时严格退化为纯图像通路。在PAD-UFES-20上患者无交叠五折协议下取得Acc 74.17±2.85%、BACC 70.11±2.11%、Macro-F1 68.85±0.85%、Macro-AUROC 93.42±1.20%，优于全部重实现的单模态与多模态基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机采集的临床图像为资源受限场景提供低成本皮肤病变分类数据源，但图像无法完整反映病史与皮损症状。现有融合面临三个问题：异质临床变量缺乏结构化组织、观测值与插补值界限模糊（可用性歧义）、临床信息可能干扰视觉证据。

Method: 将十个临床变量按语义分为人口学、病史相关风险、皮损表现三组，每组同时编码字段取值与字段级可用性掩码；图像分支输出基础分类logits，各临床组生成以图像为条件的类别级残差证据；仅可用组参与可用性归一化的等权融合，当临床信息全部缺失时模型精确退化为纯图像通路。

Result: 在PAD-UFES-20上采用患者无交叠的五折划分（训练/验证/测试分离），取得Accuracy 74.17±2.85%、BACC 70.11±2.11%、Macro-F1 68.85±0.85%、Macro-AUROC 93.42±1.20%，优于统一协议下重实现的全部图像单模态与多模态基线；相比最强基线MetaBlock分别提升2.37、2.59、4.14、1.02个百分点。消融显示：临床信息显著改善类别均衡性能；受限等权融合优于动态融合；皮损表现组提供最有信息量的临床补充。

Conclusion: 以语义分组加可用性掩码编码、图像条件残差证据与可用性归一化等权融合，可在保证视觉证据主导的同时稳健利用不完整临床信息，并在缺失情形下保持一致退化行为，适合资源受限场景的皮肤病变分类。

Abstract: Smartphone clinical images provide a low-cost data source for computer-aided skin lesion classification in resource-constrained settings, but images alone cannot fully capture patients' medical histories and lesion symptoms. To address the lack of structured organization among heterogeneous clinical variables, the ambiguity between observed and imputed values, and the risk that clinical information may interfere with visual evidence, we propose GAMF-Net, a grouped availability-aware multimodal fusion network. Ten clinical variables are divided into three semantic groups---demographics, history-related risk, and lesion presentation---and each group jointly encodes field values and field-level availability masks. The image branch produces the base classification logits, while each clinical group generates image-conditioned class-level residual evidence. Only available groups participate in availability-normalized equal-weight fusion; when all clinical information is unavailable, the model reduces exactly to the image-only pathway. We evaluate the method on PAD-UFES-20 under a patient-disjoint five-fold protocol with separate training, validation, and test partitions. GAMF-Net achieves an Accuracy of $74.17\pm2.85\%$, a balanced accuracy (BACC) of $70.11\pm2.11\%$, a Macro-F1 of $68.85\pm0.85\%$, and a Macro-AUROC of $93.42\pm1.20\%$, outperforming all image-only and multimodal baselines reimplemented under a unified protocol. Compared with the strongest baseline, MetaBlock, the corresponding improvements are 2.37, 2.59, 4.14, and 1.02 percentage points, respectively. Ablation results show that clinical information substantially improves class-balanced performance, constrained equal-weight fusion is more effective than dynamic fusion, and the lesion-presentation group provides the most informative clinical complement.

</details>


### [99] [OV-Foam: How 3D Representations Shape Semantic Feature Lifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28131)
*Yousef Rajeh,Alejandra Esparza Pelcastre,Ivan Viola,Dominik Engel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OV-Foam，用PowerFoam（幂图划分场景）替代3D高斯溅射作为底层表示，消除基元重叠，使特征提升问题更接近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇分割的特征提升方法依赖简单反投影把特征分配给基元。3D高斯溅射的基元重叠严重，一条射线上有大量基元，反投影在此情形下难以得到最优解（反投影仅在无两条基元共享同一条射线时才等价于最小二乘可解问题），因此重建效果次优。

Method: 受上述观察启发，提出OV-Foam，采用PowerFoam作为底层表示。该表示利用幂图（power diagram）对场景进行划分，使基元之间零重叠，并显著减少每条射线上的基元数量，从而简化特征提升问题，实现更接近最优的特征分配。

Result: 理论分析表明OV-Foam的空间划分基元可达到接近最优的分配；实验显示其开放词汇分割性能优于基于高斯的基线方法。

Conclusion: 用零重叠的幂图划分表示替代高斯基元，是改善开放词汇分割特征提升质量的有效途径。

Abstract: Existing feature lifting approaches for open-vocabulary segmentation rely on simple backprojection to assign features to primitives. In the case of 3D Gaussian splatting reconstruction, the primitives overlap heavily, leading to suboptimal reconstructions, governed by many primitives per ray. Backprojection, however, reduces to a least-squares solvable problem, exactly when no two primitives share a ray. Motivated by this insight, we propose OV-Foam, which uses PowerFoam as the underlying representation. This representation uses a power-diagram to partition the scene, resulting in zero overlap between primitives and a substantial reduction in primitives per ray, which ultimately simplifies the lifting problem and allows for closer-to-optimal feature assignment. We show that the space partitioning primitives of OV-Foam reach near-optimal assignment, and our experiments demonstrate improved open-vocabulary segmentation over Gaussian baselines.

</details>


### [100] [DocLayout-YOLO: Axis-Attentive Document Layout Detector with Mesh-Guided Synthetic Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28171)
*Zhiyuan Zhao,Hengrui Kang,Guang Liang,Bin Wang,Conghui He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DocLayout-YOLO，结合合成文档预训练与页面轴上下文建模，实现异构文档的高精度实时版面分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YOLO 是实时检测的主流范式，但其在异构文档版面分析场景下的适配性研究不足，现有实时检测器难以兼顾准确性与效率。

Method: 数据层面：提出 Mesh-candidate BestFit 算法构建大规模合成预训练语料 MeshLayout-1M，覆盖多样文档版面以提升异构场景适应性；模型层面：提出 Page-Axis Layout Attention (PALA)，沿水平与垂直页面轴捕捉长程依赖，同时保持计算高效。

Result: 在四个异构文档基准上取得最优性能，mAP50:95 分别为 D4LA 72.4%、M6Doc 75.5%、HJDataset 96.0%、PRIMA-LAD 73.1%；相较现有实时检测器实现了更优的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 合成预训练数据与页面轴注意力机制可有效提升实时检测器对异构文档的适配能力，DocLayout-YOLO 在文档版面分析中达到精度与效率的良好平衡，代码、模型与数据均已开源。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient document layout analysis is critical for modern document parsing systems. However, although the YOLO framework represents a leading paradigm for the real-time detector, its adaptation to heterogeneous documents remains underexplored. To address this gap, we propose DocLayout-YOLO, a document-specialized real-time detector that integrates diverse synthetic document pretraining and page-axis context modeling. From the data perspective, we introduce MeshLayout-1M, a synthetic pretraining corpus generated by the proposed Mesh-candidate BestFit algorithm, which provides large-scale and diverse document layouts for pretraining and improves adaptation to heterogeneous document scenarios. From the model perspective, we propose Page-Axis Layout Attention (PALA), which captures long-range dependencies along the horizontal and vertical page axes while remaining computationally efficient. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DocLayout-YOLO achieves state-of-the-art performance on four heterogeneous document benchmarks, obtaining 72.4%/75.5%/96.0%/73.1% mAP50:95 on D4LA, M6Doc, HJDataset, and PRIMA-LAD, respectively. Compared with existing real-time detectors, DocLayout-YOLO establishes a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off in document layout analysis. Code, model, and data are available at https://github.com/opendatalab/DocLayout-YOLO/tree/release-v2.

</details>


### [101] [CrossFit-GS: Out-of-Fold Pose Estimation for COLMAP-Free 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28190)
*Zijian Wu,Jinliang Wang,Mingfeng Jiang,Zidian Lin,Ying Song,Ziqian Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CrossFit-GS是一个免位姿3DGS渐进式框架，通过交错子图与延迟提交策略，用未被目标帧优化过的"对偶子图"作为独立几何参考来交叉精化位姿，从而抑制累积轨迹漂移与错误地图的自强化循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免位姿3DGS希望绕开繁琐的SfM，直接从无位姿图像序列重建场景。但现有方法存在位姿与地图耦合导致的累积轨迹漂移，以及畸变地图误差自我强化的循环问题。

Method: 将输入序列划分为交错子集并维护两个互补子图；每个候选位姿与一个"冻结的"对偶子图交叉精化（该子图从未用目标帧优化过，提供独立几何参考）；采用棋盘式验证与空间覆盖保障机制拒绝不可靠更新，仅将通过验证的位姿提交至其所属子图；轨迹精化完成后丢弃中间子图，用固定的精化位姿从零训练最终全局表示。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明，CrossFit-GS显著降低了累积轨迹漂移，并在相机位姿估计和新视角合成两方面均稳定优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过延迟提交与对偶子图交叉验证的解耦设计，可有效打破位姿-地图误差传播闭环，为免位姿3DGS提供更稳健的轨迹与重建质量。

Abstract: Pose-free 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) aims to bypass cumbersome Structure-from-Motion (SfM) by directly reconstructing scenes from unposed image sequences. In this work, we present CrossFit-GS, a deferred-commitment progressive framework that mitigates cumulative trajectory drift and breaks the self-reinforcing loop of distorted-map error propagation. Specifically, we partition the input sequence into interleaved subsets and maintain two complementary submaps. Each candidate pose is cross-refined against a frozen opposite submap that has never been optimized using the target frame, thereby providing an independent geometric reference for pose estimation. Checkerboard validation and spatial coverage safeguards further reject unreliable updates, and only verified poses are committed to their corresponding owner submap. Once the trajectory has been refined, the intermediate submaps are discarded and the final global representation is trained from scratch using the fixed refined poses.
