Extensive evaluations across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that CrossFit-GS substantially reduces cumulative trajectory drift and consistently improves both camera pose estimation and novel-view synthesis over existing methods.

</details>


### [102] [Unified RAW Image Exposure Correction and Restoration via Linear Illumination and Frequency-Adaptive Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28191)
*Yingkai Zhang,Yunhao Zou,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的RAW域曝光校正与恢复框架RIECR，将问题分解为线性光照校正与残差退化恢复两个耦合子问题，并构建包含223个场景、1,784对真实配对RAW数据的基准，实验表明其在真实RAW曝光校正上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曝光不当会同时损害图像亮度和可恢复的场景细节。现有sRGB方法受非线性ISP流程限制，而RAW方法通常只针对单一退化类型（如低光去噪或高光恢复），缺乏对欠曝和过曝的统一处理。

Method: 遵循RAW成像模型，将问题分解为两个耦合子问题：（1）线性光照校正网络LICN，利用通道特定的RAW线索估计曝光感知的线性增益，借助Green通道高信噪比实现稳健提亮、利用R/B通道相对不易饱和实现高光压暗；（2）双分支频域增强网络FEN，通过频率动态卷积FDC将卷积响应分解为高低频分量，按曝光上下文自适应抑制放大噪声或恢复截断纹理。

Result: 构建了包含223个场景、1,784对配对样本的真实RAW基准，覆盖复杂多重曝光误差；与16种先进方法对比，RIECR在真实RAW曝光校正上取得最优性能。

Conclusion: 直接在RAW域进行联合曝光校正与恢复的统一框架是可行且有效的，物理模型驱动的分解结合频域动态卷积能同时处理欠曝与过曝场景，并优于现有sRGB与RAW方法。

Abstract: Incorrect exposure degrades both image brightness and recoverable scene detail. Existing sRGB-based correction methods are limited by the non-linear image signal processing pipeline, whereas RAW-based methods are usually developed for a single degradation type, such as low-light denoising or high-light recovery. In this paper, we focus on joint exposure correction and restoration directly in the RAW domain and propose a unified RAW Image Exposure Correction and Restoration (RIECR) framework for both under- and over-exposed images. RIECR decomposes the problem into two coupled sub-problems that follow the RAW imaging model: linear illumination correction and residual degradation restoration. First, a Linear Illumination Correction Network (LICN) estimates exposure-aware linear gains by using channel-specific RAW cues. It exploits the high signal-to-noise ratio of Green channels for robust brightening and the relatively unsaturated Red/Blue responses for high-light darkening. Second, a dual-branch Frequency-based Enhancement Network (FEN) restores the remaining degradation with a Frequency Dynamic Convolution (FDC) module. FDC decomposes convolutional responses into low- and high-frequency components and adaptively suppresses amplified noise or recovers clipped textures according to the exposure context. We further build a real paired RAW benchmark containing 223 scenes and 1,784 pairs covering complex multi-exposure errors. Experiments against sixteen state-of-the-art methods show that RIECR achieves state-of-the-art performance on real RAW exposure correction.

</details>


### [103] [Offline Makes Online Better: Calibrated Cache Compression for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28192)
*Zhen Wang,Ning Lai,Qingtao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CALM：利用离线校准得到的层-头风险图，结合动态规划在给定缓存预算下为不同层与注意力头分配不同的token合并比例，从而在同等内存下显著优于均匀合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成中，token合并通过对时间冗余token聚类并仅保留少量代表来压缩KV-cache。但现有方法在所有注意力层和头上采用统一的合并比例，忽略了不同层/头对合并的敏感度差异。作者观察到各层各头对token合并的敏感度差异显著却彼此相关，且这种模式在不同prompt间保持一致，因此存在可被利用的固定结构。

Method: CALM（Calibrated Allocation for Layer-Head Merging）采用离线校准+在线压缩的框架。由于穷举所有层-头分配代价过高，作者提出以注意力输出误差作为端到端视频质量的轻量代理指标来评估分配风险，并据此离线构建固定的层-头风险图；推理时在给定缓存预算下用动态规划求解最优的合并比例分配方案。

Result: 在三个不同的自回归视频模型上，CALM在相同内存预算下一致优于均匀token合并，LPIPS相对原视频降低约21%，同时视频质量评分更高。

Conclusion: 层-头粒度的差异化合并比例分配相比均匀分配能更有效地利用缓存预算；注意力输出误差可作为可靠且低成本的代理指标指导离线分配，且所得风险图具有跨prompt的稳定性，具备实用价值。

Abstract: Token merging is an effective approach to reducing KV-cache memory consumption in autoregressive video generation by clustering temporally redundant tokens and retaining only a small set of representatives. Existing methods, however, typically apply a uniform merging ratio across all attention layers and heads. We observe that different layers and heads exhibit substantially different yet jointly correlated sensitivities to token merging, and that these sensitivity patterns remain consistent across prompts. Based on this observation, we introduce CALM, a Calibrated Allocation for Layer-Head Merging that uses offline calibration to improve online cache compression. Exhaustively evaluating all possible layer-head allocations is prohibitively expensive. To enable efficient calibration, we show that attention-output error strongly correlates with end-to-end video quality and can therefore serve as a lightweight proxy for allocation risk assessment. We evaluate different allocation strategies using this proxy and construct a fixed layer-head risk map offline. Given a cache budget at inference time, CALM uses dynamic programming to determine the best allocation of merging ratios. Experiments across three different autoregressive video models demonstrate that CALM consistently outperforms uniform token merging under the same memory budget, by reducing the LPIPS to original videos by around 21% with higher video-quality scores.

</details>


### [104] [Attractive Faces Skew Text-to-Image Reward Models and Leaderboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28195)
*Harvey Mannering,Zhiwu Huang,Adam Prügel-Bennett*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出，人类偏好评价中"感知吸引力"会压过图像质量，导致人脸图像的质量评测、Elo排行榜和人类偏好模型（如PickScore等）被吸引力偏差主导，并提出数据收集与训练后的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类偏好已成为文生图模型评估、引导、微调与排序的核心，但"人类偏好本身易受图像生成中的偏差影响"这一问题研究不足。作者希望揭示吸引力偏差如何扭曲质量判断与偏好模型，从而推动能解耦图像质量与人类认知偏差的评估方法。

Method: (1) 受控用户研究：让人工标注者在同等保真度的真实照片之间、以及不吸引人的真实人脸与吸引人的AI生成人脸之间进行质量排序；(2) 基于用户研究数据模拟Elo排名；(3) 对40个已标注人脸属性单独编辑，分析各属性与5个偏好模型（PickScore、MPS、HPSv2.1、HPSv3）分数的相关性及回归预测力；(4) 考察偏好对齐生成对脸部特征的偏移与对显式提示的控制削弱；(5) 探索数据收集与训练后阶段的缓解策略。

Result: 用户研究会将更有吸引力的脸评为更高质量，即使不吸引人的真实人脸与吸引人的AI生成人脸对比时也如此；模拟Elo排名显示吸引力可颠倒排行榜，使合成吸引人脸超越真实不吸引人脸。在5个偏好模型中，4个（PickScore、MPS、HPSv2.1、HPSv3）上吸引力是分数的最大相关因素，回归分析中亦为最强预测因子。偏好对齐生成使脸部朝更年轻、更女性化方向偏移，并能覆盖对不吸引人脸部的显式提示，限制用户控制力。作者还提出了若干缓解策略。

Conclusion: 人类偏好模型混入了吸引力等认知偏差，使评估与生成控制不可靠；需要能够将图像质量与人类认知偏差解耦的偏好模型，以实现更可信的评估。

Abstract: Human preference is now central to the evaluation, guidance, finetuning, and ranking of text-to-image models. However, the susceptibility of human preferences to bias in image generation remains underexplored. We identify a key observation: for human faces, perceived attractiveness overrides image quality in preference judgments, even when comparing real photographs of identical fidelity. In a controlled user study, annotators rank more attractive faces as higher quality, even when unattractive real faces are compared with attractive AI-generated ones. We examine the downstream consequences of this bias. Firstly, simulating Elo rankings from user study data shows attractiveness can invert a leaderboard's ranking, elevating synthetic attractive faces over real, unattractive ones. Secondly, the same bias reappears in the human preference models. Editing 40 annotated facial attributes in isolation, we find that attractiveness is the strongest correlate of score for four of five human preference models (PickScore, MPS, HPSv2.1, HPSv3), and the strongest predictor of score in regression analysis. We further show that preference aligned generation shifts faces toward younger, more feminine faces, and can override explicit prompts for unattractive faces, thereby limiting user control. Finally, we explore mitigation strategies, both for data collection and post training on human preference models. These findings highlight the need for preference models that disentangle image quality from human cognitive bias, enabling more reliable evaluation.

</details>


### [105] [FRAGMENT: Factorized Graph Representations for Document Generation and Editing via Entity-Aware Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18679)
*Ayoub El Bouchtili,Guilhaume Leroy-Meline*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FRAGMENT框架，将文档表示为类型化关系图，并以p(structure)·p(content|structure)分解建模，用两阶段（Architect生成拓扑、Builder填充内容）实现文档生成、伪造检测与可控编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化文档（发票、表单、报告、论文）的意义来自空间布局、文本内容与逻辑结构三者的交互；像素级或token级的生成模型难以有效捕捉这些依赖关系，因此需要一个显式刻画结构与内容交互的生成框架。

Method: 将文档建模为类型化关系图，把分布分解为p(structure, content)=p(structure)·p(content|structure)。第一阶段Architect为以文档类别为条件、带因果掩码的Transformer，自回归生成图拓扑与类型化空间关系；第二阶段Builder为基于GATv2的图注意力网络，为图补充归一化边界框、文本与视觉风格属性。两阶段均为显式似然模型，可得到可计算的文档级似然用于伪造检测；另加提示条件扩展，通过交叉注意力将指令嵌入注入Builder以支持语义与实体感知的编辑。在DocLayNet上训练，在FUNSD和SROIE上微调。

Result: 在DocLayNet、FUNSD、SROIE上与代表性自回归、仅布局和基于图的基线进行对比实验，给出该因子化图生成框架特性的经验性分析与权衡；文档级似然可作为伪造检测的异常分数，提示条件扩展支持可控编辑。

Conclusion: 以类型化关系图为核心、显式分解结构与内容的生成建模，为结构化文档的理解与生成提供了统一框架，可同时服务于生成、伪造检测与可控编辑，并在多个数据集上给出了与基线对比的实证分析。

Abstract: Structured documents such as invoices, forms, reports, and scientific articles derive meaning from the interplay between spatial layout, textual content, and logical structure. Generative models operating at the pixel or token level often struggle to capture these dependencies effectively. We explore FRAGMENT, a generative framework that represents a document as a typed relational graph and factorizes its distribution as p(structure, content) = p(structure) * p(content | structure). The framework consists of two stages. The first stage, the Architect, is a causally masked Transformer conditioned on document category that autoregressively generates the graph topology and typed spatial relations. The second stage, the Builder, is a GATv2-based graph attention network that enriches the graph with normalized bounding boxes, text, and visual style attributes. Both stages define explicit likelihood models, yielding a tractable document-level likelihood that serves as an anomaly score for forgery detection. For controlled editing, a prompt-conditioned extension injects instruction embeddings into the Builder through cross-attention, enabling semantic and entity-aware modifications. We describe training on DocLayNet and fine-tuning on FUNSD and SROIE. Experiments on DocLayNet, FUNSD, and SROIE evaluate FRAGMENT alongside representative autoregressive, layout-only, and graph-based baselines, providing an empirical analysis of the characteristics and trade-offs of the proposed factorized graph generation framework.

</details>


### [106] [Learn to Modify: Self-evolving Multi-agent Refinement Framework for Conversational Text-to-Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28210)
*Jianan Li,Jiawei Zhao,Yangtao Zhou,Zhifu Zhao,Shaoxin Wu,Yutao Wang,Dezhen Wang,Hua Chu,Fu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出对话式动作生成范式与自进化多智能体精修框架SMART，通过语义引导的掩码动作编辑与基于记忆的自进化策略，实现多轮对话下的局部动作精修。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到动作生成方法多为单次生成：用户若对某些动作细节不满，只能整段重新生成，既不高效，也会破坏已建立的运动学上下文。实际系统应支持迭代式用户反馈，仅修改受影响的时空区域而保留其余动作。

Method: 引入对话式动作生成范式，要求系统协同完成指令理解、动作生成、局部编辑与质量评估，并借助LLM驱动的多智能体角色分工架构；在此基础上提出SMART框架，包含语义引导的掩码动作编辑机制（将自然语言反馈精确落到时空动作区域，实现局部精修且不改动未编辑区域）与基于记忆的自进化策略（从交互历史中累积动作编辑知识并复用成功经验，减少冗余探索）。

Result: 大量实验表明，SMART能有效支持多轮对话式动作生成，并实现高效的动作精修。

Conclusion: 对话式、局部化、可进化的生成方式优于单次整段重生成，多智能体分工加上语义定位编辑与经验记忆复用是可行且有效的技术路线。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress in text-to-motion generation, existing methods are commonly designed for single-shot generation. When users are dissatisfied with specific motion details, they are often forced to regenerate the entire sequence from scratch, which is computationally inefficient and potentially destroys previously established kinematic context. A practical generation system should instead support iterative user feedback and refine only the affected spatio-temporal regions while preserving the remaining motion. Therefore, we introduce a conversational motion generation paradigm that requires the system to coordinate instruction understanding, motion generation, localized editing, and quality assessment. Recent advances in LLM-driven multi-agent systems provide a promising foundation for such role-specialized coordination. To tackle these challenges, a Self-evolving Multi-Agent RefinemenT Framework (SMART) is proposed for conversational motion generation. Within SMART, to preserve semantic consistency, we design a semantic-guided masked motion editing mechanism, which precisely grounds natural language feedback into spatiotemporal motion regions to enable localized refinements without altering unedited regions. To reduce redundant exploration, we propose a memory-based self-evolving strategy that dynamically accumulates motion editing knowledge from interaction history and reuses past successful experience. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SMART effectively supports multi-turn conversational motion generation and enables efficient motion refinement.

</details>


### [107] [Region-Guided Food-Package Evidence Extraction and Abstention-Aware Draft R3337 Screening with Selective VLM Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28211)
*Mayimunah Nagayi,Alice Scaria Khan,Tamryn Frank,Rina Swart,Clement Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种区域引导的证据抽取与"可弃权"筛查流程，将南非R3337草案警示准则应用于食品包装照片；选择性VLM复核在保持高准确率的前提下把覆盖率从51.7%提升到65.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将南非R3337草案警示准则（按营养、配料、产品形态判定）直接应用于食品包装照片，需要可靠的多模态证据；而在图像证据不完整、不确定或相互冲突时，强行给出自动结论会带来误判风险，因此需要可弃权、可复核的筛查方法。

Method: 构建区域引导的证据抽取流程：三分类检测器定位营养成分表、配料表与产品标识区域，再用PaddleOCR和结构化抽取获取营养数值、配料证据与产品形态信息；汇总后用确定性的R3337规则判定。证据不全或冲突时弃权为Review或Insufficient evidence；对符合条件的未决样本，引入Qwen3-VL 8B Instruct进行选择性VLM复核，合并接受证据后重新应用同一确定性规则。数据为442个产品、3,929张图像，并以65个产品的人工验证集评估。

Result: 选择性VLM复核前，60个可判定产品中31个获得自动结论、30个正确，覆盖率51.7%，选择性准确率96.8%；VLM复核额外解决8个产品，覆盖率升至65.0%，39个自动结论中38个正确（97.4%）。唯一的误报来自纯规则流程，复核后未出现危险的漏报；但证据级评估发现VLM恢复的营养、配料与产品形态证据存在错误。

Conclusion: 选择性VLM复核能有效恢复未决证据并提升覆盖率，但确定性规则筛查与显式弃权机制仍是图像型R3337筛查可靠性的必要保障；证据级错误表明仅看产品级结果不足，需进一步验证证据质量。

Abstract: Applying South Africa's draft R3337 warning criteria to food-package photographs requires nutrition, ingredient and product-form evidence. This study presents a region-guided evidence extraction and abstention-aware screening workflow for draft R3337. A three-class detector localises nutrition information panels, ingredient lists and product-identity regions, after which PaddleOCR and structured extraction recover nutrient values, ingredient evidence and product-form information. Recovered evidence is aggregated and assessed using deterministic draft R3337 rules. When evidence is incomplete, uncertain or conflicting, the workflow abstains through Review or Insufficient evidence rather than forcing an automatic outcome. For qualifying unresolved cases, a selective vision-language model (VLM) review stage using Qwen3-VL 8B Instruct reassesses unresolved evidence. Accepted evidence is merged and the same deterministic rules are reapplied. The workflow was applied to 442 products represented by 3,929 images. Evaluation used a manual validation set of 65 products. Before selective VLM review, 31 of 60 decidable products received automatic outcomes and 30 were correct, giving 51.7% coverage and 96.8% accuracy among automatic outcomes (selective accuracy). Selective VLM review resolved eight additional products, increasing coverage to 65.0%. Of 39 automatic outcomes, 38 were correct, giving 97.4% selective accuracy. The single false warning was retained from the rules-only workflow, while no unsafe warning omissions occurred after selective review. Evidence-level evaluation nevertheless identified incorrect VLM-recovered nutrition, ingredient and product-form evidence. These findings show that selective VLM review can recover unresolved evidence, while deterministic screening and explicit abstention remain necessary for reliable image-based draft R3337 screening.

</details>


### [108] [Convergent Performance, Divergent Mechanisms in LoRA-Adapted Vision Foundation Models for Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28255)
*Cecilia Diana-Albelda,Fiona Kekwick,Raghav Mehta,Aleksandra Kulbaka,Paul M. Matthews,Jesus Bescos,Álvaro García-Martín,Wenjia Bai,Bernhard Kainz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探究"性能相同是否意味着内部表征变化相同"：四个 ViT-B/16 视觉基础模型用 LoRA 适配脑肿瘤分割后 Dice 相近（78–81%），但内部动态与跨任务迁移差异显著；LoRA 仅施加于早中期层可匹配全层分割性能并缓解分类退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前将视觉基础模型通过 LoRA 适配医学影像已成常规做法，但模型选择几乎只看下游任务指标，把性能相近的骨干网络视为可互换。作者由此提出疑问：性能相同是否意味着内部表征漂移也相同。

Method: 选取四个 ViT-B/16 骨干（DINOv3、SigLIP2、MerMED-FM、BiomedCLIP），覆盖不同预训练目标（自蒸馏 vs. 对比式视觉-语言）与不同数据域（自然图像 vs. 医学图像）。各自用 LoRA 适配脑肿瘤分割，在六个数据集上评估分割与阿尔茨海默病分类；采用注意力散度、权重变化分析与激活修补（activation patching）进行机制层面分析。

Result: 四个模型收敛到相近的分割性能（平均 Dice 78–81%），但内部动态差异明显；跨任务迁移出现分化：分割适配提升 DINOv3 的阿尔茨海默病分类，却降低其余三个骨干的分类表现。发现修改幅度与因果重要性存在解离——被修改最多的层并非因果相关层，早中期层即足以恢复分割性能。

Conclusion: 将 LoRA 限制在早中期层，可在分割性能上匹配或接近全层适配，同时减少三个受影响骨干的分类退化，提供了一种有机制依据、更参数高效且更稳健的适配策略；也说明同等性能不应被视为可互换。

Abstract: Adapting Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) to medical imaging via LoRA has become standard practice, yet model selection is guided almost entirely by downstream task metrics, treating backbones with similar performance as interchangeable. We investigate whether equal performance implies equal internal representational shift. We analyse four ViT-B/16 backbones, DINOv3, SigLIP2, MerMED-FM, and BiomedCLIP, pre-trained with different objectives (self-distillation vs. contrastive vision-language) and on different data domains (natural vs. medical images). We adapt each to brain tumour segmentation and evaluate on six datasets covering both segmentation and Alzheimer's disease classification. Using attention divergence, weight-change analysis, and activation patching, we find that the four models converge to similar segmentation performance (mean Dice 78-81%) through markedly different internal dynamics, yet diverge in cross-task transfer: segmentation adaptation improves Alzheimer's disease classification for DINOv3 but degrades it for the other three backbones. We reveal a dissociation between modification magnitude and causal importance: the most heavily modified layers are not the causally relevant ones, with early-to-mid layers identified as sufficient for segmentation recovery. Restricting LoRA to these layers matches or approaches full-layer segmentation while reducing classification degradation in the three affected backbones, providing a mechanistically grounded strategy that is more parameter-efficient and more robust than standard full-layer adaptation.

</details>


### [109] [SplatFlux: Feed-Forward 4D Gaussian Reconstruction and Generation from Video Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28257)
*Runmin Zhang,Binglin Ren,Zhu Yu,Fudong Wang,Jiangke Lin,Yichun Shentu,Si-Yuan Cao,Yi Yuan,Hui-Liang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatFlux 是一个前馈式 latent-to-4DGS 框架，统一了基于视频的 4D 重建与条件式 4D 生成，通过静态-动态解耦的 4D 高斯表示实现紧凑、时序一致的 4D 场景构建，并在 3D/4D 重建与生成任务上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文观察到观测视频与条件生成内容可以被表示在同一个预训练视频隐空间中，因此这两类任务都能统一表述为"隐变量到 4DGS 的解码"问题；同时现有方法在构建紧凑且时序连贯的 4D 场景、以及分离静态结构与动态区域时仍存在困难，且缺乏低成本标注。

Method: 提出 SplatFlux 前馈式 latent-to-4DGS 框架。核心是静态-动态解耦的 4D 高斯表示，显式预测高斯基元的动态概率，将静态场景结构与运动区域分离，并通过静态聚合与动态运动插值以��馈方式构建完整 4D 场景；此外设计辅助蒸馏策略，提供伪动态掩码与光流线索，从而在无需昂贵标注的情况下学习静态-动态分解与动态高斯运动。

Result: 在 3D 与 4D 重建及生成任务上开展了大量实验，SplatFlux 均取得当前最优（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: 共享视频隐空间 + 解耦式 4D 高斯表示 + 辅助蒸馏，是一个可行且高效的统一 4D 重建与生成范式。

Abstract: This work presents SplatFlux, a feed-forward latent-to-4DGS framework for both video-based 4D reconstruction and conditional 4D generation. Our key observation is that observed videos and conditionally generated content can be represented in the same pretrained video latent space, allowing both tasks to be formulated as latent-to-4DGS decoding. To construct compact and temporally coherent 4D scenes, SplatFlux introduces a static-dynamic decoupled 4D Gaussian representation that explicitly predicts dynamic probabilities for Gaussian primitives. This representation separates static scene structures from moving regions, enabling complete 4D scene construction in a feed-forward manner through static aggregation and dynamic motion interpolation. To learn static-dynamic decomposition and dynamic Gaussian motion without costly annotations, we further develop an auxiliary distillation strategy that provides pseudo dynamic masks and optical-flow cues. Extensive experiments across 3D and 4D reconstruction and generation demonstrate that SplatFlux achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [110] [Reliability-Aware Reconstruction of V-Shaped Surfaces with a Low-Cost Multi-Zone SPAD Sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28258)
*Yohanssen Pratama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向V形表面的可靠性感知深度重建框架，融合两种TMF8821采集配置，并学习逐点可靠性用于图优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本多区域SPAD传感器虽能提供紧凑的飞行时间测量，但采样稀疏，且在几何不连续处深度估计不可靠。

Method: 采用两种互补的TMF8821采集配置；基于传感器置信度、瞬态峰值幅度与峰值锐度学习逐点可靠性，并将其引入基于图的深度优化；以稳健双平面拟合与垂直截面评估重建几何。

Result: 在60°、90°、120°目标上，平均V角误差由11.21°降至9.66°（13.8%）；代表性90°目标的点对面RMSE由14.56 mm降至13.65 mm。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效改善低成本SPAD传感器在V形不连续表面上的重建精度与可靠性，但整体误差仍有进一步降低空间。

Abstract: Low-cost multi-zone single-photon avalanche diode (SPAD) sensors provide compact time-of-flight measurements but are limited by sparse sampling and unreliable depth estimates near geometric discontinuities. This letter presents a reliability-aware reconstruction framework for V-shaped surfaces using two complementary TMF8821 acquisition configurations. Point-wise reliability is learned from sensor confidence, transient peak amplitude, and peak sharpness, and is incorporated into a graph-based depth optimization. The reconstructed geometry is evaluated by robust two-plane fitting and a perpendicular cross-section. Experiments on 60-degree, 90-degree, and 120-degree targets reduce the mean V-angle error from 11.21 degrees to 9.66 degrees (13.8%). For the representative 90-degree target, the combined point-to-plane RMSE decreases from 14.56 mm to 13.65 mm.

</details>


### [111] [PI-MLLM: Psychophysics-Inspired Multimodal Large Language Model for Oddly Satisfying Video Emotion Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28264)
*Yuheng Zhang,Jie Zhang,Yuecong Min,Yunwei Zhao,Meichen Liu,Qiankun Li,Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含12,000个5秒片段的Oddly Satisfying Video Dataset（OSVD，提供视频、音频、视听三种条件下的情绪标签及感官证据解释），并提出心理物理学启发的多模态大模型PI-MLLM，通过模态特定动态对齐（MSDA）与联盟式视听归因（CAVA）提升情绪预测与解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Oddly Satisfying Videos（OSV）依靠重复运动、视觉触感与类ASMR声音引发强烈观感反应，但缺乏显式语义情绪线索；现有视频情绪分析（VEA）方法主要关注语义内容，对塑造观众反应的物理感官动态建模不足，因此需要专门的OSV情绪分析（OSVEA）设定与数据集。

Method: 1）构建OSVD数据集：12,000个标注5秒片段，每个片段在纯视频、纯音频、视听三种条件下给出观众情绪标签，并附基于视觉与声学感官证据的解释。2）提出PI-MLLM：包含Modality-Specific Dynamics Alignment（MSDA），用辅助预测目标将各模态表征与视觉运动、声学能量起始动态对齐；以及Coalition-Based Audio-Visual Attribution（CAVA），在各模态联盟上评估情绪分数并用Shapley归因识别主导模态、判断视听证据是协同还是冲突。

Result: 实验表明PI-MLLM在观众侧情绪预测与感官依据解释质量两方面均有提升，说明物理感官线索对视频情绪分析具有价值。

Conclusion: OSVEA是一个值得独立研究的任务；显式建模视觉运动与声学动态并对视听证据进行归因，可有效提升OSV情绪预测与可解释性，表明物理感官线索是超越语义内容的重要情绪信息源。

Abstract: Oddly Satisfying Videos (OSVs) can evoke strong viewer responses through repetitive motion, visual tactility, and ASMR-like sounds despite containing few explicit semantic emotion cues. This makes OSVs a challenging setting for Video Emotion Analysis (VEA), since most existing methods focus on semantic video content and under-model the physical sensory dynamics that shape viewer responses. To support Oddly Satisfying Video Emotion Analysis (OSVEA), we construct the Oddly Satisfying Video Dataset (OSVD), comprising 12,000 annotated 5-second clips. Each clip provides viewer-emotion labels under video-only, audio-only, and audio-visual conditions, together with explanations grounded in visual and acoustic sensory evidence. Building on OSVD, we propose PI-MLLM, a psychophysics-inspired multimodal large language model that integrates Modality-Specific Dynamics Alignment (MSDA) and Coalition-Based Audio-Visual Attribution (CAVA). MSDA uses auxiliary prediction objectives to align modality representations with visual motion and acoustic energy-onset dynamics. CAVA evaluates emotion scores across modality coalitions and applies Shapley attribution to identify the dominant modality and determine whether visual and auditory evidence is synergistic or conflicting. Experiments show that PI-MLLM improves both viewer-side emotion prediction and sensory-grounded explanation quality, suggesting the value of physical sensory cues to video emotion analysis.

</details>


### [112] [VGL-stGen: Learning a shared multimodal model for lung histology and spatial transcriptomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28266)
*Haenara Shin,Dongjoo Lee,Sungwoo Bae,Kwon Joong Na,Daeseung Lee,Hongyoon Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VGL-stGen：将预训练医学视觉-语言模型适配为多模态大模型，利用配对肺H&E与空间转录组（含单细胞参考）联合训练，一个检查点支持13项任务（组织/细胞类型分类、基因预测、条件表达补全）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接组织形态学与分子状态：现有方法难以在同一生成式框架下同时完成分类、基因预测与表达水平补全，缺少将转录组细胞类型监督迁移到组织学图像的手段。

Method: 以预训练医学视觉-语言模型为骨干；训练融合图像—表达对与表达—细胞类型对；用基因条件伪标签把转录组细胞类型监督迁移到H&E图像；任务特定解码输出类别标签、基因列表与结构化表达水平；条件补全时固定查询基因身份并自回归预测其表达水平；对照实验考察仅对所需表达水平token施加监督的图像条件补全效果；并通过图像置换实验检验配对组织学的额外信息贡献。

Result: 单一部署检查点完成13项评测任务；自回归固定基因身份并预测水平提升结构化预测；限制监督到所需表达水平token可进一步改善图像条件补全；图像置换实验显示正确配对的H&E在已观测表达之外提供额外信息。

Conclusion: 在所述条件下（外部分类部分依赖共享注释谱系的计算参考、部分H&E来源在训练或模型开发中已暴露），VGL-stGen表明医学视觉-语言骨干可通过共享生成式建模从空间转录组与组织学中学习，连接组织形态与分子状态。

Abstract: VGL-stGen is a multimodal large language model adapted from a pretrained medical vision-language model using paired lung H&E and spatial transcriptomics with single-cell references. Training integrates image-expression and expression-cell-type pairs, and gene-conditioned pseudo-labels transfer transcriptomic cell-type supervision to histology images. One deployed checkpoint supports thirteen evaluated tasks spanning tissue and cell-type classification, gene prediction and conditional expression completion from combinations of histology, cell identity and observed gene expression. Task-specific decoding produces categorical labels, gene lists and structured expression levels. For conditional completion, fixing queried gene identities and predicting their levels autoregressively improves structured prediction. Controlled adaptation shows that restricting supervision to requested expression-level tokens can further improve image-conditioned completion. Image-permutation experiments indicate that correctly paired histology adds information beyond observed expression. External classification relies partly on computational references with shared annotation lineage, and some H&E sources were exposed during cancer training or model development. Within these conditions, VGL-stGen demonstrates how a medical vision-language backbone can learn from spatial transcriptomics and histology to connect tissue morphology and molecular states through shared generative modeling.

</details>


### [113] [HRoPE: Homography Relative Positional Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28284)
*Huachen Gao,Yang Yang,Xiaoqian Liang,Xingyu Chen,Youjia Zhang,Qi Zhang,Baorui Ma,Anpei Chen,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HRoPE将每个patch的局部几何表示为倾斜3D平面，通过平面诱导的单应变换作为相对位置编码注入注意力，替代以传感器/相机为中心的几何位置编码，在新视角合成任务上取得更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视角Transformer的几何相对位置编码以相机级视锥或光线步进深度为中心，只描述token"从何处被观测"，而未刻画token所描绘的物理表面区域的几何结构。

Method: 提出表面感知的位置编码：借鉴经典多视角立体中的倾斜支撑窗假设，将每个patch的局部几何表示为倾斜3D平面；把token级、由平面诱导的单应变换作为相对变换注入注意力。无需真值几何——平面参数可在纯视图合成目标下端到端预测，或由基础模型导出。

Result: 在RealEstate10K、CO3Dv2和DL3DV上，最佳HRoPE变体在新视角合成任务中一致优于所评估的各几何位置编码；消融与分析验证了编码的有效性。

Conclusion: 从传感器中心转向表面感知的几何位置编码，可为Transformer提供校正透视畸变、跨视角对齐patch特征的几何先验，是一种有效且易集成的方案。

Abstract: Geometric Relative Positional Encoding has become the primary mechanism for injecting spatial inductive biases into multi-view transformers. Existing designs condition attention on camera-level frustums or ray-marched depth, associating cross-view tokens through the sensor geometry. Such sensor-centric formulations, however, describe only where a token is observed from, leaving the rich surface geometry of the scene region depicted by the token. In this paper, we advocate a shift toward surface-aware positional encoding that explicitly grounds patch tokens in the geometry of physical surfaces. We introduce HRoPE, which represents the local geometry of each patch as a slanted 3D plane, following the slanted support-window assumption of classical multi-view stereo. We inject token-level, plane-induced homographies into attention as relative transformations. This equips the transformer with a geometric prior for rectifying perspective distortions and aligning patch features across viewpoints. Our encoding requires no ground-truth geometry: the plane parameters can be predicted end-to-end under a view-synthesis objective alone, or derived from foundation models. Extensive experiments on RealEstate10K, CO3Dv2, and DL3DV show that our best variant of HRoPE consistently outperforms the evaluated geometric positional encodings for novel view synthesis. Extensive ablations and analysis show the effectiveness of the proposed encoding. Please refer to our project page at: https://gaohchen.github.io/HRoPE

</details>


### [114] [CPVB-RG: Cross-Prior Visual Bridging for Prior-Aware Chest X-Ray Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28289)
*Sike Xiang,Shuang Chen,Julie Cox,Amir Atapour-Abarghouei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CPVB-RG——一种"先验感知"的胸部X光报告生成框架，通过Cross-Prior Visual Bridge（含PGFA与两个交叉注意力单元）区分"间隔变化"与"稳定解剖"，并在MIMIC-CXR与IU X-Ray上取得语言与临床指标的一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科医生阅读随访胸片时，会对比当前与既往影像以识别新发、吸收或进展的病灶。然而多数报告生成系统要么忽略这种纵向上下文，要么直接融合既往特征，却不区分"间期变化"与"长期稳定的解剖结构"，导致模型对不变区域产生冗余响应。

Method: 框架将当前影像、既往影像与可用临床文本联合建模。核心模块CPVB包含：PGFA（Progression-Guided Focus Attention），将"进展差异注意力"与"共享空间注意力"相对比，在抑制不变响应的同时保留检查间的粗粒度空间对应；随后由两个交叉注意力单元把纵向视觉证据与临床语义连接。训练分两阶段：先做先验引导的图像—文本对齐，再用轻量语言解码器进行自回归报告生成。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR与IU X-Ray上，语言指标与临床指标均一致提升。MIMIC-CXR上达到0.2847 F1RadGraph与0.6692 VLScore，相对解码器规模匹配的最近先验感知基线具有统计显著优势。

Conclusion: 结果表明，差异感知与空间引导的融合设计对先验感知的胸片报告生成是有效的。

Abstract: Radiologists interpret follow-up chest X-rays by comparing current and prior examinations to identify new, resolved or progressing findings. Most report generation systems either omit this longitudinal context or fuse prior features without distinguishing interval changes from anatomy that remains stable over time. We propose CPVB-RG, a prior-aware framework that combines current and prior radiographs with available clinical text. Its Cross-Prior Visual Bridge (CPVB) contains Progression-Guided Focus Attention (PGFA) and two Cross-Attention units. PGFA is designed to contrast progression-difference attention with shared-spatial attention, aiming to reduce invariant responses whilst retaining coarse spatial correspondence between examinations. The Cross-Attention units then connect the resulting longitudinal visual evidence with clinical semantics. CPVB-RG is trained in two stages, using prior-guided image-text alignment followed by autoregressive report generation with a lightweight language decoder. Experiments on MIMIC-CXR and IU X-Ray show consistent improvements across language and clinical metrics. On MIMIC-CXR, CPVB-RG achieves 0.2847 F1RadGraph and 0.6692 VLScore, with statistically significant gains over the closest decoder-matched prior-aware baseline. These results support the effectiveness of the proposed difference-aware and spatially guided fusion design for prior-aware chest X-ray report generation.

</details>


### [115] [AGGR-Mamba: Mamba-based Network with Attention-Guided Gated Residual Refinement for Undersampled MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28291)
*Yue Zhuo,Wenbin Deng,Lingjun Liu,Xuhang Chen,Zhonghua Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AGGR-Mamba，一种基于 Mamba 骨干的轻量级输出端残差精修方法，用于欠采样脑部 MRI 重建，通过注意力精修头与门控残差分支修正残留伪影与局部结构误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠采样 MRI 因频域测量缺失，常产生残余混叠伪影、边缘模糊及局部结构细节丢失；作者希望在不大幅扰动骨干重建结果的前提下，进一步修正这些局部误差。

Method: 以 Mamba 骨干先产生初始重建，再接入输出端精修模块：一是 Attention Refine Head，通过通道与空间注意力重新校准局部解剖结构、边缘纹理与残差误差区域；二是 GDFN 风格的门控残差精修分支，选择性保留有信息的残差特征并抑制冗余或不稳定响应；二者结合以受控残差方式进行修正。

Result: 在 IXI 脑部 MRI 数据集的 T1、T2 加权图像上，于加速因子 R=4 与 R=8 条件下实验，AGGR-Mamba 在不同重建设置下取得具有竞争力的 PSNR 与 SSIM 表现。

Conclusion: 结果表明输出端残差精修策略有效，可在保持骨干重建稳定的同时改善局部结构恢复；该方法定位为轻量级后处理精修模块。

Abstract: Undersampled magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) reconstruction aims to recover high-quality images from partially acquired k-space data, thereby reducing scan time. However, missing frequency-domain measurements often lead to residual aliasing artifacts, blurred edges, and loss of local structural details. In this paper, we propose AGGR-Mamba, a lightweight output-stage residual refinement method built upon a Mamba backbone for undersampled brain MRI reconstruction. The Mamba backbone first produces an initial reconstruction, and the proposed refinement modules then correct the remaining local errors and high-frequency artifacts in a controlled residual manner. Specifically, an Attention Refine Head is introduced to recalibrate local anatomical structures, edge textures, and residual-error regions through channel and spatial attention. In addition, a GDFN-style gated residual refinement branch is designed to selectively preserve informative residual features while suppressing redundant or unstable responses. By combining attention-based recalibration with gated residual selection, AGGR-Mamba improves local structure recovery without substantially disturbing the backbone reconstruction. Experiments are conducted on the IXI brain MRI dataset with T1- and T2-weighted images under acceleration factors of R=4 and R=8. The results show that AGGR-Mamba achieves competitive PSNR and SSIM performance across different reconstruction settings, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed output-stage refinement strategy.

</details>


### [116] [Towards Consistent Geospatial Representations: Aligning One Encoder to Many Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28313)
*Arthur Ouaknine,David Rolnick*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布 Pangaea-Latents（5 个 GFM 在 11 个数据集上的冻结嵌入与复现基线），发现 GFM 表示既非冗余也非互不相交，并提出 GeoMixture 框架用 mREPA 目标将 ViT 与多个 GFM 对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型（GFM）在预训练目标、模态和分辨率上差异巨大，且没有任何单一模型能在所有基准上领先。作者因此想回答两个问题：不同 GFM 的表示是否互补？单个编码器能否继承这种互补性？

Method: 构建 Pangaea-Latents：在 Pangaea 基准的 11 个数据集上提取 5 个 GFM 的冻结嵌入，并复现基线用于系统的互补性分析；在此基础上提出 GeoMixture，通过多表示对齐目标（mREPA）在逐数据集层面将一个 ViT 与多个 GFM 对齐。

Result: 嵌入分析显示 GFM 表示既非冗余也非互不相交，存在可利用的互补性；GeoMixture 编码器在九个 Pangaea 数据集上的平均排名追平最佳 GFM，以牺牲峰值性能换取跨任务一致性。

Conclusion: GFM 之间存在可被继承的互补性，单一编码器可通过多表示对齐获得跨任务稳健表现。GeoMixture 目前是初步框架，其价值在于以一致性替代单一任务上的最优表现，后续可进一步扩展。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models (GFMs) differ widely in pretraining objective, modality and resolution, and none leads across benchmarks. We ask whether their representations are complementary, and whether a single encoder can inherit that complementarity. We introduce Pangaea-Latents, an extension of the Pangaea benchmark providing frozen embeddings of $5$ GFMs over its $11$ datasets, along with reproduced baselines, and find GFM embeddings to be neither redundant nor disjoint. We then propose GeoMixture, a preliminary framework to align a ViT to several GFMs per dataset through a multi-representation alignment (mREPA) objective. Our encoder ties the best GFM in average rank across nine Pangaea datasets, trading peak performance for cross-task consistency.

</details>


### [117] [ABACUS: A Benchmark for Image Editing Models to Assess Object Category and Counting Under Scene Conservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28316)
*Aymen Lassoued,Angelique Loesch,Romaric Audigier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ABACUS 基准与评分体系，用于准确衡量指令式图像编辑模型的“数量编辑”能力，并揭示主流编辑模型在数量控制上的普遍失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令式图像编辑模型常在“增删指定数量物体或改变物体类别”上出错，且错误随场景复杂度与目标数量上升而加剧。已有基准多用整体相似度指标或本身不擅长计数的 VLM 评判，导致数值错误被忽略或误判，编辑模型的真实计数能力从未被准确测量。

Method: 提出 ABACUS：新基准 + 评估协议 + 专用评分指标。协议为“免评委”式：不由 VLM/MLLM 打分或计数（VLM 仅用于确认少见歧义裁剪块的类别），而是基于全景点真值（panoptic ground truth，经检测器共识确认）做实例级校验，在 add/remove/change 三类操作上同时检查“是否达到精确请求数量”和“场景其余部分是否保持不变”。

Result: 在主流图像编辑模型上的评测显示，存在大量数值编辑失败，且失败率随场景复杂度与物体数量增加而恶化；这些失败在此前基准中常被忽略或误判。

Conclusion: 数值能力应作为图像编辑模型独立且严格的评测维度；免评委、实例级的评测协议能更真实地暴露模型缺陷，为后续模型改进提供可靠度量基础。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editing models can now add, remove a specified number of objects or change their category while preserving the scene. Yet these models often fail to produce the correct number, and these failures grow sharply as scenes become more complex and the requested count increases. Current benchmarks largely miss this problem: they score edits with holistic similarity metrics or with vision-language model (VLM) judges that are themselves poor at counting, so numerical errors go unnoticed or are graded incorrectly. As a result, the true numerical ability of today's editing models has never been accurately measured. We address this issue with ABACUS, a new benchmark, evaluation protocol and dedicated scoring metric for numerical image editing. Our protocol is judge-free: no VLM or MLLM scores an edit or counts objects (a VLM only confirms the category of rare ambiguous crops). Instead, it verifies each edit at the instance level against panoptic ground truth confirmed by detector consensus, checking both that the exact requested count was achieved and that the rest of the scene was preserved, across the add, remove and change operations. By evaluating leading image editors, our method reveals substantial numerical editing failures that worsen with increasing scene complexity and object count; failures that prior benchmarks often overlooked or misjudged.

</details>


### [118] [PACE: Plan, Attend, Check, and Emit for Training-Free Response Timing in Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28323)
*Siqi Guo,Yuxia Chen,Wenbo Zhu,Yangguang Ji,Zhenxiang Jiang,Teng Zhong,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架PACE，按"计划-关注-校验-输出"四步组织流式视频理解中"何时作答"的问题，在ProactiveVideoQA与OVO-Bench Forward上平均PAUC最高提升5.9点、准确率提升6.2点，达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解不仅要知道"答什么"，还要知道"何时答"。证据随时间逐步到达，而现有基准显示"事件尚未结束就过早作答"是常见失败模式。已有方法多依赖额外训练来学习作答时机，免训练方案则多用标量门控信号，无法显式表达"答案何时才算最终确定"。

Method: 作者将分块流式误差归纳为三类模式：采样波动导致答案不稳定、答案稳定但缺乏已观测视频的证据支撑、答案有当前证据支撑但事件尚未结束。据此提出PACE（Plan, Attend, Check, Emit）免训练框架：给定问题先起草计划，指明要观察什么以及何种信号代表完成；随视频流推进，在信息量高的时刻调用现成视频LLM，对每份草稿分别评估稳定性、与所引用帧的时序关联性（grounding）以及完成度，并将通过验证的观察记入事件账本（event ledger）。

Result: 在ProactiveVideoQA与OVO-Bench Forward上，PACE在专有与开源骨干模型上较官方协议平均PAUC最高提升5.9点、平均准确率最高提升6.2点，取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 显式建模三类流式失败模式、并以免训练方式通过计划、稳定/关联/完成三重校验与事件账本管理作答时机，可在不额外训练的情况下显著优于既有协议，说明"何时答"可通过结构化推理流程有效解决。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires a model to decide not only what to answer but also when to answer. Evidence arrives progressively, and recent benchmarks identify premature responses, issued before the queried event has concluded, as a recurring failure mode. Existing approaches often learn response timing through additional training, while training-free alternatives typically rely on scalar gating signals that do not explicitly represent when an answer becomes final. We organize chunk-wise streaming errors into three patterns: sampling variation can make an answer unstable, a stable answer may lack support in the observed video, or an answer may be supported by the current evidence while the event remains unfinished. We propose PACE (Plan, Attend, Check, and Emit), a training-free framework designed around these three failure modes. Given a question, PACE drafts a plan specifying what to observe and what will signal completion. As the stream unfolds, it invokes an off-the-shelf video LLM at informative moments, evaluates each draft for stability, grounding in its cited frames, and completion, and records verified observations in an event ledger. Experiments on ProactiveVideoQA and OVO-Bench Forward show that PACE improves over the official protocols by up to 5.9 average PAUC points and 6.2 average accuracy points across proprietary and open-source backbones, yielding state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [119] [HistoLoop: Iterative Contextual Inference with a Looped Transformer for Histology-Based Spatial Gene Expression Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28339)
*Ziang Liu,Jie Liu,Shuai Li,Yigui Feng,Qinglin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HistoLoop 是一个循环式 Transformer，把从 H&E 图像预测空间基因表达建模为多轮迭代的上下文推断：每轮先将当前 spot 状态聚合为对应分辨率的上下文 token，用共享空间 Transformer 处理，再通过交叉注意力把信息回填到持久的 spot 状态，并在更细分辨率上重建下一轮上下文，在 HEST-1K 六个任务上取得最高宏平均基因级 Pearson 相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组实验成本高、通量低，用 H&E 图像预测空间基因表达是可行的规模化替代方案。已有工作虽在空间上下文建模上有进展，但"如何由早期上下文交互更新后的 spot 状态来构建后续上下文"这一关键环节仍缺乏探索，现有方法多依赖一次性、固定的上下文构建方式。

Method: 提出 HistoLoop，一种循环（looped）Transformer 架构，将预测形式化为迭代式上下文推断。每个精化步骤包含三步：①将当前 spot 状态按对应分辨率聚合为上下文 token；②用共享的空间 Transformer 处理这些上下文 token；③通过交叉注意力为每个 spot 检索其特定的上下文信息。随后以记忆介导（memory-mediated）的更新方式将信息写入持久化的 spot 状态，这些更新后的状态又用于在下一个更细分辨率上构建上下文，形成由粗到细的循环。

Result: 在 HEST-Bench HVG50 评测协议下，覆盖 5 个器官的 6 个癌症相关任务，HistoLoop 在所比较方法中取得最高的宏平均基因级 Pearson 相关系数。消融实验验证了循环状态精化、以及基于更新后 spot 状态在渐进更细分辨率上重建上下文这两个设计的必要性。基于表达谱的聚类结果进一步表明，HistoLoop 能更好地刻画具有空间组织性的多基因表达模式。

Conclusion: 从图像预测空间表达的关键不在于单次上下文建模，而在于"状态更新—上下文重建"的闭环迭代。HistoLoop 通过循环共享参数的 Transformer 与记忆介导的状态更新，实现了从粗到细的渐进式上下文推断，在多个癌症任务上优于对比方法，为空间转录组的高性价比计算替代提供了新范式。

Abstract: Predicting spatial gene expression from H&E images offers a scalable complement to experimental spatial transcriptomics. However, despite progress in spatial context modeling, how to construct subsequent context from spot states updated by earlier contextual interactions remains underexplored. We introduce HistoLoop, a looped Transformer that formulates prediction as iterative contextual inference. At each refinement step, HistoLoop aggregates the current spot states into context tokens at the corresponding resolution, processes them with a shared spatial Transformer, and retrieves spot-specific contextual information through cross-attention. A memory-mediated update integrates this information into persistent spot states, which are then used to construct context at the next, finer resolution. Under the HEST-Bench HVG50 evaluation protocol, HistoLoop achieves the highest macro-averaged gene-wise Pearson correlation among the compared methods across six cancer-specific tasks spanning five organs in HEST-1K. Ablation studies support the importance of recurrent state refinement and reconstructing context from updated spot states at progressively finer resolutions. Expression-derived clustering further suggests that HistoLoop better captures spatially organized multigene patterns.

</details>


### [120] [Auto-Director: Leveraging Multi-Agent Collaboration for Autonomous Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28346)
*Yuxia Chen,Wenbo Zhu,Yangguang Ji,Zhenxiang Jiang,Teng Zhong,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出工具增强的多智能体框架 Auto-Director，模拟专业后期制作流程（规划-素材-剪辑-评审）实现开放世界资源下的自主视频剪辑，并构建 VEdit-Bench 基准与五维评测体系，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界资源下的自主视频剪辑仍是难题：检索式方法受限于固定素材、缺乏创作灵活性；生成式模型难以保持长时程一致性且常偏离用户意图；两者都无法复现专业后期制作中结构化、多阶段的推理过程。

Method: 提出 Auto-Director 多智能体框架：Planning Agent 通过检索增强规划将抽象主题分解为有事实依据的脚本；Material Agent 通过镜头分割与视觉打标索引开放世界素材；Editor Agent 借助 MLLM 驱动的空间适配组装语义对齐的片段；Critic Agent 通过迭代的 Reflect-and-Revise 循环检查场景级对齐与节奏。同时构建 VEdit-Bench：100 个专家挑选主题、人工标注 Golden Scripts，以及覆盖叙事逻辑、事实准确性、语义对齐、构图质量、风格一致性五个维度的评测指标。

Result: 大量实验表明 Auto-Director 在自动指标与人工评估上均显著优于基线方法，有效缩小了业余创作与专业级视频制作之间的差距。

Conclusion: 将专业后期流程结构化为多智能体协作与工具调用，是开放世界自主视频剪辑的可行范式；配套基准与多维指标为这一新范式提供了严格评测基础。

Abstract: Autonomous video editing from open-world resources remains a challenging problem in multimedia content creation. Retrieval-based methods are constrained by fixed source material and lack creative flexibility, while generative models struggle with long-horizon coherence and often diverge from user intent. Both paradigms fail to replicate the structured, multi-stage reasoning that underlies professional post-production. To address these limitations, we present Auto-Director, a tool-augmented multi-agent framework that mirrors professional editorial workflows: a Planning Agent decomposes abstract topics into fact-grounded scripts via retrieval-augmented planning; a Material Agent indexes open-world footage through shot segmentation and visual tagging; an Editor Agent assembles semantically aligned clips with MLLM-driven spatial adaptation; and a Critic Agent governs an iterative Reflect-and-Revise loop that checks scene-level alignment and pacing. To rigorously evaluate this new paradigm, we introduce VEdit-Bench, a benchmark of 100 expert-curated topics with human-annotated Golden Scripts and a five-dimensional metric suite covering Narrative Logic, Factual Accuracy, Semantic Alignment, Compositional Quality, and Stylistic Cohesion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Auto-Director significantly outperforms baseline methods in both automated metrics and human evaluation, effectively bridging the gap between amateurs and professional-grade video production.

</details>


### [121] [Gated Prototype Transfer for Zero-Support Medical Vision Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28350)
*Sumaiya Fazal,Rizwan Qureshi,Laleh Behjat,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GProtoT：一种无需训练、无验证数据的门控式原型迁移模块，将支持类的视觉残差迁移到缺失类，用证据门控限制不可靠外推，在极端少样本医学VLM适配中提升缺失类召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实少样本医学视觉-语言模型适配中，低流行类别可能在支持集中完全缺失；SS-Text+ 虽可运行，但零支持类的原型无视觉适配，只能退化为纯文本原型。

Method: 提出 GProtoT（Gated Knowledge-Guided Prototype Transfer），作为排斥前的模块，借助 VLM 相似度与基于本体的临床距离，把有支持类的视觉残差迁移到缺失类；并设计无需验证集的“证据门”按语义相关性与残差一致性缩放迁移强度，从而抑制无支持外推。

Result: 在 MESSIDOR（FLAIR 特征）50 组配对真实单样本试验中，重度与增殖性糖尿病视网膜病变在每个支持集中均缺失。相比 SS-Text+，门控 ProtoT 使缺失类召回+5.23 点、平均类准确率+1.37 点、缺失/支持召回调和平均+1.83 点；负迁移频率由无门控的 46–48% 降至 2%，代价是支持类召回−1.20 点、top-1 准确率−0.45 点。

Conclusion: 基于证据的门控为知识源提供了安全机制；GProtoT 建于 SS-Text+ 之上，无需梯度、验证数据或额外模型推理，为不完整临床监督下的可问责适配提供低算力路径。

Abstract: Realistic few-shot adaptation of medical vision-language models (VLMs) can leave low-prevalence classes without any labeled support. The training-free SS- Text+ solver remains operational in this setting, but its zero-support prototypes receive no visual adaptation and fall back to a purely textual class prototype. We in- troduce Gated Knowledge-Guided Prototype Transfer (GProtoT), a pre-repulsion module that transfers supported-class visual residuals to missing classes using VLM similarity and an ontology-based clinical distance. A validation-free evidence gate scales transfer based on semantic relevance and residual agreement, thereby limiting unsupported extrapolation. In 50 paired realistic one-shot trials on MESSIDOR with FLAIR features, severe and proliferative diabetic retinopathy were absent from every support set. Relative to SS-Text+, gated ProtoT improved missing-class recall by 5.23 points, average class accuracy by 1.37 points, and the missing/supported recall harmonic mean by 1.83 points. It reduced negative- transfer frequency from 46-48% for ungated transfer to 2%, while at the cost of reductions of 1.20 percentage points in supported-class recall and 0.45 percentage points in top-1 accuracy. These results indicate that evidence-based gating provides a safety mechanism for the knowledge source. GProtoT is based on SS- Text+ and requires no gradients, validation data, or additional model inference, of- fering a low-compute path toward accountable adaptation under incomplete clini- cal supervision. Code is available: https://github.com/sumaiyaafridi/GProtoT

</details>


### [122] [RayCo4D: Ray-Conditioned Continuous Motion Model for Monocular 4D Dynamic Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28351)
*Yuanxiang Ni,Xianliang Huang,Ruihan Hou,Feng Xie,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RayCo4D提出一种射线条件化的连续运动模型，将视觉观测与潜在3D运动状态显式分离，用稀疏支撑时刻上的SE(3)位姿+体坐标系速度节点与白噪声-加速度先验实现连续时间插值，把长程碎片的轨迹统一约束到共享运动序列，并通过对偶四元数混合传输到高斯基元，辅以参考锚定局部SE(3)残差刻画非刚性形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视频重建动态3D场景时，观测噪声大且目标间歇可见；长程轨迹虽提供几何约束，但其时间覆盖碎片化，难以融入共享且连续的运动表示中。现有方法往往把测量与运动状态纠缠在一起，导致快速运动和轨迹不完整时结构与时序一致性下降。

Method: 每条轨迹关联一个约束在其相机射线上的参考时刻3D查询点；图像与深度观测通过局部多对多关联约束共享的潜在运动节点。每个节点在稀疏支撑时刻保持一个SE(3)位姿与体坐标系速度，用白噪声-加速度过程先验耦合相邻状态并定义连续时间插值，使不同可见区间内的观测共同约束同一潜在状态序列。推断出的共享运动经对偶四元数混合转移到高斯图元，再用参考锚定的局部SE(3)残差捕捉共享骨架之外的细尺度非刚性形变。

Result: 在多种单目动态场景实验中，RayCo4D取得有竞争力的新视角合成质量，在快速运动与轨迹不完整情形下改善了结构与时间一致性，并支持连续时间渲染。

Conclusion: 将测量与潜在运动状态解耦、并用连续时间随机过程先验桥接碎片化长程轨迹，是提升单目4D重建鲁棒性的有效途径；共享骨架加局部残差的组合兼顾了全局一致性与细粒度非刚性形变。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic 3D scenes from monocular video requires inferring motion from noisy and intermittently visible observations. Long-range tracks provide valuable geometric constraints, but integrating their fragmented temporal coverage into shared, continuous motion remains challenging. We introduce \textbf{RayCo4D}, a ray-conditioned continuous motion model for monocular 4D reconstruction that explicitly separates visual measurements from latent 3D motion states. Each track is associated with a reference-time 3D query constrained to its camera ray, and its image and depth observations constrain shared latent motion nodes through local many-to-many associations. Each node maintains an $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ pose and body-frame velocity at sparse support times. A white-noise-on-acceleration process prior couples neighboring states and defines continuous-time interpolation, allowing observations across visibility intervals to jointly constrain the same latent state sequence.The inferred shared motion is transferred to Gaussian primitives through dual-quaternion blending, while reference-anchored local $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ residuals capture fine-scale non-rigid deformation beyond the shared scaffold. Experiments on diverse monocular dynamic scenes show that RayCo4D achieves competitive novel-view synthesis quality, improves structural and temporal consistency under rapid motion and incomplete tracks, and supports continuous-time rendering.

</details>


### [123] [CountVideoBench: A Benchmark to Evaluate Prediction Biases on the Object Counting task by Video-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28356)
*Marc Chapus,Carlos Crispim-Junior,Véronique Eglin,Atilla Baskurt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有视频问答基准的真值计数分布严重偏向小数值，掩盖了多模态大模型在计数上的依数偏置。作者提出 CountVideoBench（2600 个合成视频，26 个基数，范围 [5,50]），在零样本设置下评测 16 个模型，发现无一超过 27.6% 的精确匹配，且准确率随基数变化呈非单调的"峰值-崩塌"模式，模型倾向于集中在各自偏好的整数上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频计数是多模态大语言模型一项尚未被充分刻画的能力。已有 VideoQA 基准虽含计数问题，但真值计数分布高度偏斜：所调查的九个基准中，计数中位数最多为 5，三个最高频数值合计占视频的 45%–93%。这导致平均准确率被高频计数主导，稀有计数上的依赖数值的偏置贡献可忽略、因而不可见。因此需要一个分布均衡、具有诊断性的计数基准来可靠评估这一基础视觉能力。

Method: 构建 CountVideoBench：2600 个合成视频，覆盖区间 [5,50] 内的 26 个基数（每个 100 个视频），划分为六组连续计数簇，既覆盖整个范围又支持近邻比较。每个视频只包含单一类别的静态物体，并采用受控的相机运动，从而排除物体运动并降低识别难度带来的混淆。在零样本设置下评测来自九个架构族的 16 个视频-语言模型。

Result: 没有任何模型超过 27.6% 的精确匹配（Exact Match）。准确率对基数的依赖因模型而异：从单调下降，到非单调的"峰值-崩塌"模式，这排除了"难度随基数增大而上升"作为计数失败主因的解释。此外，每个模型都将预测集中在各自偏好的一组整数上，准确率峰值与这些数值重合，而在相邻计数处急剧崩塌。

Conclusion: 上述发现表明，要可靠评估计数这一基础视觉能力，需要分布均衡、具备诊断性的计数基准；现有基准的偏斜分布会系统性掩盖模型的依赖数值的计数偏置。

Abstract: Object counting in videos remains a poorly characterized capability of multimodal large language models. Counting questions are included in many existing VideoQA benchmarks, but the ground-truth count distributions in these benchmarks are heavily skewed toward small values. Across the nine benchmarks we survey, the median ground-truth count is at most five, and the three most frequent values together account for between 45% and 93% of the videos. The resulting mean accuracy is then dominated by the most frequent counts; a value-dependent bias at a rare count contributes negligibly to the average and remains invisible. We introduce CountVideoBench, a benchmark of 2,600 synthetic videos covering 26 cardinalities in the interval [5, 50] (100 videos each), grouped into six clusters of consecutive counts that span the range while enabling near-neighbor comparisons. Each video contains a single class of static objects under a controlled camera motion, removing object motion and reducing recognition as confounds. We evaluate sixteen video-language models from nine architecture families in a zero-shot setting. No model exceeds 27.6% Exact Match. The dependence of accuracy on cardinality varies across models, ranging from monotonic decline to a non-monotonic peak-and-collapse pattern, which rules out increasing difficulty with cardinality as the dominant explanation of counting failure. Every model further concentrates its predictions on its own set of preferred integers, with accuracy peaks coinciding with these values and collapsing at adjacent counts. These findings highlight the need for balanced, diagnostic counting benchmarks to reliably assess this fundamental visual capability.

</details>


### [124] [RACE: Learning Multimodal Search through Referential Abstraction and Compositional Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28382)
*Eric Tao,Minhao Sun,Liyan Liu,Zhijing Wu,Xiaohan Yu,Lang Mei,Li Huang,Chao Feng,Jinchao Ma,Sichen Kang,Siyu Mo,Chong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RACE 框架，通过构建"图像→目标"与"目标证据→答案"的依赖关系，合成难以走捷径的多模态搜索任务，微调 Qwen3.5-27B 后在十个多模态基准上平均准确率由 53.5 提升至 56.0（相对提升约 4.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练多模态搜索智能体需要"必须依赖视觉 grounding 才能确定答案"的任务。但现有合成问题常含文本身份线索（直接泄露目标身份）和未被使用的目标证据，使模型可以走捷径，从而削弱了对视觉搜索与证据整合能力的监督。

Method: 提出 RACE——一种以图像为条件的任务合成框架，核心是两个操作：1）指代抽象（Referential Abstraction, RA）：用具体的视觉指代或需在图中候选间搜索才能消歧的模糊关系指代，替换暴露身份的文本；2）组合证据（Compositional Evidence, CE）：将同一图像中多个实体或来自不同图像实体的检索事实联结成联合问题，答案需要每个指定信息都有贡献。两种操作亦适用于搜索过程中新发现的图像，使新的视觉观察决定后续搜索目标或提供答案证据。

Result: 仅使用 RACE 生成的 12K 条教师轨迹微调 Qwen3.5-27B，在十个多模态基准上的平均准确率从 53.5 提升到 56.0，相比匹配的基线智能体相对提升约 4.7%。

Conclusion: 通过消除文本捷径、强制视觉依赖与多源证据整合，RACE 能以较小的合成数据量有效提升多模态搜索智能体的综合性能；代码、数据与模型将公开。

Abstract: Training multimodal search agents requires tasks that make visually grounded search necessary for determining the answer. Textual identity clues and unused target evidence allow shortcuts in synthetic questions, weakening supervision for visual search and evidence integration. We propose RACE, an image-conditioned task synthesis framework that constructs dependencies from images to targets and from target-specific evidence to answers. Referential Abstraction (RA) replaces identity-revealing text with specific visual references or ambiguous relational references resolved by searching among depicted candidates. Compositional Evidence (CE) links retrieved facts about multiple entities in one image or entities from different images into joint questions whose answers require every designated contribution. Both operations also apply to images found during search, allowing new visual observations to determine subsequent search targets or supply answer evidence. Fine-tuning Qwen3.5-27B on only 12K teacher-generated trajectories from RACE raises average accuracy across ten multimodal benchmarks from 53.5 to 56.0, a relative improvement of approximately 4.7% over the matched base agent. Our code, data, and model will be publicly available.

</details>


### [125] [VisualEvolve: A Skill Self-Evolution Framework for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28386)
*Jiawei Wang,Yucun Li,Xueqi Li,Shenghao Wang,Xinquan Gong,Dan Zhang,Zheqi Lv,Yuquan Le,Shaozu Yuan,An Zhang,Da Cao,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VisualEvolve 自进化框架，通过"选择—执行—进化—评估"循环演化可执行视觉操作与工具使用策略，在24个设置中23个优于初始技能库，最高提升15.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉工具使用能帮助VLM获取任务相关证据，但其效果受限于预定义的视觉操作和训练中习得的工具使用策略；将操作与策略表示为可复用外部技能可借助自进化精炼且无需更新模型参数，但技能精炼会带来性能回退，且同时检视累积轨迹与技能演化史会造成"进化证据过载"。

Method: VisualEvolve 采用 Select–Execute–Evolve–Evaluate 循环；其中 Skill-Library Snapshot Selection 用与执行任务不相交的固定验证集表现来挑选有效历史快照，抑制性能回退传播；Progressive Evidence Access (PEA) 以由粗到细的层级（技能-性能报告、演化日志、轨迹摘要、相关原始轨迹片段）引导能力差距诊断。

Result: 在4个VLM骨干、3个基准的6个评测集上，24个设置中23个优于初始技能库，Qwen3.5-Plus 在 VisualProbe Hard 上最大提升15.1个百分点；消融实验支持 Snapshot Selection 与 PEA 的贡献；技能可借不同数据设置的经验改进并跨骨干直接复用；所需任务求解轨迹比代表性RL方法估计量少一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 以外部可复用技能配合快照选择与渐进式证据访问，可在不更新参数的前提下实现稳定、高效的VLM视觉工具使用技能自进化，并在样本效率上显著优于RL类方法。

Abstract: Visual tool use enables vision-language models (VLMs) to acquire task-relevant evidence, but its effectiveness can be limited by predefined visual operations and the tool-use strategies learned during training. Representing these operations and strategies as reusable external skills allows them to be refined through self-evolution without updating model parameters. However, skill refinement can introduce performance regressions. Meanwhile, inspecting accumulated task execution trajectories together with skill evolution history may lead to evolution evidence overload. To address these challenges, we propose VisualEvolve, a self-evolving framework that evolves executable visual operations and tool-use strategies for VLMs through a Select--Execute--Evolve--Evaluate cycle. Skill-Library Snapshot Selection uses performance on a fixed validation set disjoint from execution tasks to select an effective historical snapshot for subsequent evolution, limiting the propagation of performance regressions. Progressive Evidence Access (PEA) guides capability-gap diagnosis through a coarse-to-fine hierarchy comprising the Skill-Performance Report, Evolution Log, Trajectory Summaries, and relevant raw trajectory segments. Across four VLM backbones and six evaluation sets from three benchmarks, VisualEvolve improves over the Initial Skill Library in 23 of 24 settings, with a maximum gain of 15.1 percentage points on VisualProbe Hard using Qwen3.5-Plus. Ablation studies support the contributions of Snapshot Selection and PEA, while further experiments show that skills can be improved using experience from different data settings and directly reused across backbones. Moreover, VisualEvolve enables effective skill self-evolution with over an order of magnitude fewer task-solving trajectories than estimated for representative RL-based methods.

</details>


### [126] [StreamEval: Evaluating Interactive Streaming Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28402)
*StreamEval Team*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 StreamEval，一个覆盖五项任务（虚拟人对话、虚拟人表演、世界生成、指令编辑、参考编辑）的交互式流式视频生成统一基准，包含 300 个时长 15–300 秒的脚本化测试用例，从任务执行、状态一致性、视觉保真度、视听协调四个维度构建十项指标，并对 37 个模型进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频生成快速发展，实时交互能力日益重要，但缺乏统一评测手段来衡量模型在增量到达的多模态请求、时序约束与内容保持要求下的真实表现。

Method: 构建 StreamEval 基准：300 个脚本化测试用例，每个用例指定初始素材、增量到达的多模态请求以及对状态变化与内容保持的明确要求；设计四维评测框架（任务执行、状态一致性、视觉保真度、视听协调），细化为十项任务专属指标，采用 0–100 打分，并在 300 个用例上评测 37 个不同模型。

Result: 高视觉质量与身份保持并不必然伴随可靠的请求执行或持续的状态一致性。请求执行尤为困难：七项虚拟人表演模型中 Following 最高仅 36.90，文本条件世界生成的 Following 最高 25.93，三项参考编辑模型的 Edit Success 仅为 15.00。

Conclusion: 在时序与内容保持约束下实现可靠的请求执行，是推进全模态实时交互系统的核心挑战；StreamEval 提供了统一、可复现的评测平台（榜单见 https://streameval.ai）。

Abstract: As streaming video generation advances, enabling real-time interaction with generated video is becoming increasingly important. We introduce StreamEval, a unified benchmark for interactive streaming video generation spanning five tasks: avatar dialogue, avatar performance, world generation, instruction editing, and reference editing. StreamEval comprises 300 scripted test cases lasting 15-300s, each specifying initial assets, incrementally arriving multimodal requests, and explicit requirements for state changes and content preservation. Performance is assessed along four evaluation dimensions: task execution, state consistency, visual fidelity, and audiovisual coordination, instantiated through ten task-specific metrics on a 0-100 scale. Evaluating 37 distinct models across 300 test cases, we find that high visual quality and identity retention do not necessarily coincide with reliable request execution or sustained state consistency. In particular, request execution remains challenging: the highest task-specific scores are 36.90 for Following among seven avatar-performance models, 25.93 for Following in text-conditioned world generation, and 15.00 for Edit Success among three reported reference-editing models. These findings identify reliable request execution under timing and preservation constraints as a central challenge for advancing omni-modal real-time interactive systems. The leaderboard is available at https://streameval.ai

</details>


### [127] [A Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Machine Learning with Evidence-based Planning for Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28410)
*Yiqing Shen,Mathias Unberath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MedicalMLOmni，一个多智能体 AutoML 框架，通过自然语言接口自动化多种单模态与多模态医学影像任务的模型训练，在 20 个基准中 19 个取得最佳成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已成为医学图像分析的默认工具，但架构选择与超参数优化仍被视为"艺术而非科学"；通用 AutoML 缺乏医学专用架构与预处理，医学专用方案又依赖工程专业知识且局限于 3D 分割等狭窄任务，导致拥有丰富数据但工程能力有限的领域专家难以获得最优性能。

Method: MedicalMLOmni 采用多智能体 AutoML 设计：一是基于证据的规划，检索已发表的实验结果来指导模型选择与配置；二是并行超参数优化，多个智能体同时探索不同的调参策略；整体通过自然语言接口驱动。

Result: 在 20 个基准中 19 个取得最佳分数，唯一例外是 PMC-VQA，该任务上预训练更充分的视觉-语言模型领先。采用主干网络匹配的对照实验表明性能提升来自框架本身而非主干网络。

Conclusion: MedicalMLOmni 以消除编码门槛的方式降低了领域专家的使用壁垒，使其无需大量工程投入即可在多样化医学任务上获得高性能模型。

Abstract: Deep learning has become the default tool for medical image analysis, driven by the open-source movement. However, achieving the best performance on a given task remains difficult, with architecture selection and hyperparameter optimization (HPO) still considered "more of an art than a science". Automated machine learning (AutoML) automates parts of model training, yet helps little in medical imaging: general-purpose approaches lack medical architectures and preprocessing, and medical-focused ones demand engineering expertise and remain confined to narrow tasks such as 3D segmentation. We introduce MedicalMLOmni, a multi-agent AutoML framework that automates model training for diverse single- and multi-modal medical tasks through a natural language interface. Specifically, MedicalMLOmni performs evidence-based planning by retrieving published results to guide model selection and configuration, and employs parallel HPO where multiple agents explore adjustment strategies concurrently. MedicalMLOmni achieves the best score on 19 of 20 benchmarks; PMC-VQA is the exception, where a vision-language model with broader pre-training leads. Backbone-matched controls attribute the gains to the framework, not the backbone. By removing coding requirements, MedicalMLOmni lowers the barrier for domain experts with rich datasets but limited engineering expertise.

</details>


### [128] [Brain2Vec: A Self-Supervised 3D Foundation Model for Structural Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28411)
*Spiros Maggioros,Guray Erus,Gareth Harman,George Aidinis,Alexander Getka,Aristeidis Sotiras,Christos Davatzikos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出脑MRI基础模型Brain2Vec，基于74,425张T1加权MRI预训练，可作微调骨干或冻结特征提取器；在脑龄预测上提升精度并加速收敛，在5个下游分类任务中4个优于解剖体积特征，并具可解释的空间定位表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像深度学习端到端训练受标注数据有限与算力约束限制，需要可复用的预训练基础模型来缓解数据与计算瓶颈。

Method: 汇集多项研究的74,425张T1加权MRI进行自监督预训练得到Brain2Vec；从三条路径评估：下游预测性能、收敛速度、嵌入质量；对比随机初始化、解剖学体积特征及更大数据预训练的SOTA基础模型；并通过表征分析与潜变量操控检验可解释性。

Result: 以预训练权重初始化并微调后，脑龄预测最终精度更高且收敛更快；冻结嵌入在5个下游分类任务中4个优于体积特征，并与数据量更大的SOTA基础模型表现相当；模型主要编码空间局部化且具神经解剖意义的脑区信息，操纵部分潜特征可产生单调、区域特异的解剖重建变化。

Conclusion: Brain2Vec是数据高效、可解释且易用的脑影像基础模型，支持微调与免训练特征提取，并已开源及通过NiChart云平台提供，降低使用门槛。

Abstract: Deep learning models have become essential tools in medical imaging, yet training them end-to-end for diverse tasks remains challenging due to limited labeled data and computational constraints. To address this, we introduce Brain2Vec, a foundation model pre-trained on 74,425 T1-weighted Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) scans pooled from multiple studies. Brain2Vec can serve both as a weight initialization backbone for fine-tuning downstream tasks and as a standalone feature extractor. We evaluated Brain2Vec in three tasks: downstream predictive performance, convergence speed, and embedding quality. Using Brain2Vec's pretrained weights as initialization and fine-tuning on labeled data for a brain age prediction task, results demonstrate improved final accuracy and faster convergence compared to random initialization. Additionally, its frozen embeddings outperform anatomically defined volumetric features on four of five downstream classification tasks and perform competitively with a state-of-the-art foundation model pre-trained on substantially more data. Lastly, we show that Brain2Vec encodes information primarily from a spatially localized and neuroanatomically meaningful set of brain regions, and that manipulating a subset of latent features produces monotonic, region-specific effects on the reconstructed anatomy. Brain2Vec code and trained models are available as an open-source package on https://github.com/CBICA/Brain2Vec. Users can apply Brain2Vec locally by installing the package, or directly through the NiChart cloud platform https://cloud.neuroimagingchart.com, where Brain2Vec features can be derived without requiring local installation or specialized infrastructure.

</details>


### [129] [Code2Video-Compass: A Comprehensive Benchmark for General-Purpose Code-Based Video Generation across T2V, I2V, and V2V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28416)
*Zhenghan Chen,Zekai Shao,Xin Lin,Leting Cheng,Siming Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Code2Video-Compass，一个面向代码式视频生成的通用基准，覆盖 T2V、I2V、V2V 三类输入，包含 2,104 个任务、10 大类 24 子类及三个复杂度维度，并设计客观（12 项）+主观（5 项）双层评测框架，评测 15 个多模态大模型后发现领导模型仍难稳定生成高质量完整视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码式视频生成相比直接视频生成具有细粒度控制、定向编辑和组件复用优势，但需要多模态大模型把视觉与时间需求转化为可执行程序。现有代码式基准多聚焦专用任务，缺乏对跨创意类别、跨输入模态的"完整视频创作"的系统评测。

Method: 构建基准 Code2Video-Compass：含 2,104 个任务，覆盖 10 类、24 子类，以及构造、动态动画、状态依赖三个复杂度维度；任务来源结合文本指令与视觉参考，并从专业及用户创作视频派生出对应的 T2V/I2V/V2V 任务。提出双层评测框架，客观层含 12 项指标（运行性能、视频质量、文视对齐、参考保真），主观层由 MLLM 评审评估指令遵循与整体视频质量。

Result: 对来自 9 个模型家族、15 个近期闭源与开放权重的多模态大模型进行评测，结果显示即使领导模型也难以可靠生成符合文本与视觉要求的高质量完整视频，且性能随构造、动态动画、状态依赖复杂度递增而普遍下降。

Conclusion: Code2Video-Compass 有望推动可靠、连贯、可控的代码式视频生成研究，并促进程序化视觉创作的更广泛进展。

Abstract: Code-based video generation offers fine-grained control, targeted editing, and component reuse compared with direct video generation. This approach requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to translate visual and temporal requirements into executable programs that produce complete videos. However, existing code-based benchmarks primarily target specialized tasks, leaving complete video creation across diverse creative categories and input modalities insufficiently evaluated. We introduce Code2Video-Compass, a general-purpose benchmark for code-based video generation across text-to-video (T2V), image-to-video (I2V), and video-to-video (V2V) settings. Code2Video-Compass comprises 2,104 tasks spanning 10 categories, 24 subtypes, and three complexity dimensions covering construction, dynamic animation, and state dependency. It combines tasks curated from textual instructions and visual references with corresponding T2V, I2V, and V2V tasks derived from complete professional and user-created videos. We propose a dual-level evaluation framework combining 12 objective metrics and five subjective metrics. Objective evaluation covers operational performance, video quality, text-video alignment, and reference fidelity, while subjective evaluation uses an MLLM judge to evaluate instruction following and holistic video quality. Extensive evaluation of 15 recent proprietary and open-weight MLLMs from nine families reveals that even leading models face challenges in reliably generating high-quality complete videos aligned with textual and visual requirements, with performance generally declining as construction, dynamic animation, and state-dependency complexity increases. We anticipate that Code2Video-Compass will advance reliable, coherent, and controllable code-based video generation and inspire broader progress in programmatic visual creation.

</details>


### [130] [DISTRACT: Degradation-Indexed Study of Textual Reference Across Competing Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28419)
*Dan Zlotnikov,Ohad Ben-Shahar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 DISTRACT 基准，在固定视频、目标与干扰物的条件下，交叉组合四种文本先验与四种图像退化，检验视觉-语言跟踪器（VLT）是否真正利用文本内容改变跟踪决策。行为对比、逐层表征探测与激活修补显示：Molmo2 与 JointNLT 存在有方向的文本利用，UVLTrack、MMTrack、DUTrack 对文本语义响应很弱；干预揭示了早期的"文本敏感状态"，但未能证实目标特异性的语义挽救。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言跟踪器虽接受文本描述，但"接受文本"并不等于"文本内容真的影响跟踪决策"，尤其在视觉证据退化时更不清楚文本何时、何处起作用。作者希望用一个控制严格的匹配干预实验，定量刻画文本对跟踪的实际因果贡献。

Method: 构建 DISTRACT 匹配干预基准：保持视频、目标与干扰物不变，将四种文本先验与四种图像退化按控制的严重度轴交叉组合。在四个数据集上评估五个 VLT，采用三类手段：行为对比、逐层表征探测（decodability）以及激活修补（activation patching），并配合多重比较校正（FDR）与端点显著性检验。

Result: Molmo2 与 JointNLT 表现出有方向的文本利用，部分效应随退化而收缩，另一些在多重校正后端点无显著变化。UVLTrack、MMTrack、DUTrack 对文本语义变化响应微弱，在测试设置下无有方向的行为效应通过 FDR 校正。内部表征分析显示，在接近决策的状态中，正确文本与冲突文本仅在 Molmo2 与 JointNLT 中可解码。可解码性峰值出现在后期状态，但替换后期状态几乎不改变行为；替换部分较早状态则可将冲突相关轨迹导向目标。不过匹配的正确供体并不显著优于无关供体，说明干预定位到的是"早期干预敏感状态"，尚未证明目标特异的语义挽救。

Conclusion: DISTRACT 提供了诊断视觉-语言跟踪器中文本何时、何处真正影响决策的框架，揭示了多数模型文本利用薄弱、可解码性与行为因果性错位的现象，并为后续研究指明"早期状态干预"这一方向，同时强调需进一步区分一般性敏感与目标特异性语义作用。

Abstract: Vision-language trackers (VLTs) accept descriptions intended to assist tracking, but accepting text does not establish that its content changes tracking decisions, particularly as visual evidence deteriorates. We introduce DISTRACT, a matched-intervention benchmark that holds video, target, and distractor fixed while crossing four text priors with four image degradations along a controlled severity axis. We evaluate five VLTs on four datasets using behavioral contrasts, layerwise representation probes, and activation patching. Molmo2 and JointNLT exhibit directed text use. Some effects contract under degradation, whereas others show no significant endpoint change after multiplicity correction. UVLTrack, MMTrack, and DUTrack respond only weakly to changes in text semantics, with no directed behavioral effect surviving FDR correction in the tested settings. Model-internal analyses reveal that at decision-near states, correct-versus-conflicting text is decodable in Molmo2 and JointNLT but not in the other three systems. In the responsive models, decodability peaks at late states where replacement produces little behavioral change, whereas replacing selected earlier states redirects conflict-associated trajectories toward the target. Because matched-correct donors do not significantly outperform unrelated donors, these interventions identify earlier intervention-sensitive states without establishing target-specific semantic rescue. DISTRACT thus exposes when and where text affects tracking under degraded visual evidence.

</details>


### [131] [WorldSR: Harnessing World Knowledge Search for Grounded Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28423)
*Junyuan Deng,Xinyi Wu,Zhenyao Wu,Song Wang,Yu Wang,Congchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 WorldSR 框架，用实体级外部视觉世界知识（检索到的参考图）替代/补充生成式先验，通过多参考超分模型实现更保真的超分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式先验本质上是随机的、面向多样化生成优化的，与超分所需的确定性高保真重建相冲突；且它绑定于基础模型、更新代价高，并随真实数据演进而易产生幻觉。

Method: 输入被自动分解为语义实体，逐实体从大规模外部图像库中检索结构与语义对齐的图像；引入记忆机制复用已检索结果以提升效率，并用多步过滤流程精炼候选参考；最终通过新颖的多参考超分模型将选中的参考作为"世界知识"融合，为细节合成与结构恢复提供细粒度约束。

Result: 在真实世界超分基准上，WorldSR 持续提升结构保真度与视觉质量，优于现有生成式超分方法。

Conclusion: 实体级的外部视觉世界知识接地，是模型内生成式先验的一种简单而有效的补充。

Abstract: Generative-prior-based super-resolution (SR) methods leverage implicit knowledge learned from large-scale models to enhance low-resolution inputs. However, such priors are inherently stochastic and optimized for diverse generation, conflicting with the deterministic, high-fidelity reconstruction required for SR. It is also tied to the base model, costly to update, and prone to hallucinations as real-world data evolves. To address these limitations, we introduce WorldSR, a novel framework that augments SR with explicit, entity-aligned visual world knowledge. WorldSR automatically decomposes the input into semantic entities and performs entity-wise retrieval of structurally and semantically aligned images from a large-scale external corpus. For efficiency and reliability, we incorporate a memory mechanism to reuse previously retrieved results and a multi-step filtering process to refine candidate references. The selected references as world knowledge are then integrated through a novel multi-reference super-resolution model, which provides fine-grained constraints for detail synthesis and structural recovery. Experiments on real-world SR benchmarks show that WorldSR consistently improves structural fidelity and visual quality, outperforming existing generative super-resolution methods. Our results suggest that entity-level grounding in external visual world knowledge provides a simple and effective complement to model-bound generative priors.

</details>


### [132] [EvaOmni: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Audio-Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28444)
*Xiaoxing Hu,Wenxi Yao,Chaoyuan Shi,Ziqi Ye,Xiaorong Guo,Peixuan Li,Zhuoran Sun,Ziyang Gong,Peiyuan Zhang,Mingxin Liu,Shuran Ma,Hongbo Liu,Xiaomin Yu,Yu Tian,Wentao Wang,Zhenjie Yang,Jian Hu,Yunhang Shen,Haoyu Cao,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EvaOmni 证据落地型音视频推理基准（392 个视频、1027 条人工标注问答对，覆盖 15 项能力、3 个层级），要求模型同时输出答案与证据时间区间，并给出 C.G. 与 B.G. 两项证据评分指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频推理基准多依赖选择题且仅以答案对错评分，无法判断模型是否真正建立了从证据到答案的推理链；文本或单模态捷径还可能导致"未看证据也能答对"，削弱评测可靠性。

Method: 构建 392 个视频、1,027 条纯人工标注问答对的基准，覆盖 15 项能力与检索定位、基础理解、复杂推理三个层级；采用严格标注流程：专家标注音视频证据并构造视频内干扰项，人工审核题目是否满足检验标准，最后由模型过滤文本与单模态捷径。评测时要求模型同时返回答案与支撑证据时间区间，并设计 Coverage Grounded Score（C.G.，衡量标注证据被识别的比例）与 Bottleneck Grounded Score（B.G.，惩罚任一模态的时间定位不足），二者与答案正确性结合。

Result: 在 7 个开源与 17 个专有 Omni LLM 上评测，Gemini3.7-Flash 综合表现最佳：总体准确率 79.4%、C.G. 70.8%、B.G. 35.1%；在第 3 层级准确率达 79.1%，但 B.G. 仅 12.3%。

Conclusion: 即便最先进的模型在"证据落地"的音视频推理上仍有明显提升空间，尤其复杂推理层级的跨模态时间定位能力薄弱；EvaOmni 为评估完整推理链提供了更可靠的评测方式。

Abstract: Omni LLMs can naturally locate key audio and visual evidence and integrate information across modalities for audiovisual reasoning. Existing audio-visual reasoning benchmarks that mainly rely on multiple-choice questions usually score models solely based on answer correctness, making it difficult to assess whether a model truly forms a reasoning chain from evidence to the correct answer. In addition, textual or unimodal shortcuts may allow models to answer correctly without analyzing the necessary audio-visual evidence, further weakening the reliability of evaluation benchmarks. To address these issues, we introduce EvaOmni, an evidence-grounded benchmark containing 392 videos and 1,027 purely human-annotated question-answer pairs, covering 15 capabilities and three levels: retrieval and localization, basic understanding, and complex reasoning. We design a series of rigorous annotation procedures in which experts annotate audio and visual evidence and construct video-based distractors; humans review whether questions meet strict inspection criteria, and finally models filter textual and unimodal shortcuts. Beyond high-quality question-answer pairs, we require models to simultaneously return the answer and supporting evidence intervals in order to evaluate the complete reasoning chain. We propose the Coverage Grounded Score (C.G.) to measure how much annotated evidence a model identifies, and the Bottleneck Grounded Score (B.G.) to penalize poor temporal localization in either modality; both are incorporated into answer correctness. Across 7 open-weight and 17 proprietary Omni LLMs, Gemini3.7-Flash achieved the highest overall accuracy (79.4%), C.G. (70.8%), and B.G. (35.1%). At Level 3, it reached 79.1% accuracy, but its B.G. was only 12.3%. These results show that even the most advanced models still have room for development in grounded audio-visual reasoning.

</details>


### [133] [Answering in the User's Variety: Calibrated Accommodation Pairs for Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28474)
*Zhizhuo Kou,Heming Li,Jian Yang,Junyu Luo,Jingshu Peng,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文追踪监督微调、偏好优化与强化学习三个阶段如何改变模型的"语体顺应"倾向，发现后训练会把回复标准化，但DPO更新在概率校正后仍偏向匹配英式/澳式拼写而非AAE与Singlish；提出校准顺应配对（CAP）偏好数据，可在不损害标准英语用户与指令遵循的前提下提升模型对未见方言特征的顺应度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们会调整说话方式以顺应对话对象，但聊天模型往往无论用户用何种方言都以标准英语回复。统一的答复会掩盖训练是否区别对待这些用户，仅靠输出审计也难以判断。作者希望弄清后训练各阶段如何改变模型的"顺应"倾向，以及如何让模型真正学会适度顺应而非一律标准化。

Method: 沿10个开源模型谱系，逐阶段（SFT→DPO→强化学习）追踪模型匹配用户方言的倾向；采用"内容匹配的成对回复"（一条匹配用户、一条不匹配）来读取每次更新偏好什么，并对每条回复的既有似然进行校正，以排除表面概率差异。随后构造"校准顺应配对"（CAP）偏好数据，奖励匹配用户方言密度、同时为使用标准英语的用户保留标准英语；在完全开源的模型上对比CAP与单边配对的效果。

Result: 后训练对所有非正式线索都把回复标准化；但在校正回复的固有似然后，DPO更新在10个谱系上仍更偏好匹配英式与澳式拼写，而非AAE与Singlish，且模型在被要求时仍能产出AAE特征。采用CAP训练后，模型对其未训练过的特征顺应度提升，而标准英语用户的方言使用无明显增加，指令遵循也无明显损失；单边配对则需通过"过度顺应"、强行施加方言才能取得类似增益。

Conclusion: 统一的回复会掩盖选择性的更新，审计应当同时读取更新本身而非只看输出；CAP提供了一种在不损害标准英语用户与通用能力的前提下实现校准顺应的偏好数据方案。

Abstract: People adapt how they talk to the person in front of them. However, chat models tend to respond in Standard English regardless of the dialect used by the user. A uniform answer can hide whether training treats these users alike, and output audits cannot tell as well. We trace how each post-training stage, from supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to preference optimization (DPO) and reinforcement learning, changes a model's tendency to match the user, and read what each update prefers from two content-matched replies, one matching the user and one not. Across 10 open model lineages, post-training standardizes replies to every informal cue, yet after adjusting for how likely each reply already was, the DPO update still favours matching British and Australian spelling over AAE and Singlish on 10 lineages, and models still produce AAE features when asked. We then introduce calibrated accommodation pairs (CAP), preference data that reward matching users' dialect density while preserving Standard English for Standard-English users. On a fully open model, CAP raises convergence on features it was not trained on without a detectable increase in dialect for Standard-English users or a cost in instruction following. In contrast, one-sided pairs reach a similar gain only by overshooting the user and imposing dialect. Our findings show that uniform replies can hide selective updates and audits should read the update as well as the output. Code: https://github.com/kouzhizhuo/cap-accommodation.

</details>


### [134] [CLOSER: Evidence-Aware Active Visual Verification for Embodied Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28476)
*Pengying Wu,Mingye Jiang,Yunze Hu,Zhejia Wen,Kangjie Zhou,Zongru Yang,Zhe Peng,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLOSER 提出一种"证据感知的主动验证"框架，把"停止并判定目标"重新表述为闭环证据采集过程：用微调的视觉语言模型（VLM）对候选目标的每一帧视图判定为目标/非目标/不确定，用基于嵌入的多样性门控挑选互补视图，再用轻量网络融合各视图概率；若仍不确定，则用扩散策略驱动智能体基于跟踪掩码与深度主动靠近候选目标，直到判定明确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标物体导航（ObjectNav）中，智能体只能依赖有限的自我中心视频流（甚至单帧）在线判断检测到的候选是否为指定类别。检测器常会"自信地"把相似物体认错（如把沙发当床），一旦在误检处停止则整个回合失败；同时现有系统往往贪心地直接靠近候选，导致目标被遮挡或只被部分观测，进一步削弱判断可靠性。

Method: 1）微调开源 VLM，对跨帧跟踪的候选目标的每个视图输出 target / non-target / uncertain 三分类；2）用基于嵌入的多样性门控选择互补视图，避免冗余证据；3）用轻量网络融合逐视图概率得到整体判断；4）若融合后仍不确定，则用扩散策略仅依据跟踪掩码与深度驱动机器人靠近候选，实现"先验证后提交"的闭环主动感知；5）框架本地运行，可作为插件接入现有 ObjectNav 系统而无需重新训练它们。

Result: 在 Gibson 与 Habitat-Matterport 3D（HM3D）基准上，相比基线平均成功率提升 3.6 个百分点，SPL（按路径长度加权的成功率）提升 2.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: 在作出停止决策之前先主动获取并融合多视图证据，能有效缓解相似物体误检导致的失败，提升具身导航的成功率与路径效率；该框架具有即插即用、可本地部署的实用价值。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation (ObjectNav) requires an embodied agent to find an instance of a specified category in an unseen environment and stop near it. The agent typically relies on an object detector but must judge online from a limited egocentric video stream, sometimes a single frame, whether a detected candidate is the target. Detectors often confidently mistake similar objects, such as a sofa for a bed, and stopping at a false positive fails the episode. Prevailing systems also approach candidates greedily, leaving them occluded or partially observed. We present CLOSER, an evidence-aware active verification framework that treats stopping as closed-loop evidence acquisition, coupling vision-language model (VLM) verification with active perception.
