CLOSER fine-tunes an open-source VLM to label each view of a candidate tracked across frames as target, non-target, or uncertain, selects complementary views with an embedding-based diversity gate, and fuses their per-view probabilities with a lightweight network. If the fused judgment remains uncertain, a diffusion policy drives the agent toward the candidate using only the tracked mask and depth until the decision becomes decisive. CLOSER runs locally and plugs into existing ObjectNav systems without retraining them. On Gibson and Habitat-Matterport 3D, it raises average success rate by 3.6 points and success weighted by path length by 2.0 points, showing that verifying evidence before committing improves embodied navigation.

</details>


### [135] [Learning from Automatic 3D Gaussian Splatting Edits for Online Bayesian Scene Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28490)
*Changrui Zhu,Simon Julier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）离线自监督学习的在线场景变化检测方法，用广义贝叶斯证据融合更新变化信念，在PASLCD和CCS3D上达到SOTA精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线场景变化检测需要融合新观测判断相对参考场景的变化区域，但变化先验概率与证据模型事先未知，且数值贝叶斯推断计算代价高，缺乏标注数据。

Method: 对参考3DGS模型做自动编辑生成带标签训练视图（无需人工标注），结合未变化的参考观测，离线学习场景特定的证据模型并估计有效像素先验与响应统计；在线阶段融合当前证据与历史支持，用以学习到的响应统计为参数的闭式局部贝叶斯因子更新累积3D高斯场，并通过物体引导的细化生成最终变化掩码。

Result: 在PASLCD和CCS3D上分别取得67.64%和75.82% mIoU，达到当前最优精度；在PASLCD完整查询序列上以17.2 FPS运行，为所评估在线方法中最快。

Conclusion: 通过3DGS自动编辑实现免标注自监督学习，结合闭式贝叶斯证据融合与物体引导细化，可在保证实时性的同时实现高精度在线场景变化检测。

Abstract: Online scene change detection requires integrating incoming observations to determine which regions have changed relative to a reference scene. We update change beliefs as new images arrive using generalised Bayesian evidence fusion. However, the prior probability of change and evidence models are unknown beforehand, and numerical Bayesian inference can be costly. We address these challenges by learning offline from automatic edits to a reference 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model. These edits generate labelled training views without manual annotations. Together with unchanged reference observations, they provide supervision for learning scene-specific evidence models and estimating the effective pixel prior and response statistics. Online prediction combines current evidence with historical support, while closed-form local Bayes factors, parameterized by the learned response statistics, update an accumulated 3D Gaussian field. Object-guided refinement produces the final change masks. Our method achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on PASLCD and CCS3D, with $67.64\%$ and $75.82\%$ mIoU, respectively. On PASLCD, the complete pipeline runs at $17.2$\,FPS over complete query sequences, the fastest among the evaluated online methods.

</details>


### [136] [GazeLens for Efficient Egocentric Video Question Answering with Simple Gaze-Driven Adaptive Token Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28502)
*Trong-Thang Pham,Anh Nguyen,Hien Van Nguyen,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GazeLens：面向智能眼镜的小型视觉语言模型，结合帧选择与"凝视+问题"引导的token压缩，每帧仅用两个token保留聚焦与上下文信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能眼镜上的小型VLM计算预算有限：冗余帧浪费视觉预算，激进的空间压缩又丢失细节；且凝视只反映用户关注点，证据可能来自周围场景，需要兼顾聚焦与情境。

Method: 1) 视觉编码前用HOG变化与图像清晰度选帧；2) 对每个选中帧用高斯池化生成凝视聚焦摘要与互补摘要；3) 用轻量门控根据问题相关性将两者融合为一个主token，与全局场景token一起构成每帧两个token。

Result: 在StreamGaze上，宏平均与微平均准确率均高于所评估的全网格与token缩减基线，且输入前向FLOPs低于共享骨干的token缩减基线。

Conclusion: 极轻量的每帧双token表示可在有限算力下为可穿戴VLM同时保留聚焦与上下文证据，实现准确率与效率的较好平衡。

Abstract: Smart glasses can help users understand their activities and surroundings, but small vision-language models must preserve useful evidence within limited computation. This is difficult because redundant frames consume the visual budget, while aggressive spatial compression can discard relevant details. Although gaze identifies the wearer's focus, the question may require evidence from the surrounding scene. To address these constraints, we propose GazeLens, which combines frame selection with gaze- and question-guided token reduction. First, HOG transitions and image sharpness select frames before visual encoding. For each selected frame, Gaussian pooling then forms gaze-focused and complementary summaries. Because their relevance depends on the question, a lightweight gate blends them into a primary token. Together with a global scene token, this retains both focused and contextual summaries within two tokens per frame. On StreamGaze, GazeLens achieves higher macro and micro accuracy than the evaluated full-grid and token-reduction baselines, with lower input-forward FLOPs than the shared-backbone token-reduction baselines.

</details>


### [137] [Deep Learning-Based Road Lane Quality Assessment from Egocentric Dashcam Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28508)
*Shaif Chowdhury,Akhila Yeruvasu,Moshiur Rahman Tonmoy,Ayush Goyal,Dunren Che*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于智能手机行车的GPS地理参考数据集（63,839帧，其中3,339帧匹配OSM车道数弱标签），并定义三种车道标线质量指标CSI、LCR、RLCS，配合基线模型实现无参考、低成本的车道标线质量评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车道标线状况对人工驾驶和自动驾驶都至关重要，但现状主要靠人工巡检评估；现有自动驾驶数据集面向车道检测与场景理解，缺少面向养护的可见性、连续性、退化程度标签，缺乏可规模化、低成本的质量评估手段。

Method: 构建南德州采集的智能手机行车记录仪地理参考数据集（63,839帧），将3,339帧与OpenStreetMap推导的车道数匹配作为弱监督；提出三项指标：基于Laplacian响应的对比度稳定性指数CSI、基于边缘与Hough直线证据的线连续性比率LCR、以及预测与地图车道数一致性的回归车道置信度RLCS；并训练基线预测模型，用50张人工排序图像做初步排序一致性验证。

Result: 基线模型性能较强：CSI（MAE 0.039，R²=0.97）、LCR（MAE 0.093，R²=0.84）；CSI与LCR为无需密集人工标注的分割衍生无参考指标。50张人工排序图像验证中，LCR与人工排序的Spearman ρ=0.7316、Kendall τ=0.5472，优于PSNR、SSIM、BRISQUE、NIQE、LPIPS等通用图像质量指标。

Conclusion: 该数据集、指标与基线为实现可扩展、低成本、位置感知的车道标线质量评估提供了初步基础，并开源数据与代码。

Abstract: Reliable lane markings are important for both human drivers and automated driving systems, but their condition is still commonly assessed through manual inspection. Existing autonomous-driving datasets primarily support lane detection and scene understanding and usually lack maintenance-oriented labels for lane-marking visibility, continuity, or degradation. In this study, we present a geo-referenced smartphone dashcam dataset and baseline framework for lane-marking quality assessment. The dataset contains 63,839 road-facing frames collected across South Texas using a GPS-enabled dashcam application. A subset of 3,339 frames is matched with OpenStreetMap-derived lane-count labels, which are used as weak supervision for lane-count regression and map-consistency evaluation. We propose three lane-quality measures: Contrast Stability Index (CSI), which captures lane-background contrast using Laplacian responses; Line Continuity Ratio (LCR), which estimates structural continuity using edge and Hough-line evidence; and Regression Lane Confidence Score (RLCS), which measures agreement between predicted and map-derived lane counts on matched frames. The baseline prediction models attained strong performance, with CSI (MAE $0.039$, $R^2=0.97$) and LCR (MAE $0.093$, $R^2=0.84$). Notably, CSI and LCR are no-reference, segmentation-derived quality metrics that can be computed without dense human labels. In a pilot validation using 50 human-ranked images, these proposed lane-specific metrics show stronger rank agreement with human lane-quality ordering (e.g., Spearman's $ρ=0.7316$ and Kendall's $τ=0.5472$ for LCR) than general image-quality measures such as PSNR, SSIM, BRISQUE, NIQE, and LPIPS. We believe the dataset, metrics, and baseline provide an initial step toward scalable, low-cost, location-aware lane-marking quality assessment. Data and code: https://github.com/Shaif95/Road_Quality

</details>


### [138] [SenseAlign: Understanding Vision and Touch through Language at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28511)
*Chuyang Ye,Qinting Jiang,Jingyan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SenseAlign，一种无需标注数据的测试时自适应方法，通过共享的类别文本嵌入对触觉-视觉-语言模型进行模态加权与特征校正，在自然分布偏移与受控损坏下提升识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉-视觉-语言模型在实际部署中会遇到光照变化、接触质量波动或传感器硬件更换等问题，导致视觉或触觉输入发生偏移、识别性能下降。当两种模态共同参与预测时，不可靠模态可能压过另一模态的正确证据；而部署阶段通常缺少标注数据，因此需要利用无标注观测进行测试时自适应。作者还发现，仅凭置信度难以反映模态可靠性差异，而将累积观测与候选类文本嵌入对比可以揭示这种差异；同时，仅做几何对齐不足以提升识别，还需要针对竞争类别精炼特征。

Method: SenseAlign 包含两个模块，均以类别文本嵌入作为共享语义参考并保持所有编码器冻结：1）Alignment Trust：依据各模态对共享预测类别的支持程度为每个模态加权，从而识别并抑制不可靠模态；2）Anchor Realignment：利用同一组类别文本嵌入拟合一个共享的特征校正，使预测类别与备选类别在特征空间中分离。两个模块共用同一段无标注历史观测数据。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在自然分布偏移和受控数据损坏两种情形下均取得较强的识别性能。

Conclusion: 以文本嵌入作为统一语义锚点，可同时服务于测试时的模态可靠性评估与特征校正，是提升多模态触觉-视觉-语言模型部署鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: Tactile-visual-language models combine vision and touch through a shared language space to recognize objects and materials. In real-world deployment, changes in lighting, contact quality or sensor hardware can shift visual or tactile inputs and degrade recognition performance. When both modalities contribute to a prediction, an unreliable modality can override correct evidence from the other. Since labeled data are generally unavailable during deployment, test-time adaptation offers a way to recover recognition performance from unlabeled observations. In this setting, we find that comparing accumulated observations with the text embeddings of candidate classes reveals modality reliability differences that confidence can miss. Refining features against competing classes also improves recognition beyond geometric alignment alone. These findings motivate SenseAlign, which uses a common semantic reference for test-time modality weighting and feature correction. Alignment Trust weights each modality according to its support for shared predicted classes. Anchor Realignment uses the same class text embeddings to fit a shared feature correction that separates predicted classes from alternatives. Both modules use the same unlabeled history while keeping all encoders frozen. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate strong recognition performance under both natural shifts and controlled corruptions.

</details>


### [139] [Touch-JEPA: Tactile Representation Learning from Hand-Object Video with Limited Tactile Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28519)
*Minseong Kweon,Qiao Feng,Yiming Huang,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Touch-JEPA是一种从手-物交互视频中学习触觉表征的框架：先以纯视频自监督掩码预测学习接触动态表征，再在少量触觉标注下把视频与触觉统一到共享表征中，从而在有限触觉监督下实现接触判别与触觉强度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解手-物交互中的接触与触觉强度是实现物理智能系统的关键，但触觉测量成本高、覆盖范围有限，制约了触觉表征学习，因此需要一种能利用易获取的视频数据、并只需少量触觉监督的表征学习方法。

Method: 提出Touch-JEPA框架。首先设计自监督预训练目标：不依赖任何触觉标签，仅从视频学习；借助由视频导出的手部几何，在对应手表面位置上，基于空间与时间上下文预测被掩码的视觉特征，促使表征捕获潜在接触动态。随后将该形式扩展为联合表征学习：对所有视频施加掩码预测，而触觉监督仅用于一小部分有标注数据，从而把共享表征锚定到实测接触与强度上。

Result: 仅使用自监督预训练时，在冻结的Touch-JEPA特征上接简单有监督读出层，即在三个人手数据集基准的接触判别与触觉强度估计上优于有监督基线，并可零机器人触觉训练数据迁移到机器人手；加入有限触觉监督的联合学习后，人手基准上的接触与强度估计性能进一步提升。

Conclusion: 手-物视频能够为触觉表征学习提供可扩展的自监督信号，而有限的触觉测量则提供互补的物理锚定；二者结合可在低标注成本下获得有效的接触与触觉强度表征。

Abstract: Understanding contact and tactile magnitude in hand-object interaction is a key step toward physically intelligent systems, yet learning such representations is limited by the cost and restricted coverage of tactile measurements. We present Touch-JEPA, a framework for learning tactile representations from hand-object videos with limited tactile supervision. We first introduce a self-supervised pretraining objective that learns from video alone without tactile supervision. Using video-derived hand geometry, Touch-JEPA predicts masked visual features at corresponding hand-surface locations from spatial and temporal context, encouraging the representation to capture latent contact dynamics. We then extend this formulation to joint representation learning, where masked prediction is applied to all videos while tactile supervision is available only for a labeled subset, grounding the shared representation in measured contact and magnitude. With self-supervised pretraining alone, simple supervised readouts on frozen Touch-JEPA features outperform supervised baselines in contact discrimination and tactile magnitude estimation across three human-hand benchmarks, and transfer to robotic hands without robot tactile training data. Joint learning with limited tactile supervision further improves contact and magnitude estimation on the human-hand benchmarks. Together, these results show that hand-object video can provide scalable self-supervision for tactile representation learning, while limited tactile measurements provide complementary physical grounding.

</details>


### [140] [Latent Bridge: From Video Latents to 3D Geometry via Feature Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28521)
*Shuai Zhang,Zhanpeng Ouyang,Zhengqing Chen,Ruohong Mei,Shaoyu Chen,Weiqiang Ren,Hui Sun,Qian Zhang,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Latent Bridge，将视频生成潜空间与几何特征空间连接，直接从视频潜变量预测相机、深度和点云，跳过 RGB 解码再编码的中间环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成与前馈 3D 重建通常使用互不相通的表征，需要先解码 RGB 再重新编码才能从生成潜变量中恢复几何，流程冗余且低效。

Method: 为 Wan2.1 和 V-RAE 分别构建表征专属的 bridge；通过特征蒸馏在 RGB 视频上训练，视频编码器与几何模型保持冻结；训练无需真值几何，也无需运行下游几何聚合器或预测头；推理时同一潜变量可同时支持 RGB 解码与几何预测，且同一 bridge 可复用于同潜空间的图生视频与文生视频。

Result: 在 Co3Dv2 与 WildRGB-D 上取得有竞争力的几何重建质量，实现高效的端到端生成与重建，并加快潜空间到几何的解码速度。

Conclusion: 潜空间对齐可作为视频生成与 3D 重建之间实用且可复用的接口。

Abstract: Video generation and feed-forward 3D reconstruction typically use separate representations, requiring RGB decoding and visual re-encoding to recover geometry from generated latents. We propose Latent Bridge, which connects video generative latent spaces with geometric feature spaces to directly predict 3D geometry from video latents. We develop representation-specific bridges for Wan2.1 and V-RAE, enabling feed-forward prediction of cameras, depth, and 3D point clouds without the intermediate RGB pathway. Each bridge is trained on RGB videos through feature distillation, while the video encoder and geometry model remain frozen. Training requires neither ground-truth geometry nor execution of the downstream geometric aggregator or prediction heads. At inference, the same video latents support both RGB decoding and geometry prediction. The same trained bridge can be reused for image-to-video and text-to-video generation within the same video latent space, without bridge retraining. Experiments on Co3Dv2 and WildRGB-D demonstrate competitive geometric reconstruction quality, efficient end-to-end generation and reconstruction, and faster latent-to-geometry decoding, highlighting latent alignment as a practical and reusable interface between video generation and 3D reconstruction. Our code is available at: https://github.com/hustvl/Latent-Bridge.

</details>


### [141] [Copyright Leakage Detection in AI Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28524)
*Shaif Chowdhury,Moshiur Rahman Tonmoy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个AI生成视频版权泄露检测基准数据集（50个受版权保护概念×5张图片×10个生成视频），并给出基于对比检索框架的检测工具集，将检测转化为视频到图像检索问题，检测准确率达99.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型可从图像与文本提示生成逼真视频，近期进展引发了对受版权保护视觉内容被复现的担忧；然而，生成视频中的版权泄露检测仍基本处于空白状态。

Method: 构建基准数据集：50个受版权保护的概念，每个概念5张图片；每个概念生成5个视频（分别使用图像+提示视频生成模型与仅提示视频生成模型）。开发基于对比检索框架的AI工具集，将版权泄露检测表述为视频到图像检索问题：聚合视频帧嵌入并与版权图片嵌入数据库匹配，采用单一相似度阈值，并返回最相似的版权参考作为支撑证据。

Result: 检索与检测性能强劲，检测准确率达到99.6%，仅出现一个假阴性；结果表明基于检索的方法对AI生成视频的版权泄露检测具有可扩展性与可解释性。

Conclusion: 基于检索的方法可有效实现规模化、可解释的生成视频版权泄露检测；数据集与源码均已公开，便于后续研究与复现。

Abstract: Video generation models can create realistic-looking video footage from images and text prompts. Some of the recent advances have raised concerns about the reproduction of copyrighted visual content. However, copyright leakage detection in generated videos remains largely unexplored.
  In this paper, we introduce a new benchmark data set for copyright leakage detection, comprising 50 copyrighted concepts, each with 5 images. For each concept, we have 5 videos created using the image+prompt video generation model and the prompt-only video generation model. Following that, we develop an AI toolset based on a contrastive retrieval framework that formulates copyright leakage detection as a video-to-image retrieval problem by matching aggregated video frame embeddings against a database of copyrighted image embeddings. Our method uses a single similarity threshold and retrieves the most similar copyrighted reference as supporting evidence.
  We conducted experiments showing strong retrieval and detection performance, achieving 99.6\% detection accuracy with only a single false negative. These results show the effectiveness of the retrieval-based method for scalable and explainable copyright leakage detection in AI-generated videos. Data can be accessed publicly from https://zenodo.org/records/21386117 and the source code for this project is publicly available at https://github.com/Shaif95/video-cp-Leakage.

</details>


### [142] [CoGaussian: Open-Vocabulary Gaussian-based Collaborative 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28530)
*Om Shivam Verma,Shaya Farahmand,Lingjun Zhao,Lu Gan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoGaussian，一种基于高斯的开放词汇协作语义占据预测框架，用基础模型对齐的稀疏3D高斯表示场景，跨智能体通信融合几何与语义信息，摆脱稠密体素标注和预定义类别限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作感知可让智能体获取超出单机视野的场景语义与几何，提升交通安全与多机器人效率。但现有协作语义占据方法依赖稠密3D体素标注，且仅能识别预定义语义类别中的目标，泛化与扩展受限。

Method: 将场景表示为与基础模型对齐的稀疏3D高斯集合，作为多智能体间传递与融合几何、语义信息的紧凑表示，实现开放词汇的协作语义占据预测；并在Semantic-OPV2V基准上进行评估。

Result: 在Semantic-OPV2V基准上，方法优于现有开放词汇单智能体方法，体现了开放词汇协作场景理解的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于高斯的稀疏表示可有效支撑开放词汇协作语义占据预测，验证了协作+开放词汇范式的潜力。

Abstract: Collaborative perception enables agents to retrieve the scene semantics and geometry beyond the field of view of any individual agent. This broader scene understanding enhances transportation safety and improves efficiency in multi-robot systems. However, existing collaborative semantic occupancy methods rely on dense 3D voxel annotations and are limited to recognizing objects within a predefined set of semantic classes. To overcome these limitations, we present CoGaussian, an open-vocabulary Gaussian-based collaborative semantic occupancy prediction framework. CoGaussian represents a scene as a set of foundation model-aligned sparse 3D Gaussians, which serve as a compact representation for communicating and fusing geometric and semantic information across multiple agents. On the Semantic-OPV2V benchmark, our method outperforms existing open-vocabulary single-agent approaches, highlighting the effectiveness of open-vocabulary collaborative scene understanding.

</details>


### [143] [Predictive Latents for Autoregressive Language Models: Possibility, Gauge, and Obstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28540)
*Yuzhou Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文在一个潜变量过程上区分了三种风险（token风险、teacher-forced的潜空间风险R1、开环迭代风险RH），证明在特定充分条件下（Bayes后验依赖于满秩k维统计量），宽度为k的潜空间配合线性最小二乘转移和重新拟合的unembedding可匹配Bayes预测器，并指出匹配存在尖锐障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年多种自回归模型在预测下一个token之外还迭代隐藏状态（连续思考、pause步、循环深度），但缺乏对"小损失是否意味着可靠预测"的严格刻画，作者希望澄清潜过程上几类风险之间的关系与失效边界。

Method: 理论分析：定义token风险R_tok、teacher-forced潜风险R1与开环风险RH；在Bayes后验为满秩协方差k维统计量的函数时，构造宽度k潜变量+线性最小二乘转移+重拟合unembedding并证明其匹配Bayes token预测器；通过反例（常数潜变量、零空间游走）与可识别性/规范（gauge）分析刻画失败模式。

Result: 得到正面匹配定理：统计量仅在可逆gauge意义下可识别，白化残差流在重拟合unembedding后不改变token分布。同时给出三类尖锐反例：常数潜变量使R1趋零而token交叉熵退化为边际熵；完美token预测器仍可隐藏使开环误差随horizon线性增长的零空间游走；小的next-latent损失与小的next-token损失都不能单独为自由潜空间rollout背书。

Conclusion: 报告near-zero的next-latent或next-token损失时必须同时声明秩或单射性条件，否则不足以certificate token预测或潜空间自由rollout的可靠性；三种风险不可互相替代。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models are trained by next-token cross-entropy, yet several recent systems also iterate a hidden state without emitting a token: continuous thoughts, pause steps, and recurrent depth. This note separates three risks on one latent process. The token risk $R_{\mathrm{tok}}$ scores a readout. The teacher-forced latent risk $R_1$ scores a one-step map that sees the true hidden state. The open-loop risk $R_H$ scores the same map after it has been iterated on its own outputs. If the Bayes next-token posterior is a function of a $k$-dimensional statistic with full-rank covariance, a width-$k$ latent, a linear least-squares transition, and a refit unembedding match the Bayes token predictor. The statistic is identified only up to an invertible gauge: whitening the residual stream does not change the token law once the unembedding is refit. The matching obstructions are sharp. A constant latent drives $R_1$ to zero while token cross-entropy falls back to the marginal entropy. A perfect token predictor can still hide a null-space walk whose open-loop error grows linearly in the horizon. A small next-latent loss does not certificate next-token prediction, and a small next-token loss does not certificate free latent rollout, unless a rank or injectivity condition is stated with the number.

</details>


### [144] [OverSeer: Multi-Agent World Models Beyond What Agents See](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28554)
*Runjia Li,Kam Woh Ng,Minghao Chen,Junlin Han,Jianyuan Wang,Zhaochong An,Juan-Manuel Perez-Rua,Andrea Vedaldi,Shikun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出OverSeer，一种通过引入空间固定的辅助相机视角来增强多智能体视频世界模型一致性的方法，并用delta-VAE高效压缩这些冗余的半静态视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在扩展到多智能体时难以生成多个智能体专属视频流，并保持时间与智能体间的一致性。智能体视角随位置变化可完全改变，不适合作为世界状态表示，因此需要一种更稳定的世界中心表示来维持一致性。

Method: 在智能体自我中心视角之外，加入空间固定的辅助相机视角，作为世界中心表示的代理，因为固定视角的变化直接对应世界状态的局部变化。同时提出delta-VAE，仅编码相对于静态参考图像的变化，利用辅助视角的冗余与半静态特性显著减少token数量。

Result: 在Minecraft和Doom上的实验显示，世界与智能体一致性均有提升；在真实世界多相机拍摄数据上的定性结果表明方法不依赖游戏引擎。压缩分析显示delta-VAE大幅降低辅助视角生成的计算开销。

Conclusion: 空间固定的辅助视角是一种简单而高效的方式来提升多智能体世界模型的一致性，并且配合delta-VAE可降低其计算成本，具有跨域适用性。

Abstract: Despite the success of video world models, extending them to multiple agents is difficult because it requires generating several agent-specific video streams that are consistent in time and between agents. To address this gap, we introduce OverSeer, a multi-agent world model that augments egocentric agent views with auxiliary camera views. We suggest that, by fixing the auxiliary views in space, they act as a proxy for a world-centric representation: Unlike the agents' views, which can change entirely due to a simple change in agent position, changes in the auxiliary views directly map to localized changes in the world state. We show that these fixed views substantially help the model to maintain consistency. We also show that, due to their redundant and semi-static nature, the auxiliary views can be efficiently compressed with a delta-VAE that encodes only the changes relative to a static reference image, reducing the number of tokens required to process them. Experiments in Minecraft and Doom demonstrate improved world and agent consistency, with qualitative results on real-world multi-camera captures indicating that the approach is not tied to game engines. Our compression analysis further shows that delta-VAE substantially reduces the computational overhead of auxiliary-view generation. We conclude that these fixed views provide a simple and efficient way to improve multi-agent world models.

</details>


### [145] [Continual Concept Personalization in Diffusion Models via Residual-Space Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28567)
*Yuzhi Liu,Enis Simsar,Giulia Lanzillotta,Thomas Hofmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究文生图个性化中的"持续概念个性化"问题，将残差空间自适应扩展到预训练扩散模型的随机自适应过程中，实现最高概念保真度与近零遗忘，并利用残差空间动态定位关键自适应层，最多减少72%可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图个性化方法通常对每个概念独立训练，或假设所有概念同时可用；但实际应用中概念往往按顺序到达，模型需持续学习新主体、物体或风格，同时尽量减少对已学概念的干扰。论文针对这一持续概念个性化场景展开研究。

Method: 将残差空间自适应（residual-space adaptation）扩展到预训练扩散模型的随机自适应过程中，用于顺序到达的概念学习；并分析学习到的残差空间动态，据此识别扩散模型内部自适应能力集中的层，将自适应限制在这些层上。

Result: 在同类持续个性化方法中取得最高的概念保真度，同时保持接近零的遗忘；按识别出的层限制自适应后，性能几乎不变，而可训练参数最多减少72%。

Conclusion: 残差空间动态不仅能提升持续概念个性化的效果，还能揭示扩散模型中自适应容量的分布，直接指导更具参数效率的自适应策略。

Abstract: Text-to-image personalization aims to equip pretrained diffusion models with the ability to learn user-specific concepts from only a few reference images. Existing methods, however, typically train each concept independently or assume that all concepts are available simultaneously. In practical applications, concepts often arrive sequentially, requiring models to continually learn new subjects, objects, or styles while minimizing interference with previously acquired concepts. We address this continual concept personalization setting by extending residual-space adaptation to the stochastic adaptation process of pretrained diffusion models. Our work achieves the highest concept fidelity among competing continual personalization methods while maintaining near-zero forgetting. Beyond these empirical gains, the learned residual-space dynamics reveal where adaptation capacity is concentrated within the diffusion model. Restricting adaptation to the identified layers by our method preserves nearly the same performance while reducing the number of trainable parameters by up to 72\%, demonstrating that residual-space dynamics can identify the layers that contribute most to continual concept personalization and directly guide more parameter-efficient adaptation.

</details>


### [146] [Can a Display Be Used for Rapid LiDAR-Camera Extrinsic Calibration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28571)
*Zuhao Zou,Xiangcheng Hu,Jianhao Jiao,Jin Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScreenCalib 提出一种利用普通可编程显示屏（手机、平板、笔记本、显示器、电视）完成 LiDAR—相机外参标定的方法，通过屏幕显示 AprilTag 提供视觉标记、由 LiDAR 测量屏幕物理表面与边界，实现跨模态间接关联，并在仿真与三种传感器平台上验证精度优于传统标定板方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 LiDAR—相机外参标定依赖专门制作的标定板/靶标，准备成本高；而普通显示屏随手可得，既能显示已知视觉标记又具有可控几何形状，有望减少专用靶标制备。但屏幕显示几何会带来跨模态关联歧义，室内杂波也干扰屏幕提取。

Method: 提出标签引导的屏幕提取、几何假设生成与细化、以及利用周围场景边缘消解歧义三步流程；支持单姿态模式（仅一次静态摆放）与联合多姿态模式（通过跨姿态一致性融合多次独立静态观测，并联合优化共享外参与标签尺度，无需连续运动或姿态间轨迹估计）。

Result: 仿真与三种传感器平台（涵盖机械式与非重复扫描 LiDAR，干净与杂乱环境）实验显示，ScreenCalib 在平移精度上优于基于标定板的竞争方法；联合多姿态细化进一步降低三平台的平均旋转与平移误差并改善渲染地图对齐；在手机、平板、笔记本、显示器、电视等多种屏幕上的评测验证了其通用性。

Conclusion: ScreenCalib 表明普通可编程显示屏可作为专用标定靶标的实用替代方案，兼顾易得性、精度与跨设备通用性，代码已开源。

Abstract: ScreenCalib enables light detection and ranging (LiDAR)--camera extrinsic calibration using an ordinary programmable display, providing known visual markers and controlled geometry while reducing dedicated target preparation. The camera observes displayed AprilTags, whereas the LiDAR measures the physical screen surface and boundary, requiring indirect cross-modal association. Display geometry introduces association ambiguities, while indoor clutter complicates extraction. ScreenCalib addresses these challenges through tag-guided display extraction, geometric hypothesis generation and refinement, and surrounding scene edges for ambiguity resolution. The single-pose mode requires only one static placement. The joint multi-pose mode combines independent static observations through cross-pose consensus and jointly refines the shared extrinsics and tag scale, reducing sensitivity to tag-size measurement errors without requiring continuous motion or inter-pose trajectory estimation. Experiments in simulation and on three different sensor platforms cover mechanical and nonrepetitive LiDARs in clean and cluttered environments. Benchmark results show that ScreenCalib outperforms competitive calibration-board-based methods in translation accuracy. Joint multi-pose refinement further reduces mean rotation and translation errors across the three platforms and improves rendered-map alignment. Evaluation across readily available displays, including cell phones, tablets, laptops, computer monitors, and televisions, further examines ScreenCalib's versatility as a practical alternative to dedicated calibration targets. The source code is available at https://github.com/ZuhaoZou2026/ScreenCalib.

</details>


### [147] [WildMAE: Structure-Aware Masked Autoencoding for Wildlife Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28590)
*Yicheng Zhang,Yujie Jin,Ke Zhang,Jiaxin Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WildMAE是在标准MAE解码器侧做结构感知改进的野生动物掩码图像建模方法：通过零门控卷积残差引入局部邻域先验、门控交叉注意力从可见位置检索上下文，并采用归一化像素、Sobel边缘与感知损失渐进式训练；历史记录显示其在Snapshot Serengeti 4744张测试图像、75%掩码下重建指标优于原始MAE（PSNR 27.90 vs 24.33 dB，SSIM 0.840 vs 0.780，LPIPS 0.160 vs 0.320），但作者强调这些为依赖历史协议的结果，并非新近统一复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱图像在外观、光照、背景杂乱和视觉退化方面变化很大，需要无标签的表示学习框架；MAE通过重建随机遮挡图像块建模此类数据，但其解码器缺乏显式局部结构先验与定向上下文检索能力，重建质量有限。

Method: 提出WildMAE，对MAE解码器做两项扩展：(1) 零门控卷积残差路径，为解码器注入二维邻域先验；(2) 门控交叉注意力，使被掩码位置能从可见位置特征中检索上下文，同时保留原有Transformer解码器。训练采用渐进式目标：归一化像素损失、Sobel边缘损失与感知损失。

Result: 在Snapshot Serengeti原始研究的4744张测试图像、75%掩码设置下，WildMAE与原始MAE的重建指标分别为：PSNR 27.90 vs 24.33 dB，SSIM 0.840 vs 0.780，LPIPS 0.160 vs 0.320。另有多项历史记录显示在四种合成退化条件下具有较优的平均差值，并给出组件变体的描述。

Conclusion: 作者将方法、实验观察及其适用范围一并呈现，明确指出所报告指标为依赖历史协议的既有结果，既非新近复现的统一对比，也不构成对真正缺失像素恢复能力的验证。WildMAE提供了一个把全局token混合与空间及定向上下文精化相结合的实用架构案例。

Abstract: Camera-trap imagery presents substantial variation in animal appearance, illumination, background clutter, and visual degradation. Masked autoencoders (MAEs) offer a label-free framework for modeling such data through reconstruction of randomly hidden image patches. We present WildMAE, a decoder-side extension of MAE that combines local structure-aware refinement with masked-to-visible cross-attention. A zero-gated convolutional residual path introduces a two-dimensional neighborhood prior, while gated cross-attention allows masked positions to retrieve contextual information from visible-position features without replacing the original Transformer decoder. The associated training recipe progressively introduces normalized-pixel, Sobel-edge, and perceptual objectives. In the original Snapshot Serengeti study, covering 4,744 test images at 75% masking, the reported reconstruction scores were 27.90 versus 24.33 dB PSNR, 0.840 versus 0.780 SSIM, and 0.160 versus 0.320 LPIPS for WildMAE and vanilla MAE, respectively. Additional historical records document favorable average differences under four synthetic degradation conditions and describe component variants. We present the method, experimental observations, and their scope together: the retained metrics are historical protocol-dependent results, rather than a newly reproduced, harmonized comparison or a validation of restoration from genuinely unavailable pixels. WildMAE provides a practical architectural case study for combining global token mixing with spatial and targeted contextual refinement in wildlife masked-image modeling.

</details>


### [148] [Learning the 4D World through Point Trajectory Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28594)
*Byungwoo Jeon,Sunghwan Hong,Dohyeon Kim,Jonghoon Lee,Jungwoo Park,Inhee Lee,Doosan Baek,Jinil Kim,Taehyun Lim,Hanbyul Joo,Marc Pollefeys,Saining Xie,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Video-Point Model (VPM)：以多视角视频历史与语言指令为条件，联合预测任意视角的未来视频与持续跟踪场景点的 3D 轨迹，并将其扩展到机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的多视角预测要求各相机视频描述同一个演化中的 3D 场景，但纯视频目标缺乏跨视角的共享 3D 运动约束，容易出现外观与几何不一致。

Method: 在预训练世界模型上加入相机条件化的多视角处理以融合互补观测，并新增轨迹头预测物理点随时间运动；每条轨迹跨所有视角跟踪同一空间点，用其与视频生成联合监督锚定各相机外观变化于共享 3D 运动。构建 VPM-Data（同步多视角视频+持久点轨迹仿真语料）与 VP-WorldBench 基准；并把机器人动作作为 token 模态加入生成器，与未来视频联合去噪。

Result: 在 VP-WorldBench 与现有 4D 生成基准上，点监督相比仅视频版本同时提升视频保真度与 3D 运动精度；在仿真与真实机器人实验中，启用动作的模型操作成功率也得到提升。

Conclusion: 跨视角共享的点轨迹监督是一种有效的 3D 感知表示，可统一多视角视频预测与机器人策略学习。

Abstract: Reliable multi-view forecasting requires videos from different cameras to describe the same evolving 3D scene. To this end, we introduce Video-Point Model (VPM), a world model that conditions on multi-view video histories and a language instruction to jointly forecast future videos from arbitrary viewpoints and the 3D trajectories of persistent scene points. We extend a pretrained world model with camera-conditioned multi-view processing that integrates complementary observations across cameras, and add a trajectory head that predicts how physical points move through time. Each trajectory tracks a single point across all views, and supervising these trajectories alongside video generation therefore anchors appearance changes in every camera to a shared description of 3D motion. To support training and evaluation, we build VPM-Data, a simulation corpus pairing synchronized multi-view videos with persistent point trajectories, and VP-WorldBench, a forecasting benchmark spanning simulated and real environments. We further extend VPM to robot control by adding robot actions as a token modality in the generator. Action tokens share the point-supervised backbone and are denoised jointly with future videos, thereby grounding the policy in the same 3D-aware representation. On VP-WorldBench and existing 4D generation benchmarks, point supervision improves both video fidelity and 3D motion accuracy over a video-only counterpart, and in simulated and real-robot experiments, it also raises the manipulation success of the action-enabled model.

</details>


### [149] [Neural Implicit Energy Optimization for LiDAR Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28598)
*Hrishikesh Viswanath,Md Ashiqur Rahman,Chi Lin,Damon Conover,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将神经LiDAR建图表述为神经隐式表示参数空间上能量泛函的优化问题，利用基于水平集形状算子（曲率）的能量泛函正则化SDF，从而在大规模LiDAR场景中获得更平滑且保持几何保真度的重建，且无额外推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经隐式表示能以内存高效的方式参数化大规模LiDAR场景的SDF，但从稀疏且常有噪声的输入中恢复的几何存在抖动与伪影。经典几何优化通过表面能量泛函（其极小值对应光滑表面）来消除此类伪影，作者希望把这一原理引入神经LiDAR建图。

Method: 将神经LiDAR建图表述为在神经隐式表示参数空间上优化能量泛函；具体做法是在SDF水平集的形状算子上定义能量泛函（刻画表面曲率）作为正则项，沿表面促进光滑性，并将该经典几何先验适配到百万点级LiDAR地图的尺度。

Result: 在大规模数据集上的实验表明，重建结果的平滑性得到改善，同时保持了几何保真度，且不带来额外的推理阶段计算开销。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 把经典几何能量泛函先验融入神经隐式SDF建图，是提升大规模LiDAR重建质量的有效途径，可在不增加推理成本的前提下兼顾平滑性与几何精度。

Abstract: Neural implicit representations provide memory-efficient signed distance field (SDF) parameterizations for large-scale LiDAR scenes, but the geometry recovered from sparse and often noisy inputs carries jitter and artifacts. Classical geometric optimization techniques resolve such artifacts with surface energy functionals, whose minimizers correspond to smooth surfaces. Motivated by this principle, we formulate neural LiDAR mapping as the optimization of energy functionals over the parameter space of neural implicit representations. Specifically, we regularize the signed distance field with an energy functional defined on the shape operator of its level set, which represents the curvature of the surface, thus encouraging smoothness along the surface. This adapts classical geometric priors to the scale of million-point LiDAR maps. Experiments on large-scale datasets show reconstructions with improved smoothness at preserved geometric fidelity, and no additional inference-time cost. The code is publicly available at \href{https://github.com/HrishikeshVish/nieo}{https://github.com/HrishikeshVish/nieo}

</details>


### [150] [KoalaGS: Complexity-Aware Continuous Level of Detail for Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28615)
*Radu Beche,Raoul de Charette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 KoalaGS，利用输入视角预计算的图像复杂度先验来引导 3D 高斯重定位，使单一高斯基元集在缩减计算预算下仍能平滑、高效地渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Gaussian Splatting 场景在计算预算收缩时需要以更小的前缀（更少基元）渲染，即连续 Level of Detail 场景。核心难点在于决定预算下降时哪些高斯应保留容量。作者通过试点研究发现：由输入视角在优化前计算出的图像复杂度可预测重建误差在图像与 3D 空间中的集中位置；同时简单区域分配了过多高斯，在低预算下退化程度与复杂区域相当，尽管其本质上更易表达。

Method: 基于 3DGS-MCMC 重建做试点分析，获得复杂度-误差相关性结论。在 KoalaGS 训练中引入该复杂度先验，将高斯重定位偏向重建较差的区域，同时保证跨 LoD 的平滑退化；再设计基元重要性评分，用于低容量下的推理并保持场景结构。

Result: 在三个数据集上测试，KoalaGS 在满容量下保持有竞争力的质量，并在整个预算区间显著提升质量，跨 LoD 过渡更平滑，同时支持更快的渐进式流式传输。

Conclusion: 以输入视角复杂度作为先验来分配高斯容量，是解决连续 LoD 下 3DGS 预算缩减问题的有效途径，兼顾满容量质量与低预算表现。

Abstract: Deploying a trained 3D Gaussian Splatting scene under shrinking compute budgets requires one primitive set to remain effective when rendered at smaller prefixes. In this continuous level of detail (LoD) setting, the challenge is to decide which Gaussians should retain capacity as the budget decreases. A pilot study on 3DGS-MCMC reconstructions shows that image complexity, computed from the input views before optimization, predicts where reconstruction error concentrates in image and 3D space. It also reveals that simple regions contain too many Gaussians and degrade as much as complex ones at lower budgets, despite being inherently cheaper to represent. Subsequently, in KoalaGS, during training we use this complexity prior to bias relocation toward poorly reconstructed areas while ensuring smooth degradation across LoDs. A primitive importance score then enables inference at low-capacity, while preserving the scene structure. Tested across three datasets, KoalaGS maintains competitive full-capacity quality while significantly improving quality across the budget spectrum, yielding smoother transitions across LoDs, and enabling faster progressive streaming.

</details>


### [151] [MindView: Benchmarking Generative Multi-View Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28622)
*Yanbo Wang,Siqi Luo,Siyu Zhang,Chenming Zhu,Wenzhao Zheng,Qian Yu,Juncheng Yan,Bin Fu,Tai Wang,Yu Qiao,Yihao Liu,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MindView 基准（513 个场景、1500 个样本、7 项任务）用于评估部分多视角观测下的生成式多视角空间智能，并进一步用任务监督微调 BAGEL 提升 24.3 分，同时提出免训练的证据选择器 MindCue 缓解扩展上下文下的性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间智能虽已通过空间落地的图像编辑等生成任务进行评估，但"由部分多视角观测生成语言引导、空间一致的目标视角"这一问题仍缺乏系统评测。同时初步实验显示，十一种生成器在扩展上下文设置下总体得分普遍下降，说明模型利用多视角证据的能力不足，需要新的评测与改进手段。

Method: 1）构建 MindView 基准：来自三个室内数据集、513 个场景、1500 个样本，含七项任务，分为几何主导（给定相机位置与朝向变化）与语义主导（需依据指称物体、空间关系与参考系线索推断目标视角）两类；2）评测 11 个生成器并做配对上下文分析（标准 vs. 扩展上下文）；3）在 MindView 训练集上微调 BAGEL，检验任务特定监督的作用；4）提出 MindCue——由目标视角假设引导的免训练空间证据选择器，以提升扩展上下文下的证据利用。

Result: 11 个生成器在 MindView 上均有较大提升空间；扩展上下文下全部 11 个模型总体得分下降；微调 BAGEL 后总体得分提升 24.3 分，七项任务全面提升；该微调模型在未使用标注的三个空间理解基准上也取得更高分数；MindCue 使扩展上下文下的总体得分回升，并超过标准上下文基线。

Conclusion: MindView 为生成式多视角空间智能提供了具体的测试平台；任务特定监督可显著改善目标视角生成，且"学习生成空间落地视角"可能反哺空间理解；免训练的 MindCue 能有效利用扩展的多视角证据。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence has recently been assessed through generative tasks such as spatially grounded image editing, yet language-guided generation of spatially consistent views from partial multi-view observations remains underexplored. We introduce MindView, a benchmark for generative multi-view spatial intelligence with 1,500 samples from 513 scenes across three indoor datasets. Its seven tasks form two families: geometry-dominant tasks specify changes in camera position and orientation, while semantics-dominant tasks require inferring target viewpoints by grounding referred objects and interpreting spatial relations and reference-frame cues. Evaluation of eleven generators reveals substantial room for improvement on MindView. A paired context analysis further shows that all eleven generators obtain lower overall scores under the expanded context setting. These findings motivate us to investigate whether task-specific supervision can improve target-view generation and whether models can use additional multi-view evidence effectively. We therefore fine-tune BAGEL on the MindView training dataset, improving its overall score by 24.3 points and yielding gains across all seven MindView tasks. Beyond these generation gains, the fine-tuned model also yields higher scores on three spatial-understanding benchmarks without training on their annotations, suggesting that learning to generate spatially grounded views may benefit spatial understanding. To improve evidence utilization under expanded context, we propose MindCue, a training-free spatial evidence selector guided by a target-view hypothesis. MindCue reverses the drop in overall score under expanded context and outperforms the standard-context baseline. Overall, MindView provides a concrete testbed for generative multi-view spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [152] [PsycheMME: A Psychology-Informed Benchmark for Social Understanding and Interaction in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28625)
*Heran Hu,Yuhang Dong,Zhucun Xue,Kaiwei Chen,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PsycheMME——一个基于心理学理论的视频基准，包含八项能力（grounding、states、needs、interaction decisions、memory、risk 等），覆盖 630 个视频片段、1000 道题，并配套"Reasoning-Verified"评分协议（依据显式理由与标注线索的支持度与逻辑一致性加权），以区分"理解人"与"选择回应"两类判断；在 17 个模型上评测，发现 Timing 能力最弱，并系统定位模型在理解、回应选择与论证上的失败点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合准确率会掩盖"理解人类需求"与"选择合适回应"这两类本质不同判断之间的差异；现有视频基准缺乏心理学理论支撑的、能将二者分离评测的组织框架与评分方式。

Method: 以 Social Information Processing 与 Perception-Action Cycle 为理论依据，构建八项能力的视频基准 PsycheMME；从 630 个片段构造 1000 道题，采用"隐蔽参考答案的独立作答 + 专家审核"流程；提出 Reasoning-Verified 协议，依据显式理由对标注线索的支持度与逻辑一致性对答案赋权；在 17 个模型上评测，并进行输入控制与 SFT/GRPO 实验考察情境依赖与适应性。

Result: 17 个模型中 Timing 的 Verified 分数与得分保留率均为最低，且对正确 Timing 答案的支持性判断更弱；Need 分数相近的模型在开放题上的答案完整度仍有明显差异；输入控制与 SFT/GRPO 实验揭示了模型的情境依赖性与可适应性差异。

Conclusion: 将答案与理由一起评测，可定位模型在人类理解、回应选择与论证解释三个环节上的具体失败位置，为细粒度、可解释的社会智能评测提供新范式。

Abstract: Understanding human needs and selecting responses require distinct judgments that aggregate accuracy can obscure. We introduce PsycheMME, a video benchmark informed by psychology, with eight capabilities spanning grounding, states, needs, interaction decisions, memory, and risk. Drawing on Social Information Processing and the Perception-Action Cycle, this organization separates human understanding from response selection. We construct 1,000 questions from 630 episodes through independent answering with proposed references withheld and expert review. Our Reasoning-Verified protocol weights answer credit by the support and logical consistency of explicit rationales against annotated cues. Across 17 models, Timing has the lowest Verified scores and credit retention, with weaker support judgments for correct Timing answers. Models with similar Need scores still differ in answer completeness on open questions. Input controls and SFT/GRPO examine context dependence and adaptation. Evaluating answers alongside rationales locates where models fail across understanding, response selection, and justification.

</details>


### [153] [SolarRenderer: Few-Step Generative Reference Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28630)
*Guian Fang,Shengju Qian,Dongyue Lu,Junchao Huang,Lutao Jiang,Xin Wang,Yiren Song,Li Jiang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SolarRenderer 是一种结构条件视频渲染器：它在冻结的 MiniMax-H3 33B 主干上挂载两个轻量 LoRA，让结构视频（布局/运动/相机）直接走原生参考通路、仅用纯文本描述，从而能用单一控制 LoRA 泛化到任意结构表示，并用四步分布匹配渲染实现 12.3 倍加速与实时 768p 流式渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏引擎与编码智能体能给出场景的布局、运动和相机等粗粒度结构，但要把这种结构转成真实感视频，需要渲染器既遵循结构、又补全结构未指定的外观。现有渲染器的三大痛点：一是绑定单一引擎/表示形式，二是需要几十步去噪或代价高昂的专属蒸馏，三是训练条件与目标视频不对齐。

Method: 在冻结的 MiniMax-H3 33B 主干上接入两个轻量 LoRA。结构视频按其所描述帧的位置进入主干的原生参考通路，不附加类型标识符，只用纯文本描述，因此单个控制 LoRA 可适配任意结构表示。结构引导的分布匹配基于预训练的四步 LoRA 起步训练，在四步内完成渲染且保留已学到的控制能力；另提供块因果变体，将同一设计扩展到流式场景。

Result: 四步渲染比 50 步采样快 12.3 倍；块因果变体可实时渲染 768p 视频。构建了来自游戏引擎、模拟器和 YouTube 视频的 136K 帧对齐片段语料。在分布内测试集与 16 种结构表示的泛化基准上均取得 SOTA，并泛化到 11 种未见过的表示（含姿态骨架、手绘草图、点云）。

Conclusion: 通过复用主干原生参考通路并把结构"降级"为纯文本描述的帧级条件，可得到一个与表示无关、可单 LoRA 控制的结构条件渲染器；结合从预训练少步 LoRA 出发的分布匹配，兼顾了四步高效采样与实时流式渲染，并展现出跨表示的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Game engines and coding agents can specify the layout, motion, and camera of a scene, yet turning such coarse structure into realistic video requires a renderer that follows the structure and completes the appearance it leaves open. Existing renderers are tied to the representation of one engine, need tens of denoising steps or a costly distillation of their own, and learn from conditions that are not aligned with their target videos. We present SolarRenderer, a structure-conditioned video renderer that adapts a frozen MiniMax-H3 33B backbone with two lightweight LoRAs. Structural videos enter the native reference pathway of the backbone at the positions of the frames they describe, without a type identifier and described only in plain text, so that a single control LoRA follows arbitrary structural representations. Starting from a pretrained four-step LoRA, structure-guided distribution matching renders in four steps, 12.3 times faster than 50-step sampling, while preserving the learned control. A block-causal variant extends the same design to streaming and renders 768p video in real time. To train the model, we build a frame-aligned corpus of 136K clips from game engines, simulators, and YouTube videos. Experiments on an in-distribution test set and a generalization benchmark of 16 structural representations show that SolarRenderer achieves state-of-the-art results across both, and generalizes to 11 unseen representations, including pose skeletons, drawings, and point clouds.

</details>


### [154] [AEON: Unifying Video and 3D World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28631)
*Weiqi Zhang,Wenyuan Zhang,Yu-Shen Liu,Junsheng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AEON 提出统一框架，在视频生成潜空间中生成可渲染的4D高斯泼溅（4DGS）作为显式世界表示，兼顾视频模型的真实感与3D模型的三维一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显式世界模拟存在两条割裂路径：视频生成模型（如Sora）外观真实但缺乏3D一致性；3D生成模型（如Marble）几何一致但静态、真实感不足。作者希望融合二者优势。

Method: 在文本/图像条件化的视频潜空间内构建生成模型，输出4D Gaussian Splatting作为显式世界表示。先用单目视频训练4D重建模型，同时预测4DGS与相机位姿，作为初始化4D解码器；再设计域适配器，通过在视频VAE上的蒸馏，将生成式视频潜变量转换为重建式4D潜变量。

Result: AEON可沿预测相机轨迹渲染4DGS以支持基于视频的世界建模，并能构建含场景动态的持久4D环境；在标准基准上取得SOTA性能，支持多样化的世界建模应用。

Conclusion: 通过将生成式视频潜空间与可渲染4D表示对齐，AEON统一了视频式与3D式世界模型，实现兼具真实感、3D一致性与动态性的世界模拟。

Abstract: Current world models for explicit world simulation typically follow two distinct pathways: (1) video generative models (e.g., Sora), which simulate realistic appearances but fail to preserve 3D consistency; and (2) 3D generative models (e.g., Marble), which generate geometrically consistent scenes but often yield static, less realistic observations. We present AEON, a unified framework that bridges video-based and 3D-based world models and combines the benefits of both. The key insight of AEON lies in a generative model that operates within the text- or image-conditioned video latent space while producing renderable 4D graphics primitives---specifically, 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS)---as the explicit world representation. We train a 4D reconstruction model to predict both 4DGS and camera poses from monocular videos, serving as our initialized 4D decoder. Furthermore, we design a domain adapter that translates generative video latents into reconstructive 4D latents through a distillation process on the video VAE. AEON naturally supports video-based world modeling by rendering 4DGS at predicted camera trajectories, and constructs a persistent 4D environment with scene dynamics for realistic world simulation. Consequently, AEON empowers a diverse range of world modeling applications and achieves state-of-the-art performance on standard benchmarks. Project Page: https://weiqi-zhang.github.io/AEON

</details>


### [155] [Joints, Camera, Action! Co-Generating 3D Motion and Video for Arbitrary Rigs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28632)
*Noam Rotstein,Yoad Tewel,Chen Tessler,Haggai Maron,Gal Chechik,Or Litany,Yoni Kasten,Yuval Atzmon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合生成模型，可将视频与任意骨架（rig）的3D运动直接协同生成，并估计相机把运动配准到3D场景中，从而实现视频运动在3D资产上的可回放动画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型虽能生成逼真运动，但运动只停留在像素层面，无法驱动特定3D资产并在3D场景中回放。已有视频-运动模型要么只支持人体骨架且需增加数十亿参数，要么从生成的2D姿态图三角化得到3D运动，泛化性与精度受限。

Method: 将3D运动建模放在独立的、支持任意骨架的模型中，通过少量交叉注意力层与预训练视频模型连接。这种解耦使运动模型可先在更易获取、易增强的纯运动数据上学习骨架运动；同时模型学习在视频帧中定位各关节，从而恢复相机并放置3D运动。为跨多种骨架训练，使用生成式视频编辑器把动画3D资产放入与其动作匹配的场景，并筛选编辑结果以保证已知运动有效；结合人与机器人视频，构建约20万训练对。

Result: 评估表明，本模型从文本驱动多样骨架的动画比video-first流程更忠实，运动能响应周围场景；同时支持视频转骨架运动、骨架运动转视频，以及基于关键帧的3D运动控制。

Conclusion: 通过解耦运动模型与视频模型的联合生成框架，实现了通用的、可回放的3D运动动画，为视频运动迁移到任意3D资产提供了可行方案，并具备多种跨模态应用能力。

Abstract: Video generation models can now depict remarkably realistic motion. Yet it is unclear how to use this motion beyond pixels: to animate a specific 3D asset and replay the animation in its 3D scene. For instance, a video model can show a monkey jumping over a crossbar, but it cannot return that jump as replayable 3D motion placed in the 3D scene. Here we present a model that directly co-generates the video and 3D motion for any supplied rig, along with a camera that registers this motion to the scene. Prior video-motion models either handle only human skeletons, often adding billions of parameters, or triangulate 3D motion from generated 2D pose maps. We instead keep 3D motion in a separate model that supports arbitrary skeletons, and connect it to a pretrained video model through a small set of cross-attention layers. This separation lets the motion model first learn how skeletons move from motion-only data, which is easier to obtain and augment than paired video. Additionally, our model learns to locate each joint in the video frames, which lets us recover the camera and place the 3D motion in the scene. To train across many skeletons, we use a generative video editor to place animated 3D assets in scenes suited to their actions, curating the edits so their known motion remains valid. Together with human and robot video, this yields nearly 200K training pairs. Our evaluations demonstrate that our model animates diverse rigs from text more faithfully than video-first pipelines, and its motion responds to the surrounding scene. It also turns video into rig motion, rig motion into video, and enables keyframe-based control of the 3D motion. Videos and additional results are available on our project page: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/par/projects/jca/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [H2DF: Calibrated Target Bands for Controllable Language-Model Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26864)
*Jupiter Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 H2DF：一种校准式目标区间遗忘目标函数，在达到数据相关目标前施加类似梯度上升的压力，达到后抑制或反转压力，从而控制遗忘强度、保护保留效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度上升（GA）作为简单的语言模型遗忘基线，在有效遗忘已发生后仍持续增大遗忘损失，这种无界压力会损害保留效用，并使被遗忘样本出现异常高的损失值。

Method: 设计校准式目标区间目标：低于数据相关下目标时施加类 GA 压力，达到目标区间后抑制或反转该压力。目标值在训练前由匹配的非成员样本估计，基础目标每步仅需一个遗忘批次的前向/反向传播；TOFU 变体每十步加入一次稀疏保留回放。TOFU 问答采用单侧目标，MUSE-News 语言建模采用双侧目标。

Result: 三个 TOFU 随机种子上，遗忘 NLL 为 1.528（margin 0.049），保留 NLL 为 1.165（margin 0.047），与 GradDiff 相当，且仅每十步使用保留回放，变异性显著低于 GA。MUSE-News 上保留效果优于 GA 与 NPO，与 SimNPO 有竞争力但未全面胜出。消融显示上目标可减少过冲，基于验证集的检查点选择很重要。

Conclusion: 校准目标区间是一种实用的遗忘强度控制机制，但同时对随机种子和校准质量较为敏感。

Abstract: Gradient ascent (GA) is a simple language-model unlearning baseline, but it continues increasing forget loss after useful forgetting has occurred. This unbounded pressure can damage retained utility and make forgotten examples abnormally high-loss. We introduce H2DF, a calibrated target-band objective that applies GA-like pressure below a data-dependent lower target and suppresses, or reverses, that pressure after the target region is reached. The targets are estimated from matched non-member examples before training, so the base objective requires one forget-batch forward/backward pass per optimization step; our reported TOFU variant adds sparse retain replay every tenth step. We instantiate a one-sided objective for TOFU question answering and a two-sided objective for MUSE-News language modeling. Across three TOFU seeds, H2DF obtains a forget NLL of 1.528 with margin 0.049 and retain NLL of 1.165 with margin 0.047, matching GradDiff while using retain replay only every tenth step and showing substantially lower variability than GA. On MUSE-News, H2DF improves retention over GA and NPO and is competitive with SimNPO, but does not dominate it. Ablations show that the upper target reduces overshoot and that validation-based checkpoint selection is important. These results support calibrated target bands as a practical mechanism for controlling forgetting strength, while also exposing sensitivity to random seed and calibration quality.

</details>


### [157] [AutoTerm-SST: Adaptive Terminology Memory with Zero Session-Time Setup for Streaming Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26865)
*Jiaxuan Luo,Siqi Ouyang,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AutoTerm-SST 是一个无需会前上传术语表或选择领域的交互式流式语音翻译系统，通过将大规模术语库切分为主题切片并依据上下文自动激活少量相关切片，在固定检索与提示预算下达到与已知领域基线相当的术语准确率与译文质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强语音翻译（如 RASST）假设用户在每次会话前提供术语表，这在现场演讲中不便；而把所有领域合并成一个不断增长的术语表，会让大量无关术语在同一检索与提示中竞争，导致性能退化。目标是实现零设置的术语感知流式翻译。

Method: 离线将大规模术语目录组织为若干主题切片，用近期流式翻译上下文与双语切片原型进行匹配，在固定的检索与提示预算下激活一个紧凑的多切片工作集合（实验中为四切片）；系统同时展示当前激活切片与检索到的术语，并采用 vLLM 托管服务。

Result: 在 5 场 ACL 与 5 场 ESO 肿瘤学演讲上，AutoTerm 的术语准确率 88.3%（参考 88.1%）、mWER-resegmented BLEU 45.09（参考 45.19），且无需领域输入；相比一百万条目的合并术语表，术语准确率提升 3.9 点、BLEU 提升 2.30；完整系统 RTF 为 0.406，vLLM 主机可同时支撑 32 路实时会话。

Conclusion: 通过主题切片与上下文驱动的切片路由，AutoTerm-SST 在不需要会前术语表上传和领域选择的条件下，取得了与已知领域参考相当的翻译质量，并优于超大合并术语表，同时保持较低计算开销，实现零设置的术语感知流式语音翻译。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented speech translation improves rare terminology, but systems such as RASST still assume that users provide a glossary before each session. That assumption is inconvenient for live talks, while merging every domain into one growing glossary eventually places too many irrelevant terms in the same retrieval and prompt competition. We present AutoTerm-SST, an interactive system that removes session-time glossary upload and domain selection. It organizes a large offline terminology catalog into topic slices, matches recent streaming translation context to bilingual slice prototypes, and activates a compact multi-slice working set under fixed retrieval and prompt budgets. On five ACL and five ESO oncology talks, AutoTerm matches a known-domain reference in terminology accuracy (88.3% vs. 88.1%) and mWER-resegmented BLEU (45.09 vs. 45.19), while requiring no domain input. Compared with a one-million-entry merged glossary, it improves terminology accuracy by 3.9 points and BLEU by 2.30. Routing plus four-slice retrieval remains lightweight: the complete system runs at 0.406 RTF, and the vLLM host sustains 32 real-time sessions. The live demo exposes active slices and retrieved terminology as the topic changes, providing zero-setup terminology-aware streaming translation.

</details>


### [158] [WaveCommitments: Private and Verifiable Speech-Agent Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26867)
*Oguzhan Baser,Kaan Kale,Sriram Vishwanath,Sandeep Chinchali*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 WaveCommitments：在可信执行环境内用 Data Shapley 做保密资产估值，并结合多元多项式承诺实现选择性揭示与防篡改溯源，让语音智能体市场中的卖方在不泄露资产的前提下证明交付资产与估值一致。在三个数据集和 Whisper 等先进编码器上，相比最佳基准最高加速 20 倍、证明体积缩减 39 倍，并系统刻画了说话人价值随任务与编码器的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音系统正演化为由自主语音智能体构成的生态，智能体之间共同发声、协商并交易嵌入、频谱图或原始数据等资产。这类市场中存在两难：卖方受隐私约束不能公开资产供评估，买方又无法为"无法测试的东西"定价。因此需要一个既保护隐私又能让买方验证资产价值的设计。

Method: 端到端方案：1）在可信执行环境（TEE）内通过 Data Shapley 对资产进行保密估值；2）采用多元多项式承诺（multivariate polynomial commitments）支持选择性揭示；3）提供防篡改溯源，证明交付的资产与估值一致而隐藏其余信息。

Result: 在三个公开数据集、多种任务与包括 Whisper 在内的先进编码器上实验，相比最强基准最高实现 20 倍加速，证明体积最多缩小 39 倍。同时系统分析了说话人价值在不同任务与编码器间的变化，揭示了智能体层面的市场价格。

Conclusion: WaveCommitments 为隐私受限的语音资产市场提供了可验证、可溯源的估值与交付机制，兼顾隐私与可定价性，并在效率上显著优于现有基准；其分析还表明说话人/智能体价值具有任务与编码器依赖性，可作为市场化定价依据。

Abstract: Modern speech systems are evolving into ecosystems of autonomous speech agents that speak together, negotiate, and trade assets such as embeddings, spectrograms, or raw data. In such markets, sellers cannot reveal assets for evaluation due to privacy constraints, and buyers cannot price what they cannot test. Thus, we open-source WaveCommitments, an end-to-end design that pairs confidential asset valuation via Data Shapley inside a trusted execution environment with multivariate polynomial commitments. It enables selective openings and tamper-evident provenance to prove that a delivered asset matches the valuation while hiding everything else. Our extensive experiments across three known datasets and various tasks with state-of-the-art encoders, including Whisper, reveal that we speed up the best benchmarks by up to 20x while reducing their proof sizes by up to 39x. We also systematically study how a speaker's value shifts across tasks and encoders, revealing agent-level market prices.

</details>


### [159] [Multimodal Ambivalence and Hesitancy Recognition with Bidirectional Audio--Text Cross-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26868)
*TingTing Gu,JinCheng Luo,WenQiu Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个视频级音频-文本框架用于识别矛盾/犹豫（A/H），在多语言BERT与wav2vec 2.0特征基础上通过双向交叉注意力实现跨模态融合，在BAH数据集被试独立划分上取得72.19%准确率和77.95%的A/H F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矛盾与犹豫（A/H）是微妙的行为状态，既体现在说话内容上也体现在说话方式上，因此单模态线索不足，需要联合建语言与声学信息。

Method: 对整段视频转录用预训练多语言BERT编码、语音波形用wav2vec 2.0编码，并将二者投影到共享表示空间；堆叠两层双向交叉注意力，使语言token关注声学帧、声学帧关注语言上下文，无需显式时间对齐；输出序列经掩码均值池化后，用拼接、逐元素乘积和绝对差三种方式组合，输入二分类器。

Result: 在Behavioural Ambivalence/Hesitancy (BAH)数据集的被试独立划分测试集上：准确率72.19%，精确率75.00%，召回率81.13%，A/H F1为77.95%，Macro-F1为70.16%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，联合建模语言与声学信息对自动A/H识别具有潜力；交叉注意力融合可在无显式对齐条件下有效捕获跨模态线索。代码已开源。

Abstract: Ambivalence and hesitancy (A/H) are subtle behavioural states that can be expressed through both what a person says and how it is spoken. This paper presents an audio--text framework for video-level A/H recognition. Full-video transcripts and speech waveforms are encoded using pre-trained multilingual BERT and wav2vec~2.0, respectively, and projected into a shared representation space. Two stacked bidirectional cross-attention blocks allow linguistic tokens to attend to acoustic frames and acoustic frames to attend to linguistic context without requiring explicit temporal alignment. The resulting sequences are aggregated through masked mean pooling and combined using concatenation, element-wise products, and absolute differences for binary classification. We evaluate the model on the subject-independent split of the Behavioural Ambivalence/Hesitancy (BAH) dataset. The proposed system achieves 72.19\% accuracy, 75.00\% precision, 81.13\% recall, 77.95\% A/H F1-score, and 70.16\% Macro-F1 on the test set. These results demonstrate the potential of jointly modelling linguistic and acoustic information for automatic A/H recognition.code at:https://github.com/DorriG/ECCV.

</details>


### [160] [Mixture of Phonetic Experts Based Low-Rank Adaptation of Conformer Models for Accented English Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26888)
*Santosh Dahal,Anmol Guragain,Tianchi Liu,Luis Fernando D'Haro,Kiran Chandra Dahal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MoPE-LoRA，按发音方式类别而非口音划分六个低秩专家，帧级混合路由，参数高效且可迁移到未见口音。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合专家方法通常按口音分配专家，隐含把口音身份当作主要变化轴；但口音差异常表现为音素实现方式的系统性偏移，因此按音系类别组织自适应更合理。

Method: 在LoRA框架下设置固定六个低秩专家，分别对应发音方式类别；采用结合音素监督与可学习声学门控的混合机制进行帧级路由；专家集在所有口音间共享，无需口音专用模块。

Result: 在L2-ARCTIC上稳定优于单LoRA与全参数微调；在训练时留出一种口音的零样本评测中，相较单LoRA取得12.3%的相对提升。

Conclusion: 基于音系类别的分解是一种可扩展、可迁移的口音自适应替代方案。

Abstract: We present Mixture of Phonetic Experts based Low-Rank Adaptation (MoPE-LoRA), a parameter-efficient adaptation framework for accented English speech recognition. Recent mixture-of-experts approaches typically allocate experts per accent, implicitly treating accent identity as the primary axis of variation. In contrast, we organize adaptation along phonetic categories, motivated by the observation that accent differences are often expressed as systematic distortions of phoneme realization. MoPE-LoRA introduces a fixed set of six low-rank experts aligned with manner-of-articulation classes and performs frame-level routing through a hybrid mechanism that combines phoneme supervision with learned acoustic gating. Because the expert set is shared across accents, the model does not require accent-specific modules and naturally extends to previously unseen accents. Experiments on L2-ARCTIC show that MoPE-LoRA consistently improves over single LoRA and full fine-tuning. In zero-shot evaluation where one accent is held out during training, the proposed method yields a 12.3% relative improvement over single LoRA. These results indicate that phonetic-category-based decomposition offers a scalable and transferable alternative to accent-level adaptation.

</details>


### [161] [AtomSD: Procedural Atom-Guided Self-Distillation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26926)
*Bohan Yu,Pengfei Cao,Kai Tian,Chen Han,Shi-Yang Li,Chenxi Zhou,Junlin Liu,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"程序性原子"(Procedural Atoms)——由Intent、When、Do、Avoid四要素构成的细粒度、可独立复用的程序性知识单元，并据此构建AtomSD自蒸馏框架：学习者持续从自身成功轨迹中抽取原子，再通过原子条件监督与任务奖励优化将其蒸馏进演化中的策略。实验表明AtomSD稳定优于强奖励基线与蒸馏方法，平均性能最高超出SOTA基线9.31分，验证了原子级程序性知识对策略学习的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型虽具备丰富事实知识与强推理能力，但解决复杂任务还需要"如何行动"的程序性知识。现有方法多以完整轨迹或捆绑技能的形式表示程序性知识，导致其难以在不同交互历史间灵活复用，缺乏细粒度、可拆解的知识表示。

Method: 1) 提出Procedural Atoms表示：将程序性知识拆为Intent（意图）、When（适用时机）、Do（执行动作）、Avoid（规避行为）四个要素，构成细粒度、可独立应用的单元；2) 提出AtomSD框架：以原子为引导的自蒸馏，学习者持续从自身成功轨迹中提取原子，并通过原子条件化监督与任务奖励优化，将这些原子蒸馏到不断演化的策略中。

Result: 综合实验显示，AtomSD在平均性能上稳定优于强奖励基线与蒸馏方法，最高超过当前最优基线9.31分；进一步分析表明所提出的程序性原子能提供有效引导。

Conclusion: 用原子级、可复用、可独立应用的细粒度形式表示程序性知识，并结合自蒸馏与奖励优化，是提升LLM策略学习效果的有效路径，凸显了原子级程序性知识的价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) possess broad factual knowledge and strong reasoning capabilities, but solving complex tasks also requires procedural knowledge about how to act. Existing approaches often represent procedural knowledge through trajectories or bundled skills, limiting flexible reuse across interaction histories. This paper introduces Procedural Atoms, fine-grained and independently applicable units that represent Intent, When, Do, and Avoid guidance. Building on this representation, we propose AtomSD, an Atom-guided Self-Distillation framework in which the learner continually extracts Procedural Atoms from its own successful trajectories and distills them into the evolving policy through Atom-conditioned supervision and task-reward optimization. Comprehensive experiments show that AtomSD consistently outperforms strong reward-based and distillation methods, exceeding the state-of-the-art baseline by up to 9.31 points in average performance. Further analyses demonstrate that the proposed Procedural Atoms provide effective guidance, highlighting the value of atom-level procedural knowledge for policy learning.

</details>


### [162] [Do We Need the Largest LLMs for Every Clinical Decision?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26937)
*Ali H. Lazem,William Teahan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出CERTA——一种决策级统计认证机制，用于判定量化模型能否替代原生BF16模型，并将无法认证的决策升级至参考模型；在11个临床指令模型上验证了8-bit与4-bit量化的风险可控部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型临床语言模型通常需要多GPU基础设施，限制了本地部署，而临床场景对决策可靠性要求极高，简单的固定精度压缩难以提供部署层面的可靠性保障。

Method: 提出CERTA决策级统计认证框架；在来自3个模型家族（7–72B）的11个通用及领域适配的指令微调模型上，基于去标识化患者上下文（2,484名患者）评估2,500个临床核验决策，并采用患者不相交的校准与测试划分；对比运算分析中的round-to-nearest 8-bit量化与类别条件4-bit前沿，并另用精确分裂样本程序评估有限样本保证。

Result: 运算分析中，BF16存储下的8-bit量化覆盖全部11个模型99.8–100%的留出决策；类别条件4-bit前沿将Qwen2.5-72B与HuatuoGPT-o1-72B的摊销BF16等效部署成本分别降至25.8%与27.3%，实测峰值显存43.0与42.7 GB，低于单张48 GB加速器标称容量（该硬件未直接测试）；精确分裂样本证书下成本为26.7%与27.9%；外部疾病命名实体基准上，前沿选定的NF4配置平均绝对F1变化均为0.045，但模型间差异显著。

Conclusion: CERTA将该核验任务中的模型压缩从固定精度选择转变为风险受控的部署策略。

Abstract: Large clinical language models often require multi-GPU infrastructure, limiting local deployment. We introduce CERTA, a decision-level statistical certificate that determines when a quantized model may substitute for its native BF16 reference and escalates decisions that cannot be certified to it. Across eleven instruction-tuned models, both general-purpose and domain-adapted, from three families (7-72B), we evaluated 2,500 clinical-verification decisions from de-identified patient contexts (2,484 patients), with patient-disjoint calibration and testing. In the higher-power operational analysis, round-to-nearest 8-bit quantization at BF16 storage served 99.8-100% of held-out decisions across all eleven models; formal finite-sample guarantees were assessed separately using the exact split-sample procedure. In the same operational analysis, the class-conditional 4-bit frontier reduced the amortized, BF16-equivalent deployment cost to 25.8% and 27.3% of the BF16 reference for Qwen2.5-72B and HuatuoGPT-o1-72B, respectively, with measured peak memory of 43.0 and 42.7 GB, below the nominal capacity of a single 48 GB accelerator. Deployment on that specific hardware was not directly tested. Under the exact split-sample certificate, which also accounts for configuration selection, these costs were 26.7% and 27.9%. On external disease named-entity benchmarks against human annotations, the frontier-selected NF4 configurations had a mean absolute F1 change of 0.045 on both datasets, with substantial model-specific variation. CERTA converts compression from a fixed precision choice into a risk-controlled deployment policy for this verification task.

</details>


### [163] [The Bottleneck Is Retrieval, Not Reading: Diagnosing Evidence-Based Generation with RAG for Long Context Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26951)
*Debopriyo Banerjee,Owais Aijaz,Ahmer Tabassum,Zhuohan Xie,Preslav Nakov,Veselin Stoyanov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"证据式生成"(EBG)：模型答题须给出行级引用，并用联合的Ans+Evid指标同时评估正确性与证据支撑；构建多文档证据型QA基准，揭示RAG的两类结构盲区及仅评答案时不可见的引用失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG是当前将大模型锚定外部语料的主流范式，但其基于相似度的分块流水线存在结构性盲区：无法预判问题需要哪些段落，且丢弃了用于消解跨段落指代的文档级上下文。现有评测只评答案正确性，无法发现"依据不足"的问题。

Method: 1) 提出EBG任务形式，要求答案附带行级引用；2) 设计联合指标Ans+Evid，同时衡量答案正确性与证据grounding；3) 将MultiHop-RAG与QASPER-MP（QASPER的多论文改编版，含真实短论文线索）重构为证据支撑的多文档QA基准；4) 补充受控合成two-hop haystack与证据增强的NoLiMa（10K–1M字符）；5) 在10个闭源前沿模型与3个开源模型上评测。

Result: EBG隔离出两类长上下文模型可规避的RAG局限：对多事实查询，相似度检索能找到"某个"相关chunk远比找齐"全部"所需chunk可靠；分块破坏了解析身份型查询所需的文档元信息。此外，EBG暴露了仅评答案时不可见的grounding失败：two-needle设定下引用不完整；当上下文与参数知识冲突时，Ans与Ans+Evid之间差距扩大。

Conclusion: 以答案+证据为核心的EBG范式与联合指标比传统RAG+仅答案评测更能诊断检索与分块的结构性缺陷，并揭示grounding失败；数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is the dominant paradigm for grounding large language models in external corpora, but its similarity-driven, chunked pipeline has structural blind spots: it cannot anticipate which passages a question requires and discards document-level context that resolves cross-passage references. We introduce evidence-based generation (EBG), requiring models to return an answer with line-level citations, and a joint Ans+Evid metric that scores correctness and grounding simultaneously. We reformulate two corpora into evidence-grounded multi-document QA benchmarks- MultiHop-RAG and QASPER-MP, a multi-paper adaptation of QASPER with realistic short paper cues, and pair them with a controlled synthetic two-hop haystack and an evidence-augmented NoLiMa spanning 10K-1M characters. Across ten proprietary frontier and three open-weight LLMs, EBG isolates two RAG limitations that long-context models avoid: similarity retrieval surfaces any relevant chunk far more reliably than all required chunks for multi-fact queries, and chunking destroys document meta-information needed to resolve identity-bearing queries. EBG also exposes grounding failures invisible to answer-only evaluation, including incomplete citations in two-needle settings and a widening gap between Ans and Ans+Evid when context contradicts parametric knowledge. Data and code are available at https://github.com/debobanerjee/LC-EBG.

</details>


### [164] [A Multi-granularity Interaction Network for Relational Triple Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26952)
*Xiaocheng Luo,Yanping Chen,Zaifu Zhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出多粒度交互网络，通过词-词、实体-关系、头-尾三层交互与多次迭代，缓解"未见"实体导致的关系三元组抽取性能退化问题，并在 NYT-R/WebNLG-R 上显著提升 Unseen 指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统关系三元组抽取方法不依赖实体类型信息，但面对训练中"未见"（unseen）实体时，无法为其建立有效的上下文依赖，导致性能严重下降。论文旨在解决 unseen 实体带来的泛化难题。

Method: 构建多粒度交互网络，融合词-词（word-word）、实体-关系（entity-relation）、头-尾（head-tail）三种粒度的交互以捕获深层语义依赖；并通过多次迭代精炼头实体与尾实体的表示，增强关系三元组相关的全局语义特征，从而补救前序步骤中被遗漏或漏检的三元组。

Result: 在两个重排数据集上取得稳定且一致的优良表现；在 NYT-R/WebNLG-R 的 Unseen 指标上，F1 相比已有方法分别提升 3.1% 与 20.5%，对包含 unseen 命名实体的三元组识别提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: 多粒度交互与迭代精炼机制能有效增强模型对 unseen 实体的语义建模与三元组召回，验证了在不依赖实体类型信息前提下提升关系三元组抽取泛化能力的可行性。

Abstract: Relational triple extraction identifies entity relations from raw sentences without depending on entity type information. The performance of traditional methods is seriously degenerated by ``unseen'' entities, because these methods can't build contextual dependencies relevant ``unseen'' entities. This paper addresses this challenge by proposing a multi-granularity interaction network that leverages word-word, entity-relation, and head-tail interactions to capture deep semantic dependencies. By incorporating multiple iterations, the multi-granularity interaction network is effective to refine the representations corresponding to head and tail entities. It enhances the global semantic features associated with relational triples, aiding in the remediation of triples that may have been overlooked or omitted in earlier steps. Strong and consistent performance is achieved by our method on two rearranged datasets, with a significant improvement in identifying relational triples, including those involving ``unseen'' named entities. For example, our model improves F1-score by 3.1%/20.5% over previous approaches on the NYT-R/WebNLG-R datasets in the Unseen metric. Code: https://github.com/xiaocen-luo/Interaction-Network.

</details>


### [165] [LAMB: Latent Multi-Hypothesis Beliefs for Long-Horizon Large Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26955)
*Peien Ding,Hui Li,Yuhan Liu,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LAMB——一种潜在多假设信念的循环框架，用Common–Delta分解将共享证据与竞争假设分离编码，通过动作-结果预测的相对证据驱动假设更新，在ALFWorld等四个环境中匹配或超越PABU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程语言智能体需要紧凑记忆，既要保留交互证据，又要保留关于未观测状态的多个竞争假设。现有信念状态方法多用离散自然语言编码记忆，反复压缩会遗漏证据或过早丢弃合理备选假设。

Method: 提出循环框架LAMB：采用Common–Delta分解，共同Common状态存交互证据，加权的Delta分量表示竞争假设；由动作-结果预测的相对证据引导循环式假设更新，不生成文本记忆；上下文条件读出以固定token的信念前缀将各假设分别暴露给策略；信念模块与策略通过动作模仿及状态、结果、观测判别等辅助目标联合训练。

Result: 在ALFWorld、BabyAI、ScienceWorld、Crafter上匹配或超越PABU；ALFWorld SR@30提升6.0个百分点，ScienceWorld成功率提升22.0个百分点，Crafter成就进度提升26.37点；ALFWorld冗余动作率降低17.70个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果支持将潜在多假设信念作为紧凑记忆，用于有效的长程决策；相比自然语言离散记忆，可避免证据丢失与假设过早淘汰。

Abstract: Long-horizon language agents require compact memories that retain interaction evidence while preserving competing hypotheses about unobserved states. Although effective, many belief-state methods encode memory in discrete natural language, where repeated compression can omit evidence or prematurely discard plausible alternatives. We introduce LAMB, a recurrent framework for latent multi-hypothesis beliefs. A Common--Delta factorization combines a shared Common state for interaction evidence with weighted Delta components for competing hypotheses. Relative evidence from action-outcome predictions guides recurrent hypothesis updates without generating textual memory. A context-conditioned readout exposes hypotheses separately to the policy through a fixed-token belief prefix in the default configuration. The belief modules and policy are jointly trained using action imitation and auxiliary state, outcome, and observation-discrimination objectives. Across ALFWorld, BabyAI, ScienceWorld, and Crafter, LAMB matches or outperforms PABU, improving ALFWorld SR@30 and ScienceWorld success by 6.0 and 22.0 percentage points, respectively, and Crafter achievement progress by 26.37 points. On ALFWorld, it also reduces the redundant-action rate by 17.70 percentage points. These results support latent multi-hypothesis beliefs as compact memory for effective long-horizon decision making

</details>


### [166] [SZED at ArGuard Shared Tasks: Multi-Task Label-Attention and Cross-Modal Fusion for Arabic Hateful Meme Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26972)
*Selma Khelili,Zohra Abdeli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SZED 团队在 ArGuard 2026 Track A 阿拉伯语仇恨表情包检测任务中，A1 二分类融合 MARBERTv2 与 CLIP ViT-L/14 嵌入取得宏 F1=0.7597，A2 细粒度多标签使用多任务标签注意力架构取得宏 F1=0.3142（10 个活跃类别平均）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作针对阿拉伯语仇恨表情包检测这一跨模态、低资源任务，需要同时解决图文融合判别（A1）与长尾多标签细粒度标注（A2）两个子问题，并关注结果可复现性与类别级误差分析。

Method: A1 部分将 MARBERTv2 文本嵌入与 CLIP ViT-L/14 图像嵌入进行融合，对比拼接、自注意力、集成三种融合头；A2 部分采用多任务标签注意力架构，为每个类别设置查询向量，并加入辅助头进行级联校准（cascade calibration）。

Result: A1 宏 F1 为 0.7597，A2 宏 F1 为 0.3142（对 10 个活跃类别取平均）；同时报告多次运行间的可复现性、各类别误差模式，以及级联校准对长尾标签空间的影响。

Conclusion: 该提交验证了文本—图像预训练模型融合在阿拉伯语仇恨表情包二分类上的有效性，并通过标签注意力与级联校准缓解多标签长尾问题；但 A2 的宏 F1 仍偏低，细粒度标注仍是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: We present SZED's submission to ArGuard 2026 Track A: Arabic hateful meme detection. The task has two parts: binary classification (A1) and fine-grained multi-label tagging (A2). For A1, we fuse MARBERTv2 and CLIP ViT-L/14 embeddings, comparing concatenation, self-attention, and ensemble heads. For A2, we use a multi-task label-attention architecture with per-class query vectors and an auxiliary head for cascade calibration. We reach macro-F1 = 0.7597 on A1 and 0.3142 on A2 (averaged over 10 active classes). We report reproducibility across runs, per-class error patterns, and the effect of cascade calibration on the long-tailed A2 label space.

</details>


### [167] [Sharp Degree Bounds for Putinar Certificates in Terms of the Positivity Margin via Analytic Sums of Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26976)
*Yuxiang Zhao,Ting Gan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文建立了Putinar平方和（SOS）表示次数关于正性裕度 δ 的紧致上界 O(δ^{-1/2}log^2(2/δ))，并通过三变量下界实例证明该依赖阶数最优，同时给出Lasserre层级的最坏情况收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有SOS证书次数结果多依赖一般性假设，缺乏对正性裕度 δ 的精确刻画，导致Lasserre层级的收敛速率估计不够锐利。作者希望明确证书次数与 δ 的定量依赖关系并判定其是否最优。

Method: 固定Archimedean二次模与目标次数上界，对归一化多项式目标推导一致的SOS证书次数上界 O(δ^{-1/2}log^2(2/δ))；随后构造三个变量中的匹配下界例子以验证紧致性。

Result: 得到上界 O(δ^{-1/2}log^2(2/δ))，并以三变量实例给出匹配的下界，证明其对 δ 的依赖是最优的；由此导出Lasserre层级匹配的紧致最坏情况收敛速率。

Conclusion: Putinar SOS证书次数对正性裕度的依赖已完全确定，所给界在阶数意义下不可改进，并为Lasserre层级的收敛分析提供了紧致刻画。

Abstract: We establish sharp degree bounds for Putinar sum-of-squares (SOS) certificates in terms of the positivity margin $δ$. For any fixed Archimedean quadratic module with prescribed generators and any fixed bound on the degree of the objective, we prove a uniform certificate-degree upper bound of $O(δ^{-1/2}\log^2(2/δ))$ for normalized polynomial objectives. We show that this bound is sharp by constructing a matching lower-bound example in three variables, thereby establishing that the dependence on $δ$ is optimal. These matching bounds also yield sharp worst-case convergence rates for the Lasserre hierarchy.

</details>


### [168] [Think Before You Transcribe: Context-Steered Reasoning for Hotword-Aware Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26986)
*Qihao Wang,Hang Su,Fan Cui,Lichun Fan,Jian Luan,Yue Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"上下文引导推理"框架：先在转写前生成一句音频接地的短字幕，再据此评估候选热词相关性并做词级决策，提升上下文ASR的转写质量与热词识别鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文ASR通过提供候选热词改善罕见实体识别，但检索到的候选可能不完整或有误。系统需既能利用相关候选，又不能让其凌驾于声学证据之上。

Method: 1) 在转写前生成简短的、音频接地的字幕作为任务特定中间表征，使模型在词级决策前先判断候选相关性；2) 用监督微调建立"字幕→转写"的结构化行为；3) 在困难话语只产生失败 rollout 时，采用定向轨迹注入强化群组相对策略优化（GRPO）。

Result: 在多种上下文条件下实验，方法在转写质量与热词识别上均持续提升，且对无关候选保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 以音频接地字幕作为推理中间步骤，并配合监督微调与轨迹注入式强化学习，可有效平衡热词利用与声学证据忠实性。

Abstract: Contextual automatic speech recognition (ASR) improves rare-entity recognition by supplying candidate hotwords, but retrieved candidates may be incomplete or incorrect. A reliable system must therefore exploit a relevant candidate without allowing unsupported alternatives to override the acoustic evidence. We propose a context-steered reasoning framework that generates a short, audio-grounded caption before transcription. This task-specific representation lets the model assess candidate relevance before making a lexical decision. Supervised fine-tuning establishes the structured caption-to-transcription behavior, while targeted trajectory injection strengthens group relative policy optimization when difficult utterances yield only unsuccessful rollouts. Experiments across diverse context conditions show that the proposed method consistently improves both transcription quality and hotword recognition while remaining robust to irrelevant candidates.

</details>


### [169] [TRACE: Training-aware Robustness and Challenge Evaluation for Text-Vision Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26993)
*Pume Tuchinda,Tinnakit Udsa,Peerat Limkonchotiwat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 TRACE 评测框架，将 168 个任务划分为通用、适应性、挑战三类，实验表明数据适配能显著提升适应性但难以改善挑战类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本-视觉嵌入模型的基准只给出聚合分数，无法区分性能来源是通用能力、任务适配还是对困难任务的鲁棒性，因此需要更细粒度的评测方式。

Method: 构建 TRACE 框架，把来自多个数据集来源的 168 个任务重新组织为 General（通用）、Adaptability（适应性）、Challenge（挑战）三类；在两个文本-视觉嵌入模型上进行受控实验，比较通用数据与适配数据组合训练后的表现，并做进一步分析定位薄弱环节。

Result: 通用数据与适配数据结合后，Adaptability 性能分别提升 7.67 与 7.09 分，同时基本保持 General 性能；但 Challenge 性能仍低于 5.0，增加训练数据带来的提升有限。分析显示多语言任务与视觉文档任务是主要短板。

Conclusion: 仅靠数据适配不足以应对挑战类任务，需要超越以数据为中心的方法学创新。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for text-vision embedding models report aggregate scores that obscure whether performance comes from general capabilities, task-specific adaptation, or robustness to challenging tasks. We introduce TRACE, a Training-aware Robustness and Challenge Evaluation framework that reorganizes 168 tasks from various sources of datasets into three categories: General, Adaptability, and Challenge. Through controlled experiments on two text-vision embedding models, we show that combining general and adaptation data improves Adaptability performance by 7.67 and 7.09 points, respectively, while largely preserving General performance. However, Challenge performance remains below 5.0, with limited improvements from additional training data. Further analysis identifies multilingual and visual-document tasks as major weaknesses. Our findings demonstrate that data adaptation alone is insufficient to address these challenges, highlighting the need for methodological advances beyond data-centric improvements.

</details>


### [170] [How to get the best and fairest performance out of your ASR system using activation steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26994)
*Felix Herron,Alex Colagrande,Solange Rossato,François Portet,Alexandre Allauzen,Chuyuan Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文研究ASR系统中的激活引导（activation steering），比较成对激活相减与有监督优化两种方法，发现后者能同时提升整体ASR性能与性别、年龄、方言等多个维度的群体公平性，并揭示用声学相似说话人训练的引导向量可媲美甚至优于同说话人或同人口标签组训练的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导在提升ASR性能与人口统计学公平性方面尚属研究不足的方向；同时作者希望厘清计算或训练引导向量所需数据的"个人化"程度，即最少需要多少与说话人相关的信息。

Method: 考察两种激活引导形式：成对激活相减（paired activation subtraction）与有监督优化（supervised optimization）；后者用于导出引导向量（SVs）。随后系统比较不同数据来源训练SV的效果，包括同数据集中多样化说话人、声学相似说话人、同一说话人以及同一标注人口群体。

Result: 成对激活相减对改善ASR性能几乎无效；有监督优化则能同时提升整体ASR性能和性别、年龄、方言等维度的公平性。使用同一数据集中多样化说话人训练的SV已带来显著提升；而用声学相似说话人训练的向量，其ASR表现可与同说话人或同标注人口群体训练的向量相当或更优。

Conclusion: 激活引导可作为一种高效的少样本与零样本适配手段：在已知说话人人口属性时可实现少样本适配，而在完全无标签情况下也可对未见说话人实现零样本适配。

Abstract: Activation steering for ASR systems is an underexplored technique in improving both ASR performance and demographic fairness. We investigate two forms of activation steering: paired activation subtraction, and supervised optimization. We find the former to be ineffective at ameliorating ASR performance whatsoever, while the latter is quite effective at deriving steering vectors (SVs) that improve at once overall ASR performance and demographic fairness along such diverse axes as gender, age, and dialect. We also explore what types of data---from the most to the least personal---are necessary for calculating or training SVs. First, we show significant performance in performance from training SVs using an eclectic group of speakers from the same dataset. Furthermore, we find that vectors trained using acoustically similar speakers leads to ASR performances that are competitive or superior to vectors trained on the same speaker, or speakers from the same labeled demographic group. This finding shows that we can harness activation steering for few-shot adaptation given access to a speaker's demography, or zero-shot adaptation to unseen speakers without the need for any labels at all.

</details>


### [171] [Evaluating Tibetan Speech Recognition Across Dialects and Adaptation Regimes: A Multi-Granularity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27002)
*Jacob Moore,Sheng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建了联合评价框架，将字符错误率、音节错误率与分词词错误率一并用于藏语语音识别，发现字符错误率更低并不必然带来音节或词级错误更低，且这种排序差异跨语料稳定存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 藏语书写系统不标记词边界，通常只用字符或音节错误率评价ASR，这些指标在系统排序上可能不一致、对适配失败的反应也不同，因此需要多语言层级的评价方式来更可靠地衡量系统质量。

Method: 构建包含字符错误率、音节错误率和分词词错误率的统一评价框架；采用基于词典、神经网络和语言模型三种分词方法，并与专家标注边界对比；在拉萨藏语上评测五种ASR架构，并在另外五个语料子集上考察零样本迁移与目标语料适配；同时变化LoRA秩和训练数据规模以考察适配失败；另引入标准化音系表示分析拼写错误。
